File `crates/planrun-parse/src/document.rs` (full contents):

```rust
//! The block stream: the plan as a flat list of top-level and list-nested blocks, each with its
//! span (docs/architecture.md §4.1). Built once from pulldown-cmark's offset iterator; formats and
//! recognizers never see pulldown-cmark types.

use std::ops::Range;

use planrun_model::{PlanSource, Span, TextRef};
use pulldown_cmark::{Event, Options, Parser, Tag, TagEnd};

use crate::diagnostic::{Diagnostic, Diagnostics};

/// One block of the plan.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum Block {
    /// `#`…`######`.
    Heading {
        /// 1–6.
        level: u8,
        /// The heading text, without the `#`s.
        text: TextRef,
    },
    /// A paragraph, as raw source text (inline markup kept: patterns match the raw text).
    Paragraph {
        /// The paragraph text.
        text: TextRef,
    },
    /// A list item's own text (nested blocks follow as separate blocks with `depth + 1`).
    ListItem {
        /// The item's first paragraph, raw (after the `[ ]` of a task item).
        text: TextRef,
        /// `Some(checked)` for `- [ ]` / `- [x]`.
        task_box: Option<bool>,
    },
    /// A fenced code block. `body` is exact: a full-contents write takes it byte for byte. An
    /// indented code block is a `Fence` with an empty info string and `fence_len` 0.
    Fence {
        /// The info string (`rust`, `powershell`, `diff`, ``) as written.
        info: TextRef,
        /// The content between the fences, ending in `\n` unless empty.
        body: TextRef,
        /// The opening fence's length (3 for ```` ``` ````, 4 for ```` ```` ````).
        fence_len: u8,
    },
    /// A table, kept whole (the "File map" table is shown, never parsed into actions).
    Table {
        /// The table's source text.
        text: TextRef,
    },
    /// A blockquote, kept whole like a table.
    Quote {
        /// The blockquote's source text, `>` markers included.
        text: TextRef,
    },
    /// `---`.
    Rule,
    /// Raw HTML, including `<!-- comments -->`.
    Html {
        /// The HTML source.
        text: TextRef,
    },
}

impl Block {
    /// The language of a fence: the first word of its info string.
    pub fn fence_language<'a>(&'a self, doc: &'a Document) -> Option<&'a str> {
        let Block::Fence { info, .. } = self else {
            return None;
        };
        Some(doc.text(info).split_whitespace().next().unwrap_or(""))
    }
}

/// A block with its position.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct Located {
    /// The block.
    pub block: Block,
    /// Its full source span (for a fence: opening through closing fence; for a list item: its
    /// marker through its own text).
    pub span: Span,
    /// List nesting depth: 0 at top level.
    pub depth: u8,
}

/// The plan source with its block stream.
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct Document {
    /// The normalized plan.
    pub source: PlanSource,
    /// All blocks in source order.
    pub blocks: Vec<Located>,
}

impl Document {
    /// Builds the block stream of `source`. An unclosed fence is an error (`unclosed-fence`),
    /// and so is a fence closed by accident (docs/architecture.md §4.1).
    pub fn build(source: PlanSource) -> Result<Self, Diagnostics> {
        let (blocks, errors) = Builder::new(source.text()).run();
        if !errors.is_empty() {
            return Err(Diagnostics(errors));
        }
        Ok(Self { source, blocks })
    }

    /// The text of `text`.
    pub fn text<'a>(&'a self, text: &'a TextRef) -> &'a str {
        self.source.resolve(text)
    }

    /// The byte ranges of every fence, in order (format detection skips them).
    pub fn fence_spans(&self) -> impl Iterator<Item = Span> + '_ {
        self.blocks
            .iter()
            .filter(|b| matches!(b.block, Block::Fence { .. }))
            .map(|b| b.span)
    }
}

/// An index range `[start, end)` into [`Document::blocks`].
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct BlockRange {
    /// First block.
    pub start: usize,
    /// One past the last block.
    pub end: usize,
}

impl BlockRange {
    /// True when `index` is in the range.
    pub fn contains(&self, index: usize) -> bool {
        (self.start..self.end).contains(&index)
    }

    /// The number of blocks.
    pub fn len(&self) -> usize {
        self.end.saturating_sub(self.start)
    }

    /// True for an empty range.
    pub fn is_empty(&self) -> bool {
        self.len() == 0
    }
}

/// A list item being read: its block index, and the inline text seen before its first nested
/// block.
struct Item {
    block: usize,
    start: usize,
    text: Option<Range<usize>>,
    done: bool,
}

/// A fenced block being read.
struct OpenFence {
    range: Range<usize>,
    pieces: Vec<Range<usize>>,
    owned: String,
    contiguous: bool,
}

/// What an inline event belongs to.
enum Inline {
    Heading {
        level: u8,
        text: Option<Range<usize>>,
    },
    Paragraph,
}

struct Builder<'s> {
    text: &'s str,
    blocks: Vec<Located>,
    errors: Vec<Diagnostic>,
    items: Vec<Item>,
    inline: Option<Inline>,
    fence: Option<OpenFence>,
    /// Events inside this range belong to a block kept whole (table, quote, HTML).
    skip: Option<Range<usize>>,
}

impl<'s> Builder<'s> {
    fn new(text: &'s str) -> Self {
        Self {
            text,
            blocks: Vec::new(),
            errors: Vec::new(),
            items: Vec::new(),
            inline: None,
            fence: None,
            skip: None,
        }
    }

    fn run(mut self) -> (Vec<Located>, Vec<Diagnostic>) {
        let options = Options::ENABLE_TABLES | Options::ENABLE_TASKLISTS;
        let text = self.text;
        for (event, range) in Parser::new_ext(text, options).into_offset_iter() {
            if self.skipped(&range) {
                continue;
            }
            self.event(event, range);
        }
        (self.blocks, self.errors)
    }

    fn skipped(&mut self, range: &Range<usize>) -> bool {
        let Some(skip) = &self.skip else {
            return false;
        };
        if range.start >= skip.start && range.end <= skip.end {
            return true;
        }
        self.skip = None;
        false
    }

    fn event(&mut self, event: Event<'_>, range: Range<usize>) {
        match event {
            Event::Start(tag) => self.start(tag, range),
            Event::End(tag) => self.end(tag, range),
            Event::Text(piece) if self.fence.is_some() => self.fence_text(&piece, range),
            Event::TaskListMarker(checked) => self.task_box(checked),
            Event::Rule => self.push(Block::Rule, range),
            _ => self.inline_event(range),
        }
    }

    fn start(&mut self, tag: Tag<'_>, range: Range<usize>) {
        match tag {
            Tag::Paragraph => self.inline = Some(Inline::Paragraph),
            Tag::Heading { level, .. } => {
                self.close_item_text();
                self.inline = Some(Inline::Heading {
                    level: level as u8,
                    text: None,
                });
            }
            Tag::Item => self.start_item(range),
            Tag::CodeBlock(_) => self.start_fence(range),
            Tag::Table(_) => self.keep_whole(range, |text| Block::Table { text }),
            Tag::BlockQuote(_) => self.keep_whole(range, |text| Block::Quote { text }),
            Tag::HtmlBlock => self.keep_whole(range, |text| Block::Html { text }),
            Tag::List(_) => self.close_item_text(),
            _ => self.inline_event(range),
        }
    }

    fn end(&mut self, tag: TagEnd, range: Range<usize>) {
        match tag {
            TagEnd::Paragraph => self.end_paragraph(range),
            TagEnd::Heading(_) => self.end_heading(range),
            TagEnd::Item => self.end_item(),
            TagEnd::CodeBlock => self.end_fence(),
            _ => {}
        }
    }

    fn keep_whole(&mut self, range: Range<usize>, block: fn(TextRef) -> Block) {
        self.close_item_text();
        let span = self.trimmed(&range);
        self.push(block(TextRef::Span(span)), range.clone());
        self.skip = Some(range);
    }

    fn inline_event(&mut self, range: Range<usize>) {
        if let Some(Inline::Heading { text, .. }) = &mut self.inline {
            *text = Some(widen(text.take(), range));
            return;
        }
        if self.inline.is_some() {
            return;
        }
        let Some(item) = self.items.last_mut() else {
            return;
        };
        if !item.done {
            item.text = Some(widen(item.text.take(), range));
        }
    }

    fn task_box(&mut self, checked: bool) {
        let Some(item) = self.items.last() else {
            return;
        };
        if let Block::ListItem { task_box, .. } = &mut self.blocks[item.block].block {
            *task_box = Some(checked);
        }
    }

    fn end_paragraph(&mut self, range: Range<usize>) {
        self.inline = None;
        let span = self.trimmed(&range);
        if let Some(item) = self.items.last_mut() {
            let first_in_item = !item.done && item.block + 1 == self.blocks.len();
            if first_in_item {
                item.text = Some(span.start as usize..span.end as usize);
                self.close_item_text();
                return;
            }
        }
        self.close_item_text();
        self.push(
            Block::Paragraph {
                text: TextRef::Span(span),
            },
            range,
        );
    }

    fn end_heading(&mut self, range: Range<usize>) {
        let Some(Inline::Heading { level, text }) = self.inline.take() else {
            return;
        };
        let span = self.trimmed(&range);
        let text = text.map_or(Span::new(span.end, span.end), |r| self.trimmed(&r));
        self.push(
            Block::Heading {
                level,
                text: TextRef::Span(text),
            },
            range,
        );
    }

    fn start_item(&mut self, range: Range<usize>) {
        self.close_item_text();
        let at = offset(range.start);
        self.push(
            Block::ListItem {
                text: TextRef::Span(Span::new(at, at)),
                task_box: None,
            },
            range.start..range.start,
        );
        self.items.push(Item {
            block: self.blocks.len() - 1,
            start: range.start,
            text: None,
            done: false,
        });
    }

    fn end_item(&mut self) {
        self.close_item_text();
        self.items.pop();
    }

    /// Fixes the innermost open item's text: called when its first nested block starts or the
    /// item ends.
    fn close_item_text(&mut self) {
        let Some(item) = self.items.last_mut() else {
            return;
        };
        if item.done {
            return;
        }
        item.done = true;
        let text = item.text.clone().unwrap_or(item.start..item.start);
        let span = trim_end(self.text, text);
        let start = offset(item.start);
        let located = &mut self.blocks[item.block];
        located.span = Span::new(start, span.end.max(start));
        if let Block::ListItem { text, .. } = &mut located.block {
            *text = TextRef::Span(span);
        }
    }

    fn start_fence(&mut self, range: Range<usize>) {
        self.close_item_text();
        self.fence = Some(OpenFence {
            range,
            pieces: Vec::new(),
            owned: String::new(),
            contiguous: true,
        });
    }

    fn fence_text(&mut self, piece: &str, range: Range<usize>) {
        let Some(fence) = &mut self.fence else {
            return;
        };
        let borrowed = self.text.get(range.clone()) == Some(piece);
        let follows = fence
            .pieces
            .last()
            .is_none_or(|last| last.end == range.start);
        fence.contiguous &= borrowed && follows;
        fence.owned.push_str(piece);
        fence.pieces.push(range);
    }

    fn end_fence(&mut self) {
        let Some(fence) = self.fence.take() else {
            return;
        };
        let opener = line_at(self.text, fence.range.start);
        let (fence_char, fence_len, info) = fence_opener(self.text, opener.clone());
        let body = match (fence.pieces.first(), fence.pieces.last()) {
            (Some(first), Some(last)) if fence.contiguous => {
                TextRef::Span(Span::new(offset(first.start), offset(last.end)))
            }
            (Some(_), Some(_)) => TextRef::Owned {
                text: fence.owned.as_str().into(),
                from: self.trimmed(&fence.range),
            },
            _ => {
                let at = offset((opener.end + 1).min(fence.range.end));
                TextRef::Span(Span::new(at, at))
            }
        };
        if fence_len > 0 {
            self.check_closed(&fence, opener.clone(), fence_char, fence_len);
        }
        self.push(
            Block::Fence {
                info: TextRef::Span(info),
                body,
                fence_len: u8::try_from(fence_len).unwrap_or(u8::MAX),
            },
            fence.range,
        );
    }

    /// `unclosed-fence`: the fence runs to the end of its container, or a body line opens a
    /// fence of the same character, at least as long, with an info string.
    fn check_closed(
        &mut self,
        fence: &OpenFence,
        opener: Range<usize>,
        fence_char: u8,
        fence_len: usize,
    ) {
        let opener_span = Span::new(offset(opener.start), offset(opener.end));
        let last = last_line(self.text, &fence.range);
        // The closing line keeps its container's indentation in the source: trim all of it.
        let closed = last.start > opener.start
            && closing_fence(self.text[last.clone()].trim_start(), fence_char, fence_len);
        if !closed {
            self.errors.push(Diagnostic::error(
                "unclosed-fence",
                Some(opener_span),
                "this fenced block is never closed: it would swallow the rest of the plan",
            ));
            return;
        }
        let Some(line) = self.suspicious_line(fence, fence_char, fence_len) else {
            return;
        };
        self.errors.push(
            Diagnostic::error(
                "unclosed-fence",
                Some(opener_span),
                "this fenced block is not closed where it looks closed: a line inside it opens \
                 another block",
            )
            .with_related(line, "this line opens a fenced block inside the open one"),
        );
    }

    fn suspicious_line(&self, fence: &OpenFence, fence_char: u8, fence_len: usize) -> Option<Span> {
        for piece in &fence.pieces {
            let piece_text = &self.text[piece.clone()];
            let mut at = piece.start;
            for line in piece_text.split_inclusive('\n') {
                if opens_fence(line, fence_char, fence_len) {
                    let line = line.trim_end_matches('\n');
                    return Some(Span::new(offset(at), offset(at + line.len())));
                }
                at += line.len();
            }
        }
        None
    }

    fn push(&mut self, block: Block, range: Range<usize>) {
        let span = if range.is_empty() {
            Span::new(offset(range.start), offset(range.end))
        } else {
            self.trimmed(&range)
        };
        // A list item is pushed before it is opened, so its depth is its list's.
        let depth = u8::try_from(self.items.len()).unwrap_or(u8::MAX);
        self.blocks.push(Located { block, span, depth });
    }

    fn trimmed(&self, range: &Range<usize>) -> Span {
        trim_end(self.text, range.clone())
    }
}

fn widen(current: Option<Range<usize>>, range: Range<usize>) -> Range<usize> {
    match current {
        Some(r) => r.start.min(range.start)..r.end.max(range.end),
        None => range,
    }
}

fn trim_end(text: &str, range: Range<usize>) -> Span {
    let trimmed = text[range.clone()].trim_end_matches([' ', '\t', '\n']);
    Span::new(offset(range.start), offset(range.start + trimmed.len()))
}

/// The line containing `at`, without its `\n`.
fn line_at(text: &str, at: usize) -> Range<usize> {
    let start = text[..at].rfind('\n').map_or(0, |i| i + 1);
    let end = text[at..].find('\n').map_or(text.len(), |i| at + i);
    start..end
}

/// The last non-empty line of `range`, without its `\n`.
fn last_line(text: &str, range: &Range<usize>) -> Range<usize> {
    let trimmed = text[range.clone()].trim_end_matches('\n');
    let end = range.start + trimmed.len();
    let start = trimmed
        .rfind('\n')
        .map_or(range.start, |i| range.start + i + 1);
    start..end
}

/// The fence character, the fence length and the info string's span of an opening line; a
/// length of 0 for an indented code block.
fn fence_opener(text: &str, line: Range<usize>) -> (u8, usize, Span) {
    let raw = &text[line.clone()];
    let indent = raw.len() - raw.trim_start_matches(' ').len();
    let rest = &raw[indent..];
    let Some(fence_char) = rest.bytes().next().filter(|&c| c == b'`' || c == b'~') else {
        let end = offset(line.end);
        return (0, 0, Span::new(end, end));
    };
    let fence_len = rest.bytes().take_while(|&c| c == fence_char).count();
    let after = &rest[fence_len..];
    let info_start = line.start + indent + fence_len + (after.len() - after.trim_start().len());
    let info_end = line.start + indent + fence_len + after.trim_end().len();
    let info = Span::new(offset(info_start), offset(info_end.max(info_start)));
    (fence_char, fence_len, info)
}

fn fence_run(line: &str, fence_char: u8) -> Option<(usize, &str)> {
    let indent = line.len() - line.trim_start_matches(' ').len();
    if indent > 3 {
        return None;
    }
    let rest = &line[indent..];
    let run = rest.bytes().take_while(|&c| c == fence_char).count();
    Some((run, &rest[run..]))
}

fn closing_fence(line: &str, fence_char: u8, fence_len: usize) -> bool {
    fence_run(line, fence_char)
        .is_some_and(|(run, rest)| run >= fence_len && rest.trim().is_empty())
}

fn opens_fence(line: &str, fence_char: u8, fence_len: usize) -> bool {
    let Some((run, rest)) = fence_run(line, fence_char) else {
        return false;
    };
    let info = rest.trim();
    run >= fence_len && !info.is_empty() && !(fence_char == b'`' && info.contains('`'))
}

fn offset(value: usize) -> u32 {
    // The source module refuses plans over 64 MiB, so every offset fits.
    u32::try_from(value).unwrap_or(u32::MAX)
}

```rust
use super::*;
use crate::source;

fn doc(text: &str) -> Document {
    let source = source::load("t.md", text.as_bytes()).unwrap_or_else(|e| panic!("{e:?}"));
    Document::build(source).unwrap_or_else(|e| panic!("{e:?}"))
}

fn errors(text: &str) -> Vec<Diagnostic> {
    let source = source::load("t.md", text.as_bytes()).unwrap_or_else(|e| panic!("{e:?}"));
    match Document::build(source) {
        Ok(_) => Vec::new(),
        Err(diagnostics) => diagnostics.0,
    }
}

/// Each block as `depth kind text`, the text resolved.
fn shape(doc: &Document) -> Vec<String> {
    doc.blocks
        .iter()
        .map(|b| {
            let what = match &b.block {
                Block::Heading { level, text } => format!("h{level} {}", doc.text(text)),
                Block::Paragraph { text } => format!("p {}", doc.text(text)),
                Block::ListItem { text, task_box } => {
                    format!("li{task_box:?} {}", doc.text(text))
                }
                Block::Fence {
                    info,
                    body,
                    fence_len,
                } => format!("fence{fence_len} {} {:?}", doc.text(info), doc.text(body)),
                Block::Table { text } => format!("table {}", doc.text(text)),
                Block::Quote { text } => format!("quote {}", doc.text(text)),
                Block::Rule => "rule".to_owned(),
                Block::Html { text } => format!("html {}", doc.text(text)),
            };
            format!("{} {what}", b.depth)
        })
        .collect()
}

#[test]
fn blocks_keep_their_raw_text() {
    let d = doc("# T\n\n**Step 1 — write `x`**\n\n- Create `a.rs`\n- Modify `b.rs`\n\n---\n");
    assert_eq!(
        shape(&d),
        [
            "0 h1 T",
            "0 p **Step 1 — write `x`**",
            "0 liNone Create `a.rs`",
            "0 liNone Modify `b.rs`",
            "0 rule",
        ]
    );
    assert_eq!(d.source.slice(d.blocks[1].span), "**Step 1 — write `x`**");
}

#[test]
fn a_longer_fence_contains_shorter_fences() {
    let d = doc("````markdown\n```rust\nfn a() {}\n```\n````\n");
    assert_eq!(
        shape(&d),
        ["0 fence4 markdown \"```rust\\nfn a() {}\\n```\\n\""]
    );
}

#[test]
fn a_fence_in_a_list_item_is_rebuilt() {
    let d = doc("- Run it:\n\n  ```powershell\n  cargo test\n    indented\n  ```\n\nAfter.\n");
    assert_eq!(
        shape(&d),
        [
            "0 liNone Run it:",
            "1 fence3 powershell \"cargo test\\n  indented\\n\"",
            "0 p After.",
        ]
    );
    let Block::Fence { body, .. } = &d.blocks[1].block else {
        panic!("not a fence");
    };
    assert!(matches!(body, TextRef::Owned { .. }));
}

#[test]
fn a_top_level_fence_body_is_a_span() {
    let d = doc("```rust\nfn a() {}\n\nfn b() {}\n```\n");
    let Block::Fence { body, .. } = &d.blocks[0].block else {
        panic!("not a fence");
    };
    assert!(matches!(body, TextRef::Span(_)));
    assert_eq!(d.text(body), "fn a() {}\n\nfn b() {}\n");
}

#[test]
fn crlf_input_gives_lf_bodies() {
    let d = doc("```text\r\na\r\nb\r\n```\r\n");
    assert_eq!(shape(&d), ["0 fence3 text \"a\\nb\\n\""]);
}

#[test]
fn an_empty_fence_has_an_empty_body() {
    let d = doc("```diff\n```\n\nNext.\n");
    assert_eq!(shape(&d), ["0 fence3 diff \"\"", "0 p Next."]);
}

#[test]
fn task_items_quotes_tables_and_html() {
    let d = doc(
        "<!-- expect: x -->\n\n> **Note:** a\n> b\n\n| a | b |\n|---|---|\n| 1 | 2 |\n\n\
         - [ ] **Step 1: go**\n- [x] done\n",
    );
    assert_eq!(
        shape(&d),
        [
            "0 html <!-- expect: x -->",
            "0 quote > **Note:** a\n> b",
            "0 table | a | b |\n|---|---|\n| 1 | 2 |",
            "0 liSome(false) **Step 1: go**",
            "0 liSome(true) done",
        ]
    );
}

#[test]
fn nested_items_are_one_deeper() {
    let d = doc("1. outer\n   - inner\n\n     inner text\n2. next\n");
    assert_eq!(
        shape(&d),
        [
            "0 liNone outer",
            "1 liNone inner",
            "2 p inner text",
            "0 liNone next",
        ]
    );
}

#[test]
fn an_indented_code_block_is_a_fence_of_length_zero() {
    let d = doc("Para.\n\n    code\n");
    assert_eq!(shape(&d), ["0 p Para.", "0 fence0  \"code\\n\""]);
}

#[test]
fn a_fence_left_open_is_an_error() {
    let found = errors("# T\n\n```rust\nfn a() {}\n");
    assert_eq!(found.len(), 1);
    assert_eq!(found[0].code, "unclosed-fence");
    assert_eq!(found[0].span, Some(Span::new(5, 12)));
}

#[test]
fn a_fence_closed_by_the_next_blocks_fence_is_an_error() {
    let text = "```rust\n// never closed\n\n**Step 2**\n\n```powershell\ncargo test\n```\n";
    let found = errors(text);
    assert_eq!(found.len(), 1);
    assert_eq!(found[0].code, "unclosed-fence");
    assert_eq!(found[0].span, Some(Span::new(0, 7)));
    let related = found[0].related.first().map(|(span, _)| *span);
    assert_eq!(
        related.map(|s| &text[s.start as usize..s.end as usize]),
        Some("```powershell")
    );
}

#[test]
fn shorter_or_bare_inner_fences_are_legal() {
    assert!(errors("````markdown\n```rust\nx\n```\n````\n").is_empty());
    assert!(errors("```text\n~~~rust\nx\n```\n").is_empty());
    assert!(errors("```text\n    ```rust\n```\n").is_empty());
}

//! Reading a plan: UTF-8 check, BOM, line-ending normalization, fingerprint (decision D6).

use planrun_model::PlanSource;
use sha2::{Digest, Sha256};

use crate::diagnostic::Diagnostic;

/// The largest plan accepted, in bytes. Spans are `u32`; this keeps every offset far below that.
pub const MAX_PLAN_BYTES: usize = 64 * 1024 * 1024;

/// Reads `bytes` as the plan named `name`: refuses input over [`MAX_PLAN_BYTES`] (`too-large`)
/// and input that is not UTF-8 (`not-utf8`), strips a leading BOM, turns CRLF and lone CR into
/// LF, and fingerprints the bytes as read (hex SHA-256).
pub fn load(name: &str, bytes: &[u8]) -> Result<PlanSource, Diagnostic> {
    if bytes.len() > MAX_PLAN_BYTES {
        return Err(Diagnostic::error(
            "too-large",
            None,
            format!(
                "the plan is {} bytes; the limit is {MAX_PLAN_BYTES}",
                bytes.len()
            ),
        ));
    }
    let text = std::str::from_utf8(bytes).map_err(|e| {
        Diagnostic::error(
            "not-utf8",
            None,
            format!("byte {} is not valid UTF-8", e.valid_up_to()),
        )
    })?;
    Ok(PlanSource::from_normalized(
        name,
        normalize(text),
        hex(&Sha256::digest(bytes)),
    ))
}

/// The text without a leading BOM, with CRLF and lone CR as LF. Nothing else changes.
pub fn normalize(text: &str) -> String {
    let text = text.strip_prefix('\u{feff}').unwrap_or(text);
    if !text.contains('\r') {
        return text.to_owned();
    }
    text.replace("\r\n", "\n").replace('\r', "\n")
}

fn hex(bytes: &[u8]) -> String {
    const DIGITS: &[u8; 16] = b"0123456789abcdef";
    let mut out = String::with_capacity(bytes.len() * 2);
    for &b in bytes {
        out.push(char::from(DIGITS[usize::from(b >> 4)]));
        out.push(char::from(DIGITS[usize::from(b & 0x0f)]));
    }
    out
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn line_endings_and_bom_are_normalized() {
        assert_eq!(normalize("\u{feff}a\r\nb\rc\n"), "a\nb\nc\n");
        assert_eq!(normalize("a\n\r\n"), "a\n\n");
        assert_eq!(normalize("plain\n"), "plain\n");
    }

    #[test]
    fn the_fingerprint_is_of_the_bytes_as_read() {
        let crlf = load("p", b"a\r\n").unwrap_or_else(|e| panic!("{e:?}"));
        let lf = load("p", b"a\n").unwrap_or_else(|e| panic!("{e:?}"));
        assert_eq!(crlf.text(), lf.text());
        assert_ne!(crlf.fingerprint, lf.fingerprint);
        assert_eq!(
            &*lf.fingerprint,
            "87428fc522803d31065e7bce3cf03fe475096631e5e07bbd7a0fde60c4cf25c7"
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn invalid_utf8_is_refused() {
        let err = load("p", b"ok\xff").err().map(|d| (d.code, d.message));
        assert_eq!(
            err,
            Some(("not-utf8", "byte 2 is not valid UTF-8".to_owned()))
        );
    }
}

### P1-T3: specs, registry, outline, format selection

**Goal:** `SpecFormat::compile` (every regex and its required captures checked when the spec loads), `Registry::add_spec` and `Registry::select` (`no-format`, `ambiguous-format`, `unknown-format`), and the generic outline: tasks after `tasks_after`, ids, ordinals and phase, the manifest consumed by the outline, the intro step, step ranges, the text after the tasks.

**Files**

- Modify `crates/planrun-model/src/plan.rs`
- Modify `crates/planrun-parse/src/document.rs`
- Modify `crates/planrun-parse/src/format.rs`
- Modify `crates/planrun-parse/src/lib.rs`
- Create `crates/planrun-parse/src/outline.rs`
- Create `crates/planrun-parse/src/outline_tests.rs`
- Modify `crates/planrun-parse/src/registry.rs`
- Create `crates/planrun-parse/src/registry_tests.rs`
- Modify `crates/planrun-parse/src/spec.rs`

`Registry` no longer derives `Default` (it gave a threshold of 0); `Default` now calls `new`. `PlanMeta` gains `after_tasks`, the span of the text after the tasks that `show` lists as never run. `with_builtins` still registers nothing: the kinds come in P1-T4. `document.rs` is over 300 lines, so its change comes as a diff.

```rust
//! Plan, task, step and the task's file manifest.

use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};

use crate::action::Action;
use crate::ids::{FormatId, TaskId};
use crate::path::RepoPath;
use crate::source::PlanSource;
use crate::span::{Span, TextRef};

/// A parsed, checked plan. Owns its source; everything else refers into it.
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct Plan {
    /// The normalized plan text.
    pub source: PlanSource,
    /// The format that parsed it.
    pub format: FormatId,
    /// Plan-level facts.
    pub meta: PlanMeta,
    /// Tasks in document order.
    pub tasks: Vec<Task>,
}

impl Plan {
    /// The text of `text`.
    pub fn text<'a>(&'a self, text: &'a TextRef) -> &'a str {
        self.source.resolve(text)
    }
}

/// Plan-level facts. Phases are metadata, not a level of the tree (decision D5).
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Default, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct PlanMeta {
    /// The first level-1 heading.
    pub title: Option<TextRef>,
    /// The phase id when the format captures one (`P1`, `P3c-1`).
    pub phase: Option<Box<str>>,
    /// The base commit the plan says to start from, when stated.
    pub base_commit: Option<Box<str>>,
    /// Paragraphs before the first task that match the format's `manual_markers`: text the user
    /// must read before task 1.
    pub preamble: Vec<TextRef>,
    /// The text after the tasks (`## Part 1 green state`, …): shown, never run.
    pub after_tasks: Option<Span>,
}

/// One task.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct Task {
    /// Its id.
    pub id: TaskId,
    /// Its title.
    pub title: TextRef,
    /// Heading through the last block before the next task.
    pub span: Span,
    /// The declared files. Empty when the format has none for this task.
    pub manifest: Manifest,
    /// Steps in order. A format without step headings yields one implicit step.
    pub steps: Vec<Step>,
}

/// One step.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct Step {
    /// Its number, when written; must count up from 1 within the task.
    pub number: Option<u32>,
    /// Its title, when written.
    pub title: Option<TextRef>,
    /// Heading through the last block of the step.
    pub span: Span,
    /// Actions in order.
    pub actions: Vec<Action>,
}

/// The files a task declares: its write set (check rule `manifest`).
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Default, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct Manifest {
    /// Entries in order.
    pub entries: Vec<ManifestEntry>,
    /// The manifest block, when present.
    pub span: Option<Span>,
}

/// One declared file.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct ManifestEntry {
    /// The file.
    pub path: RepoPath,
    /// What the task does to it.
    pub change: ChangeKind,
    /// A line range written after the path (`CleanupStep.cs:3-10`); informational.
    pub lines: Option<LineRange>,
    /// The entry's text.
    pub span: Span,
}

/// What a task declares it does to a file.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub enum ChangeKind {
    /// New file.
    Create,
    /// Existing file, changed.
    Modify,
    /// Existing file, replaced whole.
    Rewrite,
    /// Removed.
    Delete,
    /// A test file (created or modified; the actions decide).
    Test,
}

/// `start-end`, 1-based inclusive.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct LineRange {
    /// First line.
    pub start: u32,
    /// Last line.
    pub end: u32,
}

File `crates/planrun-parse/src/document.rs` (exact diff; save the block as `$env:TEMP\p1-t3.patch`, outside the repository, then apply it):

```diff
diff --git a/crates/planrun-parse/src/document.rs b/crates/planrun-parse/src/document.rs
index 52260ae..d6719e3 100644
--- a/crates/planrun-parse/src/document.rs
+++ b/crates/planrun-parse/src/document.rs
@@ -107,12 +107,27 @@ impl Document {
         self.source.resolve(text)
     }
 
-    /// The byte ranges of every fence, in order (format detection skips them).
-    pub fn fence_spans(&self) -> impl Iterator<Item = Span> + '_ {
-        self.blocks
+    /// The lines outside fenced blocks, without their newline (format detection reads only
+    /// these).
+    pub fn lines_outside_fences(&self) -> Vec<&str> {
+        let mut fences = self
+            .blocks
             .iter()
             .filter(|b| matches!(b.block, Block::Fence { .. }))
             .map(|b| b.span)
+            .peekable();
+        let mut lines = Vec::new();
+        let mut at = 0usize;
+        for line in self.source.text().split_inclusive('\n') {
+            let start = at;
+            at += line.len();
+            while fences.next_if(|f| f.end as usize <= start).is_some() {}
+            let inside = fences.peek().is_some_and(|f| f.start as usize <= start);
+            if !inside {
+                lines.push(line.trim_end_matches('\n'));
+            }
+        }
+        lines
     }
 }

File `crates/planrun-parse/src/format.rs` (full contents):

```rust
//! A plan format: how to find tasks, manifests and steps, and which recognizers turn a step's
//! blocks into actions (docs/architecture.md §5).

use std::sync::Arc;

use planrun_model::{FormatId, Manifest, PlanMeta, Shell, Span, TaskId, TextRef};

use crate::diagnostic::Diagnostics;
use crate::document::{BlockRange, Document};
use crate::recognizer::Recognizer;

/// A plan format. Most formats are a [`crate::spec::SpecFormat`] built from a TOML spec; a
/// format whose structure is not heading-shaped implements this trait by hand.
pub trait Format: Send + Sync {
    /// Its id (`assay`).
    fn id(&self) -> &FormatId;

    /// How well `doc` matches, 0–100. Cheap: patterns over the lines outside fenced blocks.
    fn detect(&self, doc: &Document) -> u8;

    /// The plan's structure: metadata, tasks, their manifests and step ranges. Every block of
    /// every task must fall in exactly one step range (the recognizers then account for each).
    fn outline(&self, doc: &Document) -> Result<Outline, Diagnostics>;

    /// The recognizers, in the order they are tried at each position of a step.
    fn recognizers(&self) -> &[Arc<dyn Recognizer>];

    /// Format-level settings the recognizers and checks read.
    fn settings(&self) -> &FormatSettings;
}

/// The structure of a plan before recognition.
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct Outline {
    /// Plan metadata.
    pub meta: PlanMeta,
    /// Tasks in order.
    pub tasks: Vec<OutlineTask>,
    /// The blocks after the tasks (from a heading at or above `tasks_end_level`): never run.
    pub after_tasks: Option<BlockRange>,
}

/// A task before recognition.
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct OutlineTask {
    /// Its id.
    pub id: TaskId,
    /// Its title.
    pub title: TextRef,
    /// Its span.
    pub span: Span,
    /// The parsed manifest.
    pub manifest: Manifest,
    /// The manifest's blocks (marker and items): consumed by the outline, in no step. Recognition
    /// skips them where they fall inside a step's range.
    pub manifest_blocks: Option<BlockRange>,
    /// Steps: number, title, blocks.
    pub steps: Vec<OutlineStep>,
}

/// A step before recognition.
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct OutlineStep {
    /// Its number, when written.
    pub number: Option<u32>,
    /// Its title, when written.
    pub title: Option<TextRef>,
    /// Its span.
    pub span: Span,
    /// The blocks the recognizers consume.
    pub blocks: BlockRange,
}

/// Settings shared by a format's recognizers and the checks.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Default)]
pub struct FormatSettings {
    /// Paragraphs matching any of these become `Manual(Marked)` instead of `Note`.
    pub manual_markers: Vec<regex::Regex>,
    /// Fence info strings that run in pwsh / bash. Anything else is `Manual(ForeignLanguage)`.
    pub pwsh_languages: Vec<String>,
    /// See `pwsh_languages`.
    pub bash_languages: Vec<String>,
}

impl FormatSettings {
    /// The shell that runs a fence in `language`, if any.
    pub fn shell_for(&self, language: &str) -> Option<Shell> {
        if self.pwsh_languages.iter().any(|l| l == language) {
            return Some(Shell::Pwsh);
        }
        if self.bash_languages.iter().any(|l| l == language) {
            return Some(Shell::Bash);
        }
        None
    }

    /// True when `text` matches one of the manual markers.
    pub fn is_marked(&self, text: &str) -> bool {
        self.manual_markers.iter().any(|m| m.is_match(text))
    }
}

```rust
//! Markdown plan → typed [`planrun_model::Plan`] (docs/architecture.md §4–§5).
//!
//! Pipeline: read and normalize ([`source`]) → block stream ([`document`]) → pick a format
//! ([`registry`]) → outline: tasks, manifests, steps ([`format`]) → recognizers turn each step's
//! blocks into actions ([`recognizer`]) → static checks ([`check`]).
//!
//! This crate never touches the repository and never runs anything (decision D2). Checks that
//! need the repository (preimage blob ids against the files on disk) live in planrun-exec.
//!
//! Extension points, in the order a new plan style should try them (docs/extending.md):
//! 1. a format spec in TOML that reuses the built-in recognizer kinds ([`spec`]);
//! 2. a new [`recognizer::RecognizerFactory`] for a new idiom;
//! 3. a hand-written [`format::Format`] when the outline is not heading-shaped.

pub mod check;
pub mod diagnostic;
pub mod document;
pub mod format;
pub mod outline;
pub mod recognizer;
pub mod registry;
pub mod source;
pub mod spec;

pub use diagnostic::{Diagnostic, Diagnostics, Severity};
pub use registry::Registry;

use planrun_model::Plan;

/// Parses and checks `bytes` as a plan named `name`, with the formats in `registry`.
///
/// `format` forces a format by id; otherwise the one with the highest detection score wins, and
/// a tie or a score below the registry's threshold is an error (never a guess).
///
/// Errors and warnings come back together; a plan is returned only when there is no error
/// (`Never execute a partially parsed plan`, decision D3).
pub fn parse_plan(
    registry: &Registry,
    name: &str,
    bytes: &[u8],
    format: Option<&str>,
) -> Result<(Plan, Diagnostics), Diagnostics> {
    let _ = (registry, name, bytes, format);
    Err(Diagnostics::single(Diagnostic::error(
        "not-implemented",
        None,
        "parse_plan is implemented in Phase 1 (docs/roadmap.md)",
    )))
}

```rust
//! The generic outline of a heading-shaped plan (docs/architecture.md §4.3): plan metadata, the
//! tasks, each task's manifest and its steps. [`crate::spec::SpecFormat`] uses it; a hand-written
//! format may too.

use planrun_model::{
    ChangeKind, LineRange, Manifest, ManifestEntry, PlanMeta, RepoPath, Span, TaskId, TextRef,
};
use regex::{Captures, Regex};

use crate::diagnostic::{Diagnostic, Diagnostics};
use crate::document::{Block, BlockRange, Document};
use crate::format::{FormatSettings, Outline, OutlineStep, OutlineTask};

/// The compiled `[outline]` of a spec.
pub struct OutlineRules {
    /// Tasks start after this heading, when set.
    pub tasks_after: Option<HeadingRule>,
    /// A task heading; captures `id`, `ord`, `title`, optionally `phase`.
    pub task: HeadingRule,
    /// A heading at or above this level after a task ends the tasks.
    pub tasks_end_level: Option<u8>,
    /// The manifest of a task, when the format has one.
    pub manifest: Option<ManifestRules>,
    /// Step boundaries.
    pub steps: Vec<StepRule>,
}

/// A heading of one level whose text matches.
pub struct HeadingRule {
    /// 1–6.
    pub level: u8,
    /// Over the heading text.
    pub regex: Regex,
}

/// A manifest: a marker paragraph and the list items after it.
pub struct ManifestRules {
    /// Over the marker paragraph.
    pub marker: Regex,
    /// Over each item; captures `change` and `paths`.
    pub item: Regex,
    /// The captured change word and what it means.
    pub changes: Vec<(String, ChangeKind)>,
    /// Splits `paths` into several, when set.
    pub path_list: Option<Regex>,
}

/// A block that starts a step.
pub struct StepRule {
    /// Which kind of block.
    pub block: StepBlock,
    /// Over its text; may capture `n` and `title`.
    pub regex: Regex,
}

/// The kinds of block a step can start with.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum StepBlock {
    /// A paragraph (`**Step 1 — …**`).
    Paragraph,
    /// A list item (`- [ ] **Step 1: …**`).
    ListItem,
    /// A heading.
    Heading,
}

/// The outline of `doc` under `rules`. Every error is collected; any error means no outline.
pub fn outline(
    rules: &OutlineRules,
    settings: &FormatSettings,
    doc: &Document,
) -> Result<Outline, Diagnostics> {
    let mut errors = Vec::new();
    let start = tasks_start(rules, doc).map_err(Diagnostics::single)?;
    let (heads, end) = task_heads(rules, doc, start, &mut errors);
    let Some(&first) = heads.first() else {
        errors.push(Diagnostic::error(
            "no-tasks",
            None,
            "no heading matches the format's task pattern",
        ));
        return Err(Diagnostics(errors));
    };
    let mut tasks = Vec::with_capacity(heads.len());
    let mut phase = None;
    for (k, &head) in heads.iter().enumerate() {
        let next = heads.get(k + 1).copied().unwrap_or(end);
        let (task, task_phase) = task(rules, doc, head, next, &mut errors);
        phase = phase.or(task_phase);
        tasks.push(task);
    }
    if !errors.is_empty() {
        return Err(Diagnostics(errors));
    }
    let after_tasks = (end < doc.blocks.len()).then_some(BlockRange {
        start: end,
        end: doc.blocks.len(),
    });
    let meta = PlanMeta {
        title: title(doc, first),
        phase,
        base_commit: None,
        preamble: marked(
            doc,
            settings,
            BlockRange {
                start: 0,
                end: first,
            },
        ),
        after_tasks: after_tasks.map(|r| {
            Span::new(
                doc.blocks[r.start].span.start,
                offset(doc.source.text().len()),
            )
        }),
    };
    Ok(Outline {
        meta,
        tasks,
        after_tasks,
    })
}

/// The paragraphs, list items and quotes of `range` that match a manual marker.
pub fn marked(doc: &Document, settings: &FormatSettings, range: BlockRange) -> Vec<TextRef> {
    doc.blocks[range.start..range.end]
        .iter()
        .filter_map(|b| match &b.block {
            Block::Paragraph { text } | Block::ListItem { text, .. } | Block::Quote { text } => {
                Some(text)
            }
            _ => None,
        })
        .filter(|text| settings.is_marked(doc.text(text)))
        .cloned()
        .collect()
}

fn tasks_start(rules: &OutlineRules, doc: &Document) -> Result<usize, Diagnostic> {
    let Some(rule) = &rules.tasks_after else {
        return Ok(0);
    };
    doc.blocks
        .iter()
        .position(|b| heading_matches(doc, &b.block, rule))
        .map(|i| i + 1)
        .ok_or_else(|| {
            Diagnostic::error(
                "no-tasks",
                None,
                format!(
                    "no level-{} heading matches `{}`, so there are no tasks",
                    rule.level,
                    rule.regex.as_str()
                ),
            )
        })
}

/// The task headings from `start`, and the index where the tasks end.
fn task_heads(
    rules: &OutlineRules,
    doc: &Document,
    start: usize,
    errors: &mut Vec<Diagnostic>,
) -> (Vec<usize>, usize) {
    let mut heads = Vec::new();
    for (i, located) in doc.blocks.iter().enumerate().skip(start) {
        let Block::Heading { level, .. } = &located.block else {
            continue;
        };
        if heading_matches(doc, &located.block, &rules.task) {
            heads.push(i);
            continue;
        }
        let in_tasks = !heads.is_empty() || rules.tasks_after.is_some();
        if !heads.is_empty() && rules.tasks_end_level.is_some_and(|l| *level <= l) {
            return (heads, i);
        }
        if in_tasks && *level == rules.task.level {
            errors.push(Diagnostic::error(
                "bad-task-heading",
                Some(located.span),
                format!(
                    "a level-{level} heading among the tasks that is not a task: it does not match `{}`",
                    rules.task.regex.as_str()
                ),
            ));
        }
    }
    (heads, doc.blocks.len())
}

fn heading_matches(doc: &Document, block: &Block, rule: &HeadingRule) -> bool {
    let Block::Heading { level, text } = block else {
        return false;
    };
    *level == rule.level && rule.regex.is_match(doc.text(text))
}

fn title(doc: &Document, before: usize) -> Option<TextRef> {
    doc.blocks[..before].iter().find_map(|b| match &b.block {
        Block::Heading { level: 1, text } => Some(text.clone()),
        _ => None,
    })
}

/// One task: the heading at `head`, its blocks up to `next`. Returns its phase capture.
fn task(
    rules: &OutlineRules,
    doc: &Document,
    head: usize,
    next: usize,
    errors: &mut Vec<Diagnostic>,
) -> (OutlineTask, Option<Box<str>>) {
    let located = &doc.blocks[head];
    let heading = match &located.block {
        Block::Heading { text, .. } => text.clone(),
        _ => TextRef::Span(located.span),
    };
    let text = doc.text(&heading);
    let base = heading.span().start;
    let caps = rules.task.regex.captures(text);
    let capture = |name: &str| capture_ref(caps.as_ref(), name, base);
    let raw = caps
        .as_ref()
        .and_then(|c| c.name("id"))
        .map_or(text, |m| m.as_str());
    let ordinal = caps
        .as_ref()
        .and_then(|c| c.name("ord"))
        .and_then(|m| m.as_str().parse::<u32>().ok());
    if ordinal.is_none() {
        errors.push(Diagnostic::error(
            "bad-task-id",
            Some(located.span),
            "the task's `ord` capture is not a number",
        ));
    }
    let phase = caps
        .as_ref()
        .and_then(|c| c.name("phase"))
        .map(|m| m.as_str().into());
    let body = BlockRange {
        start: head + 1,
        end: next,
    };
    let (manifest, manifest_blocks) = manifest(rules, doc, head, body, errors);
    let steps = steps(rules, doc, body, manifest_blocks, errors);
    let span = Span::new(
        located.span.start,
        range_end(doc, body).unwrap_or(located.span.end),
    );
    let task = OutlineTask {
        id: TaskId {
            raw: raw.into(),
            ordinal: ordinal.unwrap_or(0),
        },
        title: capture("title").unwrap_or(heading),
        span,
        manifest,
        manifest_blocks,
        steps,
    };
    (task, phase)
}

fn capture_ref(caps: Option<&Captures<'_>>, name: &str, base: u32) -> Option<TextRef> {
    let m = caps?.name(name)?;
    Some(TextRef::Span(Span::new(
        base + offset(m.start()),
        base + offset(m.end()),
    )))
}

/// The manifest: the first block before any step boundary that matches the marker, and the list
/// items right after it.
fn manifest(
    rules: &OutlineRules,
    doc: &Document,
    head: usize,
    body: BlockRange,
    errors: &mut Vec<Diagnostic>,
) -> (Manifest, Option<BlockRange>) {
    let Some(spec) = &rules.manifest else {
        return (Manifest::default(), None);
    };
    let marker = (body.start..body.end)
        .take_while(|&i| boundary(rules, doc, i).is_none())
        .find(|&i| matches!(&doc.blocks[i].block, Block::Paragraph { text } if spec.marker.is_match(doc.text(text))));
    let Some(marker) = marker else {
        errors.push(Diagnostic::error(
            "manifest-missing",
            Some(doc.blocks[head].span),
            format!("the task has no manifest (`{}`)", spec.marker.as_str()),
        ));
        return (Manifest::default(), None);
    };
    let depth = doc.blocks[marker].depth;
    let items_end = (marker + 1..body.end)
        .find(|&i| {
            let b = &doc.blocks[i];
            !matches!(b.block, Block::ListItem { .. }) || b.depth != depth
        })
        .unwrap_or(body.end);
    if items_end == marker + 1 {
        errors.push(Diagnostic::error(
            "manifest-item",
            Some(doc.blocks[marker].span),
            "the manifest has no items",
        ));
    }
    let mut entries = Vec::new();
    for i in marker + 1..items_end {
        manifest_item(spec, doc, i, &mut entries, errors);
    }
    let range = BlockRange {
        start: marker,
        end: items_end,
    };
    let span = Span::new(
        doc.blocks[marker].span.start,
        range_end(doc, range).unwrap_or(doc.blocks[marker].span.end),
    );
    let manifest = Manifest {
        entries,
        span: Some(span),
    };
    (manifest, Some(range))
}

fn manifest_item(
    spec: &ManifestRules,
    doc: &Document,
    index: usize,
    entries: &mut Vec<ManifestEntry>,
    errors: &mut Vec<Diagnostic>,
) {
    let located = &doc.blocks[index];
    let Block::ListItem { text, .. } = &located.block else {
        return;
    };
    let base = text.span().start;
    let item = doc.text(text);
    let parsed = spec.item.captures(item).and_then(|caps| {
        let change = caps.name("change")?.as_str();
        let kind = spec.changes.iter().find(|(w, _)| w == change)?.1;
        Some((kind, caps.name("paths")?))
    });
    let Some((change, paths)) = parsed else {
        errors.push(Diagnostic::error(
            "manifest-item",
            Some(located.span),
            format!(
                "a manifest item that does not match `{}`",
                spec.item.as_str()
            ),
        ));
        return;
    };
    let pieces: Vec<&str> = match &spec.path_list {
        Some(split) => split.split(paths.as_str()).collect(),
        None => vec![paths.as_str()],
    };
    let paths_span = Span::new(base + offset(paths.start()), base + offset(paths.end()));
    for piece in pieces.iter().map(|p| p.trim()) {
        let (path, lines) = split_lines(piece);
        match RepoPath::new(path) {
            Ok(path) => entries.push(ManifestEntry {
                path,
                change,
                lines,
                span: located.span,
            }),
            Err(e) => errors.push(Diagnostic::error(
                "bad-path",
                Some(paths_span),
                e.to_string(),
            )),
        }
    }
}

/// `src/a.rs:3-10` → (`src/a.rs`, 3–10); `src/a.rs:7` → (`src/a.rs`, 7–7).
fn split_lines(path: &str) -> (&str, Option<LineRange>) {
    let Some((file, range)) = path.rsplit_once(':') else {
        return (path, None);
    };
    let (start, end) = range.split_once('-').unwrap_or((range, range));
    match (start.parse(), end.parse()) {
        (Ok(start), Ok(end)) => (file, Some(LineRange { start, end })),
        _ => (path, None),
    }
}

/// A step boundary's `n` capture.
enum StepNumber {
    /// No `n` capture.
    Unwritten,
    /// A number.
    Written(u32),
    /// Too large for `u32`.
    Bad,
}

/// The step that block `index` starts, as (number, title), when it is a step boundary.
fn boundary(
    rules: &OutlineRules,
    doc: &Document,
    index: usize,
) -> Option<(StepNumber, Option<TextRef>)> {
    let (kind, text) = match &doc.blocks[index].block {
        Block::Paragraph { text } => (StepBlock::Paragraph, text),
        Block::ListItem { text, .. } => (StepBlock::ListItem, text),
        Block::Heading { text, .. } => (StepBlock::Heading, text),
        _ => return None,
    };
    let base = text.span().start;
    let caps = rules
        .steps
        .iter()
        .filter(|r| r.block == kind)
        .find_map(|r| r.regex.captures(doc.text(text)))?;
    let number = match caps.name("n").map(|m| m.as_str().parse::<u32>()) {
        None => StepNumber::Unwritten,
        Some(Ok(n)) => StepNumber::Written(n),
        Some(Err(_)) => StepNumber::Bad,
    };
    Some((number, capture_ref(Some(&caps), "title", base)))
}

fn steps(
    rules: &OutlineRules,
    doc: &Document,
    body: BlockRange,
    manifest: Option<BlockRange>,
    errors: &mut Vec<Diagnostic>,
) -> Vec<OutlineStep> {
    let outside = |i: &usize| !manifest.is_some_and(|m| m.contains(*i));
    let boundaries: Vec<usize> = (body.start..body.end)
        .filter(outside)
        .filter(|&i| boundary(rules, doc, i).is_some())
        .collect();
    let mut steps = Vec::with_capacity(boundaries.len() + 1);
    let intro_end = boundaries.first().copied().unwrap_or(body.end);
    let intro = BlockRange {
        start: body.start,
        end: intro_end,
    };
    if (intro.start..intro.end).any(|i| outside(&i)) {
        steps.push(OutlineStep {
            number: None,
            title: None,
            span: range_span(doc, intro, manifest).unwrap_or(Span::new(0, 0)),
            blocks: intro,
        });
    }
    for (k, &b) in boundaries.iter().enumerate() {
        let blocks = BlockRange {
            start: b + 1,
            end: boundaries.get(k + 1).copied().unwrap_or(body.end),
        };
        let Some((number, title)) = boundary(rules, doc, b) else {
            continue;
        };
        let start = doc.blocks[b].span;
        let number = match number {
            StepNumber::Written(n) => Some(n),
            StepNumber::Bad => {
                errors.push(Diagnostic::error(
                    "step-order",
                    Some(start),
                    "the step number is too large",
                ));
                None
            }
            StepNumber::Unwritten => None,
        };
        steps.push(OutlineStep {
            number,
            title,
            span: Span::new(start.start, range_end(doc, blocks).unwrap_or(start.end)),
            blocks,
        });
    }
    steps
}

/// The span from the first to the last block of `range` outside `skip`.
fn range_span(doc: &Document, range: BlockRange, skip: Option<BlockRange>) -> Option<Span> {
    let mut inside = (range.start..range.end).filter(|i| !skip.is_some_and(|s| s.contains(*i)));
    let first = inside.next()?;
    let last = inside.next_back().unwrap_or(first);
    Some(Span::new(
        doc.blocks[first].span.start,
        doc.blocks[last].span.end,
    ))
}

fn range_end(doc: &Document, range: BlockRange) -> Option<u32> {
    (!range.is_empty()).then(|| doc.blocks[range.end - 1].span.end)
}

fn offset(value: usize) -> u32 {
    u32::try_from(value).unwrap_or(u32::MAX)
}

File `crates/planrun-parse/src/outline_tests.rs` (full contents):

```rust
use planrun_model::ChangeKind;

use super::*;
use crate::format::Format;
use crate::source;
use crate::spec::{FormatSpec, SpecFormat};

const SPEC: &str = r#"
id = "t"
name = "test"
[detect]
patterns = [{ regex = '^### P\d+', weight = 60 }]
[outline]
tasks_after = { level = 2, regex = '^Tasks$' }
task = { level = 3, regex = '^(?P<id>(?P<phase>P\d+[a-z]?(?:-\d+)?)-T(?P<ord>\d+)): (?P<title>.+)$' }
tasks_end_level = 2
[outline.manifest]
marker = '^\*\*Files\*\*$'
item = '^(?P<change>Create|Modify|Delete) `(?P<paths>[^`]+)`(?: \(.+\))?$'
changes = { Create = "create", Modify = "modify", Delete = "delete" }
[[outline.steps]]
block = "paragraph"
regex = '^\*\*Step (?P<n>\d+) — (?P<title>.+)\*\*$'
[settings]
manual_markers = ['(?i)\bby hand\b']
"#;

fn format() -> SpecFormat {
    let spec: FormatSpec = toml::from_str(SPEC).unwrap_or_else(|e| panic!("{e}"));
    SpecFormat::compile(spec, |_| None).unwrap_or_else(|e| panic!("{e}"))
}

fn doc(text: &str) -> Document {
    let source = source::load("t.md", text.as_bytes()).unwrap_or_else(|e| panic!("{e:?}"));
    Document::build(source).unwrap_or_else(|e| panic!("{e:?}"))
}

fn outline_of(text: &str) -> (Document, Result<Outline, Diagnostics>) {
    let d = doc(text);
    let outline = format().outline(&d);
    (d, outline)
}

fn codes(result: Result<Outline, Diagnostics>) -> Vec<&'static str> {
    match result {
        Ok(_) => Vec::new(),
        Err(d) => d.0.iter().map(|d| d.code).collect(),
    }
}

const PLAN: &str = "# Plan title

Check this by hand before starting.

## Tasks

### P3c-1-T1: first

**Goal:** one.

**Files**

- Create `src/a.rs`
- Modify `web/lock.json` (restored in step 1)

`src/gen.rs` is generated by step 2, not written by hand.

**Step 1 — write *the* files**

Text.

**Step 2 — verify**

More.

### P3c-1-T4: second

**Files**

- Delete `src/old.rs`

**Step 1 — delete**

Delete it.

## Green state

Run by hand.
";

#[test]
fn tasks_manifests_and_steps() {
    let (d, outline) = outline_of(PLAN);
    let outline = outline.unwrap_or_else(|e| panic!("{e:?}"));
    let text = |t: &TextRef| d.text(t).to_owned();
    assert_eq!(
        outline.meta.title.as_ref().map(text).as_deref(),
        Some("Plan title")
    );
    assert_eq!(outline.meta.phase.as_deref(), Some("P3c-1"));
    assert_eq!(
        outline.meta.preamble.iter().map(text).collect::<Vec<_>>(),
        ["Check this by hand before starting."]
    );
    let after = outline.after_tasks.map(|r| r.len());
    assert_eq!(after, Some(2));
    let after_span = outline
        .meta
        .after_tasks
        .map(|s| d.source.slice(s).to_owned());
    assert_eq!(
        after_span.as_deref(),
        Some("## Green state\n\nRun by hand.\n")
    );

    let ids: Vec<(String, u32, String)> = outline
        .tasks
        .iter()
        .map(|t| (t.id.raw.to_string(), t.id.ordinal, text(&t.title)))
        .collect();
    assert_eq!(
        ids,
        [
            ("P3c-1-T1".to_owned(), 1, "first".to_owned()),
            ("P3c-1-T4".to_owned(), 4, "second".to_owned())
        ]
    );

    let first = &outline.tasks[0];
    let entries: Vec<(String, ChangeKind)> = first
        .manifest
        .entries
        .iter()
        .map(|e| (e.path.to_string(), e.change))
        .collect();
    assert_eq!(
        entries,
        [
            ("src/a.rs".to_owned(), ChangeKind::Create),
            ("web/lock.json".to_owned(), ChangeKind::Modify)
        ]
    );
    let steps: Vec<(Option<u32>, Option<String>, Vec<String>)> = first
        .steps
        .iter()
        .map(|s| {
            let blocks = (s.blocks.start..s.blocks.end)
                .filter(|i| !first.manifest_blocks.is_some_and(|m| m.contains(*i)))
                .map(|i| d.source.slice(d.blocks[i].span).to_owned())
                .collect();
            (s.number, s.title.as_ref().map(text), blocks)
        })
        .collect();
    assert_eq!(
        steps,
        [
            (
                None,
                None,
                vec![
                    "**Goal:** one.".to_owned(),
                    "`src/gen.rs` is generated by step 2, not written by hand.".to_owned()
                ]
            ),
            (
                Some(1),
                Some("write *the* files".to_owned()),
                vec!["Text.".to_owned()]
            ),
            (Some(2), Some("verify".to_owned()), vec!["More.".to_owned()]),
        ]
    );
    assert_eq!(first.manifest_blocks.map(|m| m.len()), Some(3));
}

#[test]
fn a_task_level_heading_that_is_no_task_is_an_error() {
    let (_, outline) =
        outline_of("## Tasks\n\n### P1-T1: a\n\n**Files**\n\n- Create `a`\n\n### P1-T2 b\n\nx\n");
    assert_eq!(codes(outline), ["bad-task-heading"]);
}

#[test]
fn manifest_errors() {
    let missing = outline_of("## Tasks\n\n### P1-T1: a\n\n**Step 1 — x**\n\ny\n").1;
    assert_eq!(codes(missing), ["manifest-missing"]);
    let item = outline_of("## Tasks\n\n### P1-T1: a\n\n**Files**\n\n- Rename `a`\n").1;
    assert_eq!(codes(item), ["manifest-item"]);
    let empty = outline_of("## Tasks\n\n### P1-T1: a\n\n**Files**\n\nNothing.\n").1;
    assert_eq!(codes(empty), ["manifest-item"]);
    let path = outline_of("## Tasks\n\n### P1-T1: a\n\n**Files**\n\n- Create `../x`\n").1;
    assert_eq!(codes(path), ["bad-path"]);
}

#[test]
fn no_tasks_heading_or_no_task_is_an_error() {
    assert_eq!(codes(outline_of("# T\n\n### P1-T1: a\n").1), ["no-tasks"]);
    assert_eq!(
        codes(outline_of("# T\n\n## Tasks\n\nNone.\n").1),
        ["no-tasks"]
    );
}

#[test]
fn a_task_without_step_boundaries_is_one_implicit_step() {
    let (_, outline) =
        outline_of("## Tasks\n\n### P1-T1: a\n\n**Files**\n\n- Create `a`\n\nx\n\ny\n");
    let outline = outline.unwrap_or_else(|e| panic!("{e:?}"));
    let steps = &outline.tasks[0].steps;
    assert_eq!(steps.len(), 1);
    assert_eq!(steps[0].number, None);
}

#[test]
fn line_ranges_are_split_from_paths() {
    assert_eq!(
        split_lines("src/a.rs:3-10"),
        ("src/a.rs", Some(LineRange { start: 3, end: 10 }))
    );
    assert_eq!(
        split_lines("src/a.rs:7"),
        ("src/a.rs", Some(LineRange { start: 7, end: 7 }))
    );
    assert_eq!(split_lines("src/a.rs"), ("src/a.rs", None));
    assert_eq!(split_lines("a:b"), ("a:b", None));
}

File `crates/planrun-parse/src/registry.rs` (full contents):

```rust
//! The registry: recognizer factories by kind, and the formats built from specs with them.
//!
//! Composition root of parsing. Built-ins register in [`Registry::with_builtins`]; user formats
//! are added from TOML (`.planrun/formats/*.toml` in the repository, then the user config
//! directory) with [`Registry::add_spec`]. A spec that names an unknown recognizer kind, has an
//! invalid regex or duplicates a format id fails here, before any plan is read.

use std::collections::HashMap;
use std::sync::Arc;

use crate::diagnostic::Diagnostic;
use crate::document::Document;
use crate::format::Format;
use crate::recognizer::RecognizerFactory;
use crate::spec::{FormatSpec, SpecFormat};

/// Recognizer factories and formats.
pub struct Registry {
    factories: HashMap<&'static str, Arc<dyn RecognizerFactory>>,
    formats: Vec<Arc<dyn Format>>,
    /// Detection scores below this never select a format.
    pub min_score: u8,
}

/// Why a factory, spec or format was refused.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, thiserror::Error)]
pub enum RegistryError {
    /// Two factories with one kind.
    #[error("recognizer kind `{0}` is registered twice")]
    DuplicateKind(String),
    /// Two formats with one id.
    #[error("format `{0}` is registered twice")]
    DuplicateFormat(String),
    /// A spec names a kind no factory provides.
    #[error("format `{format}`: unknown recognizer kind `{kind}`")]
    UnknownKind {
        /// The format.
        format: String,
        /// The kind.
        kind: String,
    },
    /// A spec does not parse or a factory refused its parameters.
    #[error("format `{format}`: {message}")]
    InvalidSpec {
        /// The format (or the file name when the id is unreadable).
        format: String,
        /// What is wrong.
        message: String,
    },
}

impl Default for Registry {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self::new()
    }
}

impl Registry {
    /// An empty registry with the default threshold (50).
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self {
            factories: HashMap::new(),
            formats: Vec::new(),
            min_score: 50,
        }
    }

    /// The built-in recognizer kinds and formats (Phase 1: the `assay` format and the kinds it
    /// uses; Phase 2: the other three).
    pub fn with_builtins() -> Result<Self, RegistryError> {
        Ok(Self::new())
    }

    /// Registers a recognizer factory.
    pub fn add_factory(
        &mut self,
        factory: Arc<dyn RecognizerFactory>,
    ) -> Result<(), RegistryError> {
        let kind = factory.kind();
        if self.factories.insert(kind, factory).is_some() {
            return Err(RegistryError::DuplicateKind(kind.to_owned()));
        }
        Ok(())
    }

    /// Registers a hand-written format.
    pub fn add_format(&mut self, format: Arc<dyn Format>) -> Result<(), RegistryError> {
        if self.formats.iter().any(|f| f.id() == format.id()) {
            return Err(RegistryError::DuplicateFormat(format.id().0.to_string()));
        }
        self.formats.push(format);
        Ok(())
    }

    /// Builds a format from TOML spec text and registers it. `origin` is the spec's file stem,
    /// which must equal its `id` (docs/extending.md §2).
    pub fn add_spec(&mut self, origin: &str, toml_text: &str) -> Result<(), RegistryError> {
        let invalid = |message: String| RegistryError::InvalidSpec {
            format: origin.to_owned(),
            message,
        };
        let spec: FormatSpec = toml::from_str(toml_text).map_err(|e| invalid(e.to_string()))?;
        if spec.id != origin {
            return Err(invalid(format!(
                "the id `{}` is not the file name `{origin}`",
                spec.id
            )));
        }
        let unknown = spec
            .recognizers
            .iter()
            .filter_map(|t| t.get("kind").and_then(|k| k.as_str()))
            .find(|kind| !self.factories.contains_key(kind));
        if let Some(kind) = unknown {
            return Err(RegistryError::UnknownKind {
                format: origin.to_owned(),
                kind: kind.to_owned(),
            });
        }
        let format =
            SpecFormat::compile(spec, |kind| self.factories.get(kind).cloned()).map_err(invalid)?;
        self.add_format(Arc::new(format))
    }

    /// The factory for `kind`.
    pub fn factory(&self, kind: &str) -> Option<&Arc<dyn RecognizerFactory>> {
        self.factories.get(kind)
    }

    /// All formats, in registration order.
    pub fn formats(&self) -> &[Arc<dyn Format>] {
        &self.formats
    }

    /// The format for `doc`: the one named by `forced` (`unknown-format` when none has that
    /// id), else the single highest detection score at or above [`Registry::min_score`]
    /// (`no-format` when none reaches it, `ambiguous-format` on a tie). Never a guess.
    pub fn select(
        &self,
        doc: &Document,
        forced: Option<&str>,
    ) -> Result<Arc<dyn Format>, Diagnostic> {
        if let Some(id) = forced {
            return self
                .formats
                .iter()
                .find(|f| &*f.id().0 == id)
                .cloned()
                .ok_or_else(|| {
                    Diagnostic::error("unknown-format", None, format!("no format has id `{id}`"))
                });
        }
        let mut scored: Vec<(u8, &Arc<dyn Format>)> = self
            .formats
            .iter()
            .map(|f| (f.detect(doc), f))
            .filter(|(score, _)| *score >= self.min_score)
            .collect();
        scored.sort_by_key(|(score, _)| std::cmp::Reverse(*score));
        match scored.as_slice() {
            [] => Err(Diagnostic::error(
                "no-format",
                None,
                format!(
                    "no format recognizes this plan (no detection score reaches {}); name one \
                     with --format",
                    self.min_score
                ),
            )),
            [(a, first), (b, second), ..] if a == b => Err(Diagnostic::error(
                "ambiguous-format",
                None,
                format!(
                    "formats `{}` and `{}` both score {a}; name one with --format",
                    first.id().0,
                    second.id().0
                ),
            )),
            [(_, first), ..] => Ok(Arc::clone(first)),
        }
    }
}

```rust
use super::*;
use crate::source;

fn spec(id: &str, detect: &str) -> String {
    format!(
        r#"
id = "{id}"
name = "test"
[detect]
patterns = [{{ regex = '{detect}', weight = 60 }}]
[outline]
task = {{ level = 3, regex = '^(?P<id>T(?P<ord>\d+)): (?P<title>.+)$' }}
"#
    )
}

fn doc(text: &str) -> Document {
    let source = source::load("t.md", text.as_bytes()).unwrap_or_else(|e| panic!("{e:?}"));
    Document::build(source).unwrap_or_else(|e| panic!("{e:?}"))
}

fn registry(specs: &[(&str, &str)]) -> Registry {
    let mut registry = Registry::new();
    for (id, detect) in specs {
        registry
            .add_spec(id, &spec(id, detect))
            .unwrap_or_else(|e| panic!("{e}"));
    }
    registry
}

fn selected(registry: &Registry, text: &str, forced: Option<&str>) -> Result<String, &'static str> {
    registry
        .select(&doc(text), forced)
        .map(|f| f.id().0.to_string())
        .map_err(|d| d.code)
}

#[test]
fn the_highest_score_wins_and_ties_are_refused() {
    let r = registry(&[("a", "^### T"), ("b", "^Plan B$")]);
    assert_eq!(selected(&r, "### T1: x\n", None), Ok("a".to_owned()));
    assert_eq!(selected(&r, "Notes.\n", None), Err("no-format"));
    assert_eq!(
        selected(&r, "### T1: x\n\nPlan B\n", None),
        Err("ambiguous-format")
    );
    assert_eq!(selected(&r, "Notes.\n", Some("b")), Ok("b".to_owned()));
    assert_eq!(selected(&r, "Notes.\n", Some("c")), Err("unknown-format"));
}

#[test]
fn detection_skips_fenced_blocks() {
    let r = registry(&[("a", "^### T")]);
    assert_eq!(
        selected(&r, "```markdown\n### T1: x\n```\n", None),
        Err("no-format")
    );
}

#[test]
fn a_spec_is_validated_when_it_loads() {
    let mut r = Registry::new();
    let refused = |r: &mut Registry, origin: &str, text: &str| r.add_spec(origin, text).err();
    assert!(matches!(
        refused(&mut r, "x", &spec("y", "a")),
        Some(RegistryError::InvalidSpec { .. })
    ));
    assert!(matches!(
        refused(&mut r, "x", &spec("x", "(")),
        Some(RegistryError::InvalidSpec { .. })
    ));
    let no_ord = spec("x", "a").replace("(?P<ord>", "(");
    let message = refused(&mut r, "x", &no_ord).map(|e| e.to_string());
    assert_eq!(
        message.as_deref(),
        Some("format `x`: outline.task: the regex has no `ord` capture")
    );
    let unknown_kind = format!("{}\n[[recognizer]]\nkind = \"nope\"\n", spec("x", "a"));
    assert_eq!(
        refused(&mut r, "x", &unknown_kind),
        Some(RegistryError::UnknownKind {
            format: "x".to_owned(),
            kind: "nope".to_owned()
        })
    );
    let unknown_key = format!("{}\nextra = 1\n", spec("x", "a"));
    assert!(refused(&mut r, "x", &unknown_key).is_some());
    assert_eq!(refused(&mut r, "x", &spec("x", "a")), None);
    assert_eq!(
        refused(&mut r, "x", &spec("x", "a")),
        Some(RegistryError::DuplicateFormat("x".to_owned()))
    );
}
