File `crates/planrun-parse/src/spec.rs` (full contents):

```rust
//! Format specs: a plan format described in TOML (docs/extending.md §2), and the generic
//! [`Format`](crate::format::Format) built from one.
//!
//! Built-in formats are specs too (`formats/*.toml`, embedded with `include_str!`), so the path a
//! user format takes is the path every built-in format takes (decision D8).

use std::sync::Arc;

use planrun_model::{ChangeKind, FormatId};
use regex::Regex;
use serde::Deserialize;

use crate::diagnostic::Diagnostics;
use crate::document::Document;
use crate::format::{Format, FormatSettings, Outline};
use crate::outline::{self, HeadingRule, ManifestRules, OutlineRules, StepBlock, StepRule};
use crate::recognizer::{Recognizer, RecognizerFactory};

/// A format spec as read from TOML. The schema is documented in docs/extending.md §2; keep the
/// two in step.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Deserialize)]
#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]
pub struct FormatSpec {
    /// Unique id.
    pub id: String,
    /// Human name.
    pub name: String,
    /// Detection patterns.
    pub detect: DetectSpec,
    /// Structure.
    pub outline: OutlineSpec,
    /// Shells and manual markers.
    #[serde(default)]
    pub settings: SettingsSpec,
    /// Recognizer instances, in order; each table has `kind` plus the kind's parameters.
    #[serde(default, rename = "recognizer")]
    pub recognizers: Vec<toml::Table>,
}

/// Detection: each matching pattern adds its weight; the score is capped at 100.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Deserialize)]
#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]
pub struct DetectSpec {
    /// Weighted patterns.
    pub patterns: Vec<WeightedPattern>,
}

/// A pattern and its weight.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Deserialize)]
#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]
pub struct WeightedPattern {
    /// Regex over each line of the plan outside fenced blocks.
    pub regex: String,
    /// Score added when it matches anywhere.
    pub weight: u8,
}

/// Where tasks, manifests and steps are.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Deserialize)]
#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]
pub struct OutlineSpec {
    /// Tasks are searched only after a heading matching this (`^Tasks$`), when set.
    pub tasks_after: Option<HeadingPattern>,
    /// A task heading; captures `id`, `ord` (number), `title`, and optionally `phase` (the
    /// plan's phase, taken from the first task).
    pub task: HeadingPattern,
    /// A heading at or above this level after a task ends the tasks.
    pub tasks_end_level: Option<u8>,
    /// The manifest of a task.
    pub manifest: Option<ManifestSpec>,
    /// Step boundaries; a task without one is a single implicit step.
    #[serde(default)]
    pub steps: Vec<StepPattern>,
}

/// A heading of a given level whose text matches.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Deserialize)]
#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]
pub struct HeadingPattern {
    /// Heading level.
    pub level: u8,
    /// Regex over the heading text.
    pub regex: String,
}

/// The manifest: a marker block followed by list items.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Deserialize)]
#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]
pub struct ManifestSpec {
    /// Regex over the marker paragraph (`^\*\*Files:?\*\*$`).
    pub marker: String,
    /// Regex over each list item; captures `change` and `paths` (split by `path_list` when set).
    pub item: String,
    /// Maps the captured `change` word to Create / Modify / Rewrite / Delete / Test.
    pub changes: std::collections::BTreeMap<String, String>,
    /// When set, the `paths` capture is split on this regex into several paths.
    pub path_list: Option<String>,
}

/// A block that starts a step.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Deserialize)]
#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]
pub struct StepPattern {
    /// `paragraph`, `list-item` or `heading`.
    pub block: String,
    /// Regex over the block text; may capture `n` and `title`.
    pub regex: String,
}

/// Shells and manual markers.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Default, Deserialize)]
#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]
pub struct SettingsSpec {
    /// Paragraph regexes that make text `Manual(Marked)`.
    #[serde(default)]
    pub manual_markers: Vec<String>,
    /// Fence info strings run by pwsh; default `powershell`, `pwsh`, `ps1`.
    pub pwsh: Option<Vec<String>>,
    /// Fence info strings run by bash; default `bash`, `sh`, `shell`.
    pub bash: Option<Vec<String>>,
}

/// Compiles `pattern`, refusing it when it lacks one of the `required` captures. `what` names it
/// in the message (`outline.task`, `lead`).
pub fn compile_regex(what: &str, pattern: &str, required: &[&str]) -> Result<Regex, String> {
    let regex = Regex::new(pattern).map_err(|e| format!("{what}: {e}"))?;
    let names: Vec<&str> = regex.capture_names().flatten().collect();
    if let Some(missing) = required.iter().find(|r| !names.contains(r)) {
        return Err(format!("{what}: the regex has no `{missing}` capture"));
    }
    Ok(regex)
}

/// The generic format: a compiled [`FormatSpec`].
pub struct SpecFormat {
    id: FormatId,
    name: String,
    detect: Vec<(Regex, u8)>,
    outline: OutlineRules,
    settings: FormatSettings,
    recognizers: Vec<Arc<dyn Recognizer>>,
}

impl SpecFormat {
    /// Compiles `spec`: every regex, every parameter, every recognizer through `factory` (the
    /// registry's lookup by kind). Errors name the offending key.
    pub fn compile(
        spec: FormatSpec,
        factory: impl Fn(&str) -> Option<Arc<dyn RecognizerFactory>>,
    ) -> Result<Self, String> {
        let detect = spec
            .detect
            .patterns
            .iter()
            .map(|p| Ok((compile_regex("detect", &p.regex, &[])?, p.weight)))
            .collect::<Result<_, String>>()?;
        let outline = compile_outline(&spec.outline)?;
        let settings = compile_settings(&spec.settings)?;
        let recognizers = spec
            .recognizers
            .iter()
            .map(|table| build_recognizer(table, &factory))
            .collect::<Result<_, String>>()?;
        Ok(Self {
            id: FormatId(spec.id.into()),
            name: spec.name,
            detect,
            outline,
            settings,
            recognizers,
        })
    }

    /// The human name.
    pub fn name(&self) -> &str {
        &self.name
    }
}

impl Format for SpecFormat {
    fn id(&self) -> &FormatId {
        &self.id
    }

    fn detect(&self, doc: &Document) -> u8 {
        let lines = doc.lines_outside_fences();
        let score: u32 = self
            .detect
            .iter()
            .filter(|(regex, _)| lines.iter().any(|line| regex.is_match(line)))
            .map(|(_, weight)| u32::from(*weight))
            .sum();
        u8::try_from(score.min(100)).unwrap_or(100)
    }

    fn outline(&self, doc: &Document) -> Result<Outline, Diagnostics> {
        outline::outline(&self.outline, &self.settings, doc)
    }

    fn recognizers(&self) -> &[Arc<dyn Recognizer>] {
        &self.recognizers
    }

    fn settings(&self) -> &FormatSettings {
        &self.settings
    }
}

fn compile_outline(spec: &OutlineSpec) -> Result<OutlineRules, String> {
    let tasks_after = spec
        .tasks_after
        .as_ref()
        .map(|h| heading_rule("outline.tasks_after", h, &[]))
        .transpose()?;
    let task = heading_rule("outline.task", &spec.task, &["id", "ord", "title"])?;
    let manifest = spec.manifest.as_ref().map(compile_manifest).transpose()?;
    let steps = spec
        .steps
        .iter()
        .map(|s| {
            Ok(StepRule {
                block: step_block(&s.block)?,
                regex: compile_regex("outline.steps", &s.regex, &[])?,
            })
        })
        .collect::<Result<_, String>>()?;
    Ok(OutlineRules {
        tasks_after,
        task,
        tasks_end_level: spec.tasks_end_level,
        manifest,
        steps,
    })
}

fn heading_rule(what: &str, h: &HeadingPattern, required: &[&str]) -> Result<HeadingRule, String> {
    if !(1..=6).contains(&h.level) {
        return Err(format!("{what}: level {} is not 1–6", h.level));
    }
    Ok(HeadingRule {
        level: h.level,
        regex: compile_regex(what, &h.regex, required)?,
    })
}

fn compile_manifest(spec: &ManifestSpec) -> Result<ManifestRules, String> {
    let changes = spec
        .changes
        .iter()
        .map(|(word, kind)| Ok((word.clone(), change_kind(kind)?)))
        .collect::<Result<_, String>>()?;
    Ok(ManifestRules {
        marker: compile_regex("outline.manifest.marker", &spec.marker, &[])?,
        item: compile_regex("outline.manifest.item", &spec.item, &["change", "paths"])?,
        changes,
        path_list: spec
            .path_list
            .as_deref()
            .map(|p| compile_regex("outline.manifest.path_list", p, &[]))
            .transpose()?,
    })
}

fn change_kind(name: &str) -> Result<ChangeKind, String> {
    match name {
        "create" => Ok(ChangeKind::Create),
        "modify" => Ok(ChangeKind::Modify),
        "rewrite" => Ok(ChangeKind::Rewrite),
        "delete" => Ok(ChangeKind::Delete),
        "test" => Ok(ChangeKind::Test),
        other => Err(format!(
            "outline.manifest.changes: `{other}` is not create, modify, rewrite, delete or test"
        )),
    }
}

fn step_block(name: &str) -> Result<StepBlock, String> {
    match name {
        "paragraph" => Ok(StepBlock::Paragraph),
        "list-item" => Ok(StepBlock::ListItem),
        "heading" => Ok(StepBlock::Heading),
        other => Err(format!(
            "outline.steps: block `{other}` is not paragraph, list-item or heading"
        )),
    }
}

fn compile_settings(spec: &SettingsSpec) -> Result<FormatSettings, String> {
    let strings = |list: &[&str]| list.iter().map(|s| (*s).to_owned()).collect();
    Ok(FormatSettings {
        manual_markers: spec
            .manual_markers
            .iter()
            .map(|m| compile_regex("settings.manual_markers", m, &[]))
            .collect::<Result<_, String>>()?,
        pwsh_languages: spec
            .pwsh
            .clone()
            .unwrap_or_else(|| strings(&["powershell", "pwsh", "ps1"])),
        bash_languages: spec
            .bash
            .clone()
            .unwrap_or_else(|| strings(&["bash", "sh", "shell"])),
    })
}

fn build_recognizer(
    table: &toml::Table,
    factory: &impl Fn(&str) -> Option<Arc<dyn RecognizerFactory>>,
) -> Result<Arc<dyn Recognizer>, String> {
    let Some(kind) = table.get("kind").and_then(|k| k.as_str()) else {
        return Err("a [[recognizer]] without a string `kind`".to_owned());
    };
    let Some(factory) = factory(kind) else {
        return Err(format!("unknown recognizer kind `{kind}`"));
    };
    let mut params = table.clone();
    params.remove("kind");
    factory
        .build(&params)
        .map_err(|e| format!("recognizer `{kind}`: {e}"))
}
```

**Step 2 — verify**

Targeted: the outline, registry and document tests:

```powershell
cargo test -p planrun-parse --lib
```

Expected (exit code 0; summary lines):

```text
test result: ok. 24 passed; 0 failed; 0 ignored; 0 measured; 0 filtered out; finished in …s
```

Gate:

```powershell
cargo fmt --check; if ($LASTEXITCODE -eq 0) { cargo clippy --all-targets -- -D warnings }; if ($LASTEXITCODE -eq 0) { cargo test }; $gate = $LASTEXITCODE; "gate exit code: $gate"
```

Expected (exit code 0; summary lines):

```text
test result: ok. 0 passed; 0 failed; 0 ignored; 0 measured; 0 filtered out; finished in …s
test result: ok. 0 passed; 0 failed; 0 ignored; 0 measured; 0 filtered out; finished in …s
test result: ok. 5 passed; 0 failed; 0 ignored; 0 measured; 0 filtered out; finished in …s
test result: ok. 24 passed; 0 failed; 0 ignored; 0 measured; 0 filtered out; finished in …s
test result: ok. 1 passed; 0 failed; 0 ignored; 0 measured; 0 filtered out; finished in …s
test result: ok. 0 passed; 0 failed; 0 ignored; 0 measured; 0 filtered out; finished in …s
test result: ok. 0 passed; 0 failed; 0 ignored; 0 measured; 0 filtered out; finished in …s
test result: ok. 0 passed; 0 failed; 0 ignored; 0 measured; 0 filtered out; finished in …s
gate exit code: 0
```

**Step 3 — commit**

Only the task's paths are staged, only when the gate's exit code above was 0, and only when every staged file is the verified one (its blob id):

```powershell
if ($gate -eq 0) {

### P1-T4: recognition and the assay kinds

**Goal:** The cursor (it never sees the manifest's blocks), the seven kinds the assay spec uses (`file-block`, `ceremony`, `delete`, `generated`, `script`, `expected-block`, `expected-prose`), patch headers (`patch-path`, `patch-shape`), the recognition loop with the fallback and the coverage report, `parse_plan` returning `Parsed`, and `with_builtins` loading `formats/assay.toml`.

**Files**

- Modify `CLAUDE.md`
- Modify `crates/planrun-parse/src/diagnostic.rs`
- Create `crates/planrun-parse/src/kinds/ceremony.rs`
- Create `crates/planrun-parse/src/kinds/delete.rs`
- Create `crates/planrun-parse/src/kinds/expected.rs`
- Create `crates/planrun-parse/src/kinds/file_block.rs`
- Create `crates/planrun-parse/src/kinds/generated.rs`
- Create `crates/planrun-parse/src/kinds/mod.rs`
- Create `crates/planrun-parse/src/kinds/params.rs`
- Create `crates/planrun-parse/src/kinds/script.rs`
- Create `crates/planrun-parse/src/kinds/tests.rs`
- Modify `crates/planrun-parse/src/lib.rs`
- Create `crates/planrun-parse/src/patch.rs`
- Create `crates/planrun-parse/src/recognize.rs`
- Modify `crates/planrun-parse/src/recognizer.rs`
- Modify `crates/planrun-parse/src/registry.rs`
- Create `crates/planrun-parse/tests/synthetic.rs`

A `Malformed` is an error and recognition goes on at the next block, so one parse reports every broken idiom. A recognizer that answers `Yes` without consuming a block is an internal error (`recognizer-stalled`), never a loop. `CLAUDE.md` changes only its priorities heading, to `## Priorities (D1, D25)` (review ruling 10): byte for byte `47cdf72` otherwise, so its layout still lists the P0 modules.

**Step 1 — write the files**

File `CLAUDE.md` (full contents):

````markdown
# planrun

A Rust CLI that executes structured Markdown implementation plans against a git repository, task by
task, step by step: exactly what the plan says, nothing more. It is not an agent. Read
`docs/architecture.md` before changing code; `docs/decisions.md` holds the rules below with their
reasons (cited as `D<n>`).

## Priorities (D1, D25)

Correctness > maintainability/extensibility > execution speed > low memory > predictability.
When two of these conflict, the earlier one wins. Speed means no wasted process starts or repeated
reads; don't optimize I/O speculatively (cargo/npm dominate wall time).

## Layout

```
crates/planrun-model   the IR: Plan, Task, Step, Action, Span/TextRef, RepoPath, AbbrevBlobId
crates/planrun-parse   Markdown → IR: source, document, format, recognizer, registry, spec, check
crates/planrun-exec    IR → effects: workspace, git, shells, expect, policy, journal, runner
crates/planrun-cli     the `planrun` binary
formats/*.toml         built-in plan formats (embedded); the file name is the format id
docs/                  architecture, plan-grammar, extending, decisions, roadmap, plans/
tests/corpus/          synthetic/ (one plan per format), invalid/ (one error each), local/ (private)
tests/fixtures/greeter the repo the synthetic assay plan applies to
```

## Hard rules

1. **planrun-parse never executes and never touches the repository. planrun-exec never depends on
   planrun-parse** (D2). Don't add a dependency that crosses these lines.
2. **No partial plans** (D3). Any error means no `Plan`, nothing runs.
3. **Nothing is dropped.** Every block of a task becomes an action, `Note` or `Manual` (D21). Once
   a recognizer's distinctive lead matched, a broken idiom is a `Malformed` error, never a
   fallback.
4. **The action set is closed** (D4). New plan wording maps onto existing actions. Adding an
   `ActionKind` follows docs/extending.md §4, all in one change, and only with Rens' agreement.
5. **New plan styles are data first** (D8): a TOML spec, then a recognizer kind, then a hand-written
   `Format`, in that order of preference (docs/extending.md).
6. **Paths in actions are `RepoPath`** (D7). Nothing outside the repository is written except
   `.git/planrun/` and the OS temp directory. The working tree gets only what the plan writes.
7. **Patches go through `git apply`** with exact context (D20). No fuzz, no `similar`-based
   patching.
8. **Prose never passes a script on its own** (D11); expected failures are skipped by default (D12).
9. **No new dependency without a line in docs/decisions.md D9.**
10. **Real plans stay in `tests/corpus/local/`** (git-ignored, D16). Never copy their content into
    committed files, tests, snapshots, docs or commit messages. Synthetic plans only.

## Commands

```powershell
cargo fmt --check
cargo clippy --all-targets -- -D warnings
cargo test
cargo test -- --ignored local_corpus      # parses the private real plans, when present
cargo run -p planrun-cli -- check tests/corpus/synthetic/assay-style.md
```

The gate for every task is all three of fmt, clippy (warnings are errors) and test.

## Code conventions

- Library code: no `unwrap`/`expect` (clippy warns; the gate fails). Tests may use
  `unwrap_or_else(|e| panic!("{e}"))`.
- Every public item has a doc comment (`missing_docs` warns).
- Diagnostics have a stable kebab-case `code`; tests pin codes, snapshots pin messages.
- Text from the plan is a `TextRef`, never a copied `String`, except `TextRef::Owned` where the
  source isn't contiguous.
- Prefer enums over strings, traits for extension points, composition over deep generic layering.
- Keep functions flat: guard clauses and early returns, at most three levels of nesting.
- Recognizers are pure; parameters are validated when a spec loads, not when a plan is parsed.

## Tests

- Unit tests sit next to the code (`mod tests`), or in `<module>_tests.rs` once a file passes
  ~400 lines.
- `insta` snapshots for IR and CLI output. A changed snapshot is a review item: explain it in the
  commit, never re-pin by reflex.
- Adding a check rule adds a file to `tests/corpus/invalid/`. Adding a format adds a plan to
  `tests/corpus/synthetic/` with its snapshot.
- Execution tests run on a temporary copy of `tests/fixtures/greeter` (`git init` in the copy),
  never on this repository.

## Docs stay in step

- A changed decision: a new `D<n>` in docs/decisions.md that supersedes the old one.
- A changed spec schema: `spec.rs`, docs/extending.md §2 and `formats/*.toml` together (the
  `spec_files` test checks the files).
- A new recognizer kind: docs/extending.md §3.
- A new format: docs/plan-grammar.md.

## Working with Rens

- Workflow: brainstorm → design spec → implementation plan (`docs/plans/`, assay format, see
  `docs/plans/README.md`) → execution. Propose before making non-trivial edits; stop after writing
  a plan.
- Direct, technically precise; no flattery. Put review text and anything meant to be pasted in
  code blocks.
- Rens works on Windows with PowerShell 7. Commands in plans are PowerShell 7.
- One commit per task, message `P<N>-T<n>: <title>`. Don't push.
````

```rust
//! Parse and check diagnostics: a stable code, a severity, a span, a message.

use planrun_model::Span;

/// How bad a diagnostic is. Any `Error` stops the plan from being returned.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, PartialOrd, Ord)]
pub enum Severity {
    /// The plan cannot run.
    Error,
    /// The plan can run; something looks wrong (e.g. low coverage).
    Warning,
    /// Information (coverage summary lines).
    Note,
}

/// One finding. `code` is stable and kebab-case (`duplicate-task-id`); the invalid corpus pins
/// codes, never messages (tests/corpus/invalid/README.md).
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct Diagnostic {
    /// Severity.
    pub severity: Severity,
    /// Stable code.
    pub code: &'static str,
    /// Where, when known.
    pub span: Option<Span>,
    /// What, for a human.
    pub message: String,
    /// Further spans with labels ("first defined here").
    pub related: Vec<(Span, String)>,
}

impl Diagnostic {
    /// An error.
    pub fn error(code: &'static str, span: Option<Span>, message: impl Into<String>) -> Self {
        Self {
            severity: Severity::Error,
            code,
            span,
            message: message.into(),
            related: Vec::new(),
        }
    }

    /// A warning.
    pub fn warning(code: &'static str, span: Option<Span>, message: impl Into<String>) -> Self {
        Self {
            severity: Severity::Warning,
            ..Self::error(code, span, message)
        }
    }

    /// A note (information, never blocks).
    pub fn note(code: &'static str, span: Option<Span>, message: impl Into<String>) -> Self {
        Self {
            severity: Severity::Note,
            ..Self::error(code, span, message)
        }
    }

    /// Adds a related span.
    pub fn with_related(mut self, span: Span, label: impl Into<String>) -> Self {
        self.related.push((span, label.into()));
        self
    }
}

/// All diagnostics of one parse, in source order.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Default, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct Diagnostics(pub Vec<Diagnostic>);

impl Diagnostics {
    /// One diagnostic.
    pub fn single(diagnostic: Diagnostic) -> Self {
        Self(vec![diagnostic])
    }

    /// True when any diagnostic is an error.
    pub fn has_errors(&self) -> bool {
        self.0.iter().any(|d| d.severity == Severity::Error)
    }

    /// The codes of the errors, in order.
    pub fn error_codes(&self) -> Vec<&'static str> {
        self.0
            .iter()
            .filter(|d| d.severity == Severity::Error)
            .map(|d| d.code)
            .collect()
    }

    /// Sorts into source order (diagnostics without a span first); stable.
    pub fn sort(&mut self) {
        self.0.sort_by_key(|d| d.span.map(|s| s.start));
    }
}
```

File `crates/planrun-parse/src/kinds/ceremony.rs` (full contents):

```rust
//! `ceremony`: the fence directly after a patch that saves and applies it by hand. planrun
//! applies the patch itself, so the fence is absorbed: consumed, never run (decision D17).

use std::sync::Arc;

use planrun_model::{ActionKind, RecognizerId};
use regex::Regex;

use super::params::Params;
use crate::document::Block;
use crate::recognizer::{Cursor, Recognized, Recognizer, RecognizerFactory, StepContext};

/// Builds `ceremony` recognizers.
pub struct CeremonyFactory;

impl RecognizerFactory for CeremonyFactory {
    fn kind(&self) -> &'static str {
        "ceremony"
    }

    fn build(&self, table: &toml::Table) -> Result<Arc<dyn Recognizer>, String> {
        let mut p = Params::new(table);
        let id = p.id()?;
        let after = p.string("after")?;
        if after != "patch" {
            return Err(format!("`after = \"{after}\"`: only `patch` is supported"));
        }
        let body = p.regex("body", &[])?;
        p.finish()?;
        Ok(Arc::new(Ceremony { id, body }))
    }
}

struct Ceremony {
    id: RecognizerId,
    body: Regex,
}

impl Recognizer for Ceremony {
    fn id(&self) -> &str {
        &self.id.0
    }

    fn recognize(&self, cx: &StepContext<'_>, cursor: &mut Cursor<'_>) -> Recognized {
        let after_patch = cx
            .previous
            .last()
            .is_some_and(|a| matches!(a.kind, ActionKind::ApplyPatch { .. }));
        if !after_patch {
            return Recognized::No;
        }
        let Some(Block::Fence { body, .. }) = cursor.peek(0).map(|b| &b.block) else {
            return Recognized::No;
        };
        if !self.body.is_match(cx.doc.text(body)) {
            return Recognized::No;
        }
        cursor.advance(1);
        Recognized::Yes(Vec::new())
    }
}
```

File `crates/planrun-parse/src/kinds/delete.rs` (full contents):

```rust
//! `delete`: a sentence naming a file to delete → `Delete`.

use std::sync::Arc;

use planrun_model::{Action, ActionKind, RecognizerId};
use regex::Regex;

use super::params::Params;
use super::{lead_path, paragraph};
use crate::recognizer::{Cursor, Recognized, Recognizer, RecognizerFactory, StepContext};

/// Builds `delete` recognizers.
pub struct DeleteFactory;

impl RecognizerFactory for DeleteFactory {
    fn kind(&self) -> &'static str {
        "delete"
    }

    fn build(&self, table: &toml::Table) -> Result<Arc<dyn Recognizer>, String> {
        let mut p = Params::new(table);
        let id = p.id()?;
        let lead = p.regex("lead", &["path"])?;
        let via_git = p.opt_bool("via_git")?.unwrap_or(false);
        p.finish()?;
        Ok(Arc::new(Delete { id, lead, via_git }))
    }
}

struct Delete {
    id: RecognizerId,
    lead: Regex,
    via_git: bool,
}

impl Recognizer for Delete {
    fn id(&self) -> &str {
        &self.id.0
    }

    fn recognize(&self, cx: &StepContext<'_>, cursor: &mut Cursor<'_>) -> Recognized {
        let Some(lead) = cursor.peek(0) else {
            return Recognized::No;
        };
        let Some((text, base)) = paragraph(cx, lead) else {
            return Recognized::No;
        };
        let Some(caps) = self.lead.captures(text) else {
            return Recognized::No;
        };
        let path = match lead_path(&caps, base, lead.span) {
            Ok(path) => path,
            Err(d) => return Recognized::Malformed(d),
        };
        cursor.advance(1);
        Recognized::Yes(vec![Action {
            kind: ActionKind::Delete {
                path,
                via_git: self.via_git,
            },
            span: lead.span,
            origin: self.id.clone(),
        }])
    }
}
```

File `crates/planrun-parse/src/kinds/expected.rs` (full contents):

```rust
//! `expected-block` (`Expected (…):` + a `text` fence of summary lines) and `expected-prose`
//! (`Expected: …`): both amend the script directly before them.

use std::sync::Arc;

use planrun_model::{Action, ActionKind, ExitExpect, Expectation, RecognizerId, Span, TextRef};
use regex::Regex;

use super::params::{ExitRow, Params, exit_rows, first_exit};
use super::{amendable, capture_ref, paragraph};
use crate::diagnostic::Diagnostic;
use crate::document::Block;
use crate::recognizer::{Cursor, Recognized, Recognizer, RecognizerFactory, StepContext};

/// Builds `expected-block` recognizers.
pub struct ExpectedBlockFactory;

/// Builds `expected-prose` recognizers.
pub struct ExpectedProseFactory;

impl RecognizerFactory for ExpectedBlockFactory {
    fn kind(&self) -> &'static str {
        "expected-block"
    }

    fn build(&self, table: &toml::Table) -> Result<Arc<dyn Recognizer>, String> {
        let mut p = Params::new(table);
        let id = p.id()?;
        let lead = p.regex("lead", &["spec"])?;
        let exit = exit_rows(&mut p, "spec")?;
        let lines = p.opt_string("lines")?;
        if lines.is_some_and(|l| l != "summary") {
            return Err("`lines` must be \"summary\"".to_owned());
        }
        p.finish()?;
        Ok(Arc::new(ExpectedBlock { id, lead, exit }))
    }
}

impl RecognizerFactory for ExpectedProseFactory {
    fn kind(&self) -> &'static str {
        "expected-prose"
    }

    fn build(&self, table: &toml::Table) -> Result<Arc<dyn Recognizer>, String> {
        let mut p = Params::new(table);
        let id = p.id()?;
        let lead = p.regex("lead", &["text"])?;
        let exit = exit_rows(&mut p, "text")?;
        p.finish()?;
        Ok(Arc::new(ExpectedProse { id, lead, exit }))
    }
}

struct ExpectedBlock {
    id: RecognizerId,
    lead: Regex,
    exit: Vec<ExitRow>,
}

struct ExpectedProse {
    id: RecognizerId,
    lead: Regex,
    exit: Vec<ExitRow>,
}

impl Recognizer for ExpectedBlock {
    fn id(&self) -> &str {
        &self.id.0
    }

    fn recognize(&self, cx: &StepContext<'_>, cursor: &mut Cursor<'_>) -> Recognized {
        let Some(lead) = cursor.peek(0) else {
            return Recognized::No;
        };
        let Some((text, _)) = paragraph(cx, lead) else {
            return Recognized::No;
        };
        let Some(caps) = self.lead.captures(text) else {
            return Recognized::No;
        };
        let script = match amendable(cx, lead.span) {
            Ok(script) => script,
            Err(d) => return Recognized::Malformed(d),
        };
        let spec = caps.name("spec").map_or("", |m| m.as_str());
        let Some(exit) = first_exit(&self.exit, spec) else {
            return Recognized::Malformed(Diagnostic::error(
                "expected-spec",
                Some(lead.span),
                format!("`{spec}` names no exit status this format knows"),
            ));
        };
        let fence = cursor.peek(1).filter(|b| {
            matches!(b.block, Block::Fence { .. }) && b.block.fence_language(cx.doc) == Some("text")
        });
        let Some(fence) = fence else {
            return Recognized::Malformed(Diagnostic::error(
                "missing-block",
                Some(lead.span),
                "this expectation announces a ```text block of summary lines, and none follows it",
            ));
        };
        let Block::Fence { body, .. } = &fence.block else {
            return Recognized::No;
        };
        let expect = Expectation {
            exit,
            lines: Some(body.clone()),
            prose: None,
        };
        cursor.advance(2);
        Recognized::Amend(amended(script, expect, lead.span.join(fence.span)))
    }
}

impl Recognizer for ExpectedProse {
    fn id(&self) -> &str {
        &self.id.0
    }

    fn recognize(&self, cx: &StepContext<'_>, cursor: &mut Cursor<'_>) -> Recognized {
        let Some(lead) = cursor.peek(0) else {
            return Recognized::No;
        };
        let Some((text, base)) = paragraph(cx, lead) else {
            return Recognized::No;
        };
        let Some(caps) = self.lead.captures(text) else {
            return Recognized::No;
        };
        let script = match amendable(cx, lead.span) {
            Ok(script) => script,
            Err(d) => return Recognized::Malformed(d),
        };
        let said = caps.name("text").map_or("", |m| m.as_str());
        let expect = Expectation {
            exit: first_exit(&self.exit, said).unwrap_or(ExitExpect::Unknown),
            lines: None,
            prose: capture_ref(&caps, "text", base).or(Some(TextRef::Span(lead.span))),
        };
        cursor.advance(1);
        Recognized::Amend(amended(script, expect, lead.span))
    }
}

/// `script` with `expect` attached, its span grown over the expectation.
fn amended(mut script: Action, expect: Expectation, over: Span) -> Action {
    if let ActionKind::RunScript(s) | ActionKind::Commit(s) = &mut script.kind {
        s.expect = Some(expect);
    }
    script.span = script.span.join(over);
    script
}
```

File `crates/planrun-parse/src/kinds/file_block.rs` (full contents):

```rust
//! `file-block`: a lead paragraph naming a path and an operation, then one fence →
//! `WriteFile` or `ApplyPatch`.

use std::sync::Arc;

use planrun_model::{Action, ActionKind, ChangeKind, Manifest, RecognizerId, RepoPath, WriteMode};
use regex::Regex;

use super::params::Params;
use super::{lead_path, paragraph};
use crate::diagnostic::Diagnostic;
use crate::document::Block;
use crate::patch;
use crate::recognizer::{Cursor, Recognized, Recognizer, RecognizerFactory, StepContext};

/// Builds `file-block` recognizers.
pub struct FileBlockFactory;

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
enum Op {
    Write,
    Create,
    Overwrite,
    Patch,
}

impl RecognizerFactory for FileBlockFactory {
    fn kind(&self) -> &'static str {
        "file-block"
    }

    fn build(&self, table: &toml::Table) -> Result<Arc<dyn Recognizer>, String> {
        let mut p = Params::new(table);
        let id = p.id()?;
        let lead = p.regex("lead", &["path"])?;
        let ops = p
            .table("ops")?
            .iter()
            .map(|(prefix, op)| {
                let op = op.as_str().ok_or("`ops` values must be strings")?;
                Ok((prefix.clone(), op_named(op)?))
            })
            .collect::<Result<Vec<_>, String>>()?;
        let default_op = p.opt_string("default_op")?.map(op_named).transpose()?;
        p.finish()?;
        let has_op = lead.capture_names().flatten().any(|n| n == "op");
        if !has_op && default_op.is_none() {
            return Err("`lead` has no `op` capture and there is no `default_op`".to_owned());
        }
        Ok(Arc::new(FileBlock {
            id,
            lead,
            ops,
            default_op,
        }))
    }
}

fn op_named(name: &str) -> Result<Op, String> {
    match name {
        "write" => Ok(Op::Write),
        "create" => Ok(Op::Create),
        "overwrite" => Ok(Op::Overwrite),
        "patch" => Ok(Op::Patch),
        other => Err(format!(
            "op `{other}` is not write, create, overwrite or patch"
        )),
    }
}

struct FileBlock {
    id: RecognizerId,
    lead: Regex,
    ops: Vec<(String, Op)>,
    default_op: Option<Op>,
}

impl FileBlock {
    /// The op of the longest prefix of `written`.
    fn op(&self, written: &str) -> Option<Op> {
        self.ops
            .iter()
            .filter(|(prefix, _)| written.starts_with(prefix.as_str()))
            .max_by_key(|(prefix, _)| prefix.len())
            .map(|(_, op)| *op)
    }

    fn known_ops(&self) -> String {
        let names: Vec<String> = self.ops.iter().map(|(p, _)| format!("`{p}`")).collect();
        names.join(", ")
    }
}

impl Recognizer for FileBlock {
    fn id(&self) -> &str {
        &self.id.0
    }

    fn recognize(&self, cx: &StepContext<'_>, cursor: &mut Cursor<'_>) -> Recognized {
        let Some(lead) = cursor.peek(0) else {
            return Recognized::No;
        };
        let Some((text, base)) = paragraph(cx, lead) else {
            return Recognized::No;
        };
        let Some(caps) = self.lead.captures(text) else {
            return Recognized::No;
        };
        let written = caps.name("op").map(|m| m.as_str());
        let op = written.map_or(self.default_op, |w| self.op(w));
        let Some(op) = op else {
            return Recognized::Malformed(Diagnostic::error(
                "unknown-file-op",
                Some(lead.span),
                format!(
                    "`{}` is not a file operation this format knows ({})",
                    written.unwrap_or(""),
                    self.known_ops()
                ),
            ));
        };
        let fence = cursor
            .peek(1)
            .filter(|b| matches!(b.block, Block::Fence { .. }));
        let Some(fence) = fence else {
            return Recognized::Malformed(Diagnostic::error(
                "missing-block",
                Some(lead.span),
                "this sentence announces a fenced block, and none follows it",
            ));
        };
        let path = match lead_path(&caps, base, lead.span) {
            Ok(path) => path,
            Err(d) => return Recognized::Malformed(d),
        };
        let Block::Fence { body, .. } = &fence.block else {
            return Recognized::No;
        };
        let kind = match op {
            Op::Patch => match patch_action(cx, path, body, lead.span, fence.span) {
                Ok(kind) => kind,
                Err(d) => return Recognized::Malformed(d),
            },
            Op::Write => write(path.clone(), body, mode_from(cx.manifest, &path)),
            Op::Create => write(path, body, WriteMode::Create),
            Op::Overwrite => write(path, body, WriteMode::Overwrite),
        };
        cursor.advance(2);
        Recognized::Yes(vec![Action {
            kind,
            span: lead.span.join(fence.span),
            origin: self.id.clone(),
        }])
    }
}

fn write(path: RepoPath, body: &planrun_model::TextRef, mode: WriteMode) -> ActionKind {
    ActionKind::WriteFile {
        path,
        body: body.clone(),
        mode,
    }
}

/// Create or Overwrite from the manifest; `FromManifest` when it does not say (the `manifest`
/// check reports it).
fn mode_from(manifest: &Manifest, path: &RepoPath) -> WriteMode {
    let change = manifest
        .entries
        .iter()
        .find(|e| &e.path == path)
        .map(|e| e.change);
    match change {
        Some(ChangeKind::Create) => WriteMode::Create,
        Some(ChangeKind::Modify | ChangeKind::Rewrite) => WriteMode::Overwrite,
        _ => WriteMode::FromManifest,
    }
}

fn patch_action(
    cx: &StepContext<'_>,
    path: RepoPath,
    body: &planrun_model::TextRef,
    lead: planrun_model::Span,
    fence: planrun_model::Span,
) -> Result<ActionKind, Diagnostic> {
    let header = patch::header(cx.doc.text(body))
        .map_err(|e| Diagnostic::error("patch-shape", Some(fence), e))?;
    if let Some(other) = header.paths.iter().find(|p| p.as_str() != path.as_str()) {
        return Err(Diagnostic::error(
            "patch-path",
            Some(fence),
            format!("the patch changes `{other}`; the sentence before it names `{path}`"),
        )
        .with_related(lead, "the path named here"));
    }
    Ok(ActionKind::ApplyPatch {
        path,
        patch: body.clone(),
        pre: header.pre,
        post: header.post,
    })
}

//! `generated`: a sentence saying a command produces a file, not the plan → `Generated`.

use std::sync::Arc;

use planrun_model::{Action, ActionKind, RecognizerId};
use regex::Regex;

use super::params::Params;
use super::{lead_path, paragraph};
use crate::recognizer::{Cursor, Recognized, Recognizer, RecognizerFactory, StepContext};

/// Builds `generated` recognizers.
pub struct GeneratedFactory;

impl RecognizerFactory for GeneratedFactory {
    fn kind(&self) -> &'static str {
        "generated"
    }

    fn build(&self, table: &toml::Table) -> Result<Arc<dyn Recognizer>, String> {
        let mut p = Params::new(table);
        let id = p.id()?;
        let lead = p.regex("lead", &["path"])?;
        p.finish()?;
        Ok(Arc::new(Generated { id, lead }))
    }
}

struct Generated {
    id: RecognizerId,
    lead: Regex,
}

impl Recognizer for Generated {
    fn id(&self) -> &str {
        &self.id.0
    }

    fn recognize(&self, cx: &StepContext<'_>, cursor: &mut Cursor<'_>) -> Recognized {
        let Some(lead) = cursor.peek(0) else {
            return Recognized::No;
        };
        let Some((text, base)) = paragraph(cx, lead) else {
            return Recognized::No;
        };
        let Some(caps) = self.lead.captures(text) else {
            return Recognized::No;
        };
        let path = match lead_path(&caps, base, lead.span) {
            Ok(path) => path,
            Err(d) => return Recognized::Malformed(d),
        };
        cursor.advance(1);
        Recognized::Yes(vec![Action {
            kind: ActionKind::Generated { path },
            span: lead.span,
            origin: self.id.clone(),
        }])
    }
}

```rust
//! The built-in recognizer kinds (docs/extending.md §3). Phase 1: the kinds the `assay` spec
//! uses.

mod ceremony;
mod delete;
mod expected;
mod file_block;
mod generated;
mod params;
mod script;

use std::sync::Arc;

use planrun_model::{Action, ActionKind, RepoPath, Span, TextRef};
use regex::Captures;

use crate::diagnostic::Diagnostic;
use crate::document::{Block, Located};
use crate::recognizer::{RecognizerFactory, StepContext};

pub use ceremony::CeremonyFactory;
pub use delete::DeleteFactory;
pub use expected::{ExpectedBlockFactory, ExpectedProseFactory};
pub use file_block::FileBlockFactory;
pub use generated::GeneratedFactory;
pub use script::ScriptFactory;

/// Every built-in factory, for [`crate::Registry::with_builtins`].
pub fn builtin_factories() -> Vec<Arc<dyn RecognizerFactory>> {
    vec![
        Arc::new(FileBlockFactory),
        Arc::new(CeremonyFactory),
        Arc::new(DeleteFactory),
        Arc::new(GeneratedFactory),
        Arc::new(ScriptFactory),
        Arc::new(ExpectedBlockFactory),
        Arc::new(ExpectedProseFactory),
    ]
}

/// The text of a paragraph block, if it is one.
fn paragraph<'a>(cx: &'a StepContext<'_>, block: &'a Located) -> Option<(&'a str, u32)> {
    let Block::Paragraph { text } = &block.block else {
        return None;
    };
    Some((cx.doc.text(text), text.span().start))
}

/// The span of capture `name` in text that starts at `base`.
fn capture_span(caps: &Captures<'_>, name: &str, base: u32) -> Option<Span> {
    let m = caps.name(name)?;
    Some(Span::new(base + offset(m.start()), base + offset(m.end())))
}

/// The capture `name` as a span reference.
fn capture_ref(caps: &Captures<'_>, name: &str, base: u32) -> Option<TextRef> {
    capture_span(caps, name, base).map(TextRef::Span)
}

/// The `path` capture as a repository path, or a `bad-path` error at the capture.
fn lead_path(caps: &Captures<'_>, base: u32, lead: Span) -> Result<RepoPath, Diagnostic> {
    let span = capture_span(caps, "path", base).unwrap_or(lead);
    let raw = caps.name("path").map_or("", |m| m.as_str());
    RepoPath::new(raw).map_err(|e| Diagnostic::error("bad-path", Some(span), e.to_string()))
}

/// The script of the action an expectation amends: the last action, when it is a script.
fn amendable(cx: &StepContext<'_>, lead: Span) -> Result<Action, Diagnostic> {
    let script = cx
        .previous
        .last()
        .filter(|a| matches!(a.kind, ActionKind::RunScript(_) | ActionKind::Commit(_)));
    let Some(action) = script else {
        return Err(Diagnostic::error(
            "expected-without-script",
            Some(lead),
            "an expectation with no script directly before it",
        ));
    };
    let already = match &action.kind {
        ActionKind::RunScript(s) | ActionKind::Commit(s) => s.expect.is_some(),
        _ => false,
    };
    if already {
        return Err(Diagnostic::error(
            "expected-twice",
            Some(lead),
            "the script before this already has an expectation",
        )
        .with_related(action.span, "the script"));
    }
    Ok(action.clone())
}

fn offset(value: usize) -> u32 {
    u32::try_from(value).unwrap_or(u32::MAX)
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests;
```

```rust
//! Reading a `[[recognizer]]` table: every key used exactly as the kind defines it, unknown keys
//! refused, every regex compiled with its required captures (when the spec loads).

use planrun_model::{ExitExpect, RecognizerId};
use regex::Regex;

use crate::spec::compile_regex;

/// A recognizer's parameters, with the keys read so far.
pub(super) struct Params<'a> {
    table: &'a toml::Table,
    read: Vec<&'static str>,
}

impl<'a> Params<'a> {
    pub(super) fn new(table: &'a toml::Table) -> Self {
        Self {
            table,
            read: Vec::new(),
        }
    }

    fn get(&mut self, key: &'static str) -> Option<&'a toml::Value> {
        self.read.push(key);
        self.table.get(key)
    }

    /// The required `id`.
    pub(super) fn id(&mut self) -> Result<RecognizerId, String> {
        Ok(RecognizerId(self.string("id")?.into()))
    }

    pub(super) fn string(&mut self, key: &'static str) -> Result<&'a str, String> {
        self.opt_string(key)?
            .ok_or_else(|| format!("`{key}` is required"))
    }

    pub(super) fn opt_string(&mut self, key: &'static str) -> Result<Option<&'a str>, String> {
        match self.get(key) {
            None => Ok(None),
            Some(v) => v
                .as_str()
                .map(Some)
                .ok_or_else(|| format!("`{key}` must be a string")),
        }
    }

    pub(super) fn regex(&mut self, key: &'static str, required: &[&str]) -> Result<Regex, String> {
        let pattern = self.string(key)?;
        compile_regex(key, pattern, required)
    }

    pub(super) fn opt_bool(&mut self, key: &'static str) -> Result<Option<bool>, String> {
        match self.get(key) {
            None => Ok(None),
            Some(v) => v
                .as_bool()
                .map(Some)
                .ok_or_else(|| format!("`{key}` must be true or false")),
        }
    }

    pub(super) fn table(&mut self, key: &'static str) -> Result<&'a toml::Table, String> {
        self.get(key)
            .ok_or_else(|| format!("`{key}` is required"))?
            .as_table()
            .ok_or_else(|| format!("`{key}` must be a table"))
    }

    /// An array of tables, each with exactly the keys `keys`.
    pub(super) fn rows(
        &mut self,
        key: &'static str,
        keys: &[&str],
    ) -> Result<Vec<&'a toml::Table>, String> {
        let Some(value) = self.get(key) else {
            return Ok(Vec::new());
        };
        let rows = value
            .as_array()
            .ok_or_else(|| format!("`{key}` must be an array of tables"))?;
        rows.iter()
            .map(|row| {
                let row = row
                    .as_table()
                    .ok_or_else(|| format!("`{key}` must be an array of tables"))?;
                if let Some(extra) = row.keys().find(|k| !keys.contains(&k.as_str())) {
                    return Err(format!("`{key}`: unknown key `{extra}`"));
                }
                Ok(row)
            })
            .collect()
    }

    /// Refuses any key the kind did not read.
    pub(super) fn finish(self) -> Result<(), String> {
        let unknown = self.table.keys().find(|k| !self.read.contains(&k.as_str()));
        match unknown {
            Some(key) => Err(format!("unknown parameter `{key}`")),
            None => Ok(()),
        }
    }
}

/// A string field of a row.
pub(super) fn row_string<'a>(row: &'a toml::Table, key: &str) -> Result<&'a str, String> {
    row.get(key)
        .and_then(|v| v.as_str())
        .ok_or_else(|| format!("a row without a string `{key}`"))
}

/// One row of an exit table: a regex and what its match means.
pub(super) struct ExitRow {
    pub(super) regex: Regex,
    pub(super) exit: ExitValue,
}

/// What an exit row maps to.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy)]
pub(super) enum ExitValue {
    /// A fixed expectation.
    Fixed(ExitExpect),
    /// The number the row's `code` capture holds (0 is `Success`).
    Captured,
}

/// The `exit` rows of an expectation kind, matched on `field` (`spec` or `text`).
pub(super) fn exit_rows(
    params: &mut Params<'_>,
    field: &'static str,
) -> Result<Vec<ExitRow>, String> {
    params
        .rows("exit", &[field, "exit"])?
        .into_iter()
        .map(|row| {
            let exit = exit_value(row_string(row, "exit")?)?;
            let required: &[&str] = match exit {
                ExitValue::Captured => &["code"],
                ExitValue::Fixed(_) => &[],
            };
            let regex = compile_regex(field, row_string(row, field)?, required)?;
            Ok(ExitRow { regex, exit })
        })
        .collect()
}

fn exit_value(value: &str) -> Result<ExitValue, String> {
    match value {
        "success" => Ok(ExitValue::Fixed(ExitExpect::Success)),
        "failure" => Ok(ExitValue::Fixed(ExitExpect::Failure)),
        "any" => Ok(ExitValue::Fixed(ExitExpect::Unknown)),
        "code" => Ok(ExitValue::Captured),
        other => other
            .strip_prefix("code:")
            .and_then(|n| n.parse().ok())
            .map(|n| ExitValue::Fixed(code(n)))
            .ok_or_else(|| format!("exit `{other}` is not success, failure, any, code or code:N")),
    }
}

/// `n` as an expectation: 0 is `Success`.
pub(super) fn code(n: i32) -> ExitExpect {
    if n == 0 {
        ExitExpect::Success
    } else {
        ExitExpect::Code(n)
    }
}

/// The first row that matches `text`, as an expectation.
pub(super) fn first_exit(rows: &[ExitRow], text: &str) -> Option<ExitExpect> {
    rows.iter().find_map(|row| {
        let caps = row.regex.captures(text)?;
        match row.exit {
            ExitValue::Fixed(exit) => Some(exit),
            ExitValue::Captured => caps
                .name("code")
                .and_then(|m| m.as_str().parse().ok())
                .map(code),
        }
    })
}
```

```rust
//! `script`: a fence in a shell language → `RunScript`, or `Commit` when its body says so.

use std::sync::Arc;

use planrun_model::{Action, ActionKind, RecognizerId, Script, ScriptIntent};
use regex::Regex;

use super::params::{Params, row_string};
use crate::document::Block;
use crate::recognizer::{Cursor, Recognized, Recognizer, RecognizerFactory, StepContext};
use crate::spec::compile_regex;

/// Builds `script` recognizers.
pub struct ScriptFactory;

/// What an `intents` row selects.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy)]
enum Intent {
    /// The `Commit` action kind (its script carries `ScriptIntent::Other`).
    Commit,
    /// A `RunScript` with this intent.
    Run(ScriptIntent),
}

impl RecognizerFactory for ScriptFactory {
    fn kind(&self) -> &'static str {
        "script"
    }

    fn build(&self, table: &toml::Table) -> Result<Arc<dyn Recognizer>, String> {
        let mut p = Params::new(table);
        let id = p.id()?;
        let intents = p
            .rows("intents", &["intent", "body"])?
            .into_iter()
            .map(|row| {
                let intent = intent_named(row_string(row, "intent")?)?;
                Ok((
                    compile_regex("intents.body", row_string(row, "body")?, &[])?,
                    intent,
                ))
            })
            .collect::<Result<_, String>>()?;
        p.finish()?;
        Ok(Arc::new(ScriptRecognizer { id, intents }))
    }
}

fn intent_named(name: &str) -> Result<Intent, String> {
    match name {
        "commit" => Ok(Intent::Commit),
        "gate" => Ok(Intent::Run(ScriptIntent::Gate)),
        "preview" => Ok(Intent::Run(ScriptIntent::Preview)),
        "apply" => Ok(Intent::Run(ScriptIntent::Apply)),
        "verify" => Ok(Intent::Run(ScriptIntent::Verify)),
        "other" => Ok(Intent::Run(ScriptIntent::Other)),
        other => Err(format!(
            "intent `{other}` is not commit, gate, preview, apply, verify or other"
        )),
    }
}

struct ScriptRecognizer {
    id: RecognizerId,
    intents: Vec<(Regex, Intent)>,
}

impl Recognizer for ScriptRecognizer {
    fn id(&self) -> &str {
        &self.id.0
    }

    fn recognize(&self, cx: &StepContext<'_>, cursor: &mut Cursor<'_>) -> Recognized {
        let Some(located) = cursor.peek(0) else {
            return Recognized::No;
        };
        let Block::Fence { body, .. } = &located.block else {
            return Recognized::No;
        };
        let language = located.block.fence_language(cx.doc).unwrap_or("");
        let Some(shell) = cx.settings.shell_for(language) else {
            return Recognized::No;
        };
        let text = cx.doc.text(body);
        let intent = self
            .intents
            .iter()
            .find(|(regex, _)| regex.is_match(text))
            .map_or(Intent::Run(ScriptIntent::Verify), |(_, intent)| *intent);
        let script = |intent| Script {
            shell,
            body: body.clone(),
            expect: None,
            intent,
        };
        let kind = match intent {
            Intent::Commit => ActionKind::Commit(script(ScriptIntent::Other)),
            Intent::Run(intent) => ActionKind::RunScript(script(intent)),
        };
        cursor.advance(1);
        Recognized::Yes(vec![Action {
            kind,
            span: located.span,
            origin: self.id.clone(),
        }])
    }
}

````rust
//! The built-in kinds through the assay spec: outline and recognition only, no check rules.

use planrun_model::{
    Action, ActionKind, ExitExpect, ManualReason, Script, ScriptIntent, Shell, WriteMode,
};

use crate::document::Document;
use crate::recognize::{TaskCoverage, recognize};
use crate::{Registry, source};

struct Recognized {
    doc: Document,
    actions: Vec<Action>,
    coverage: TaskCoverage,
}

/// One task with the manifest `files` and one step holding `step`.
fn plan(files: &str, step: &str) -> String {
    format!("## Tasks\n\n### P1-T1: t\n\n**Files**\n\n{files}\n\n**Step 1 — s**\n\n{step}")
}

fn run(text: &str) -> Result<Recognized, Vec<&'static str>> {
    let registry = Registry::with_builtins().unwrap_or_else(|e| panic!("{e}"));
    let source = source::load("t.md", text.as_bytes()).unwrap_or_else(|e| panic!("{e:?}"));
    let doc = Document::build(source).unwrap_or_else(|e| panic!("{e:?}"));
    let format = registry
        .select(&doc, Some("assay"))
        .unwrap_or_else(|e| panic!("{e:?}"));
    let outline = format.outline(&doc).unwrap_or_else(|e| panic!("{e:?}"));
    let (tasks, coverage) =
        recognize(format.as_ref(), &doc, &outline).map_err(|d| d.error_codes())?;
    let actions = tasks
        .into_iter()
        .flat_map(|t| t.steps)
        .flat_map(|s| s.actions)
        .collect();
    let coverage = coverage
        .into_iter()
        .next()
        .unwrap_or_else(|| panic!("no task"));
    Ok(Recognized {
        doc,
        actions,
        coverage,
    })
}

fn ok(text: &str) -> Recognized {
    run(text).unwrap_or_else(|codes| panic!("{codes:?}"))
}

fn errors(text: &str) -> Vec<&'static str> {
    run(text).err().unwrap_or_default()
}

fn script(action: &Action) -> &Script {
    match &action.kind {
        ActionKind::RunScript(s) | ActionKind::Commit(s) => s,
        other => panic!("not a script: {other:?}"),
    }
}

const PATCH: &str = "```diff
diff --git a/src/a.rs b/src/a.rs
index 1111111..2222222 100644
--- a/src/a.rs
+++ b/src/a.rs
@@ -1 +1 @@
-a
+b
```";

const CEREMONY: &str = "```powershell
git apply --whitespace=nowarn \"$env:TEMP\\p.patch\"
Remove-Item \"$env:TEMP\\p.patch\"
```";

#[test]
fn full_contents_take_the_mode_from_the_manifest() {
    let step = "File `a.rs` (full contents):\n\n```rust\nA\n```\n\n\
                File `b.rs` (full contents):\n\n```rust\nB\n```\n\n\
                File `c.rs` (full contents):\n\n```rust\nC\n```\n";
    let r = ok(&plan("- Create `a.rs`\n- Modify `b.rs`", step));
    let modes: Vec<(String, WriteMode, String)> = r
        .actions
        .iter()
        .map(|a| match &a.kind {
            ActionKind::WriteFile { path, body, mode } => {
                (path.to_string(), *mode, r.doc.text(body).to_owned())
            }
            other => panic!("{other:?}"),
        })
        .collect();
    assert_eq!(
        modes,
        [
            ("a.rs".to_owned(), WriteMode::Create, "A\n".to_owned()),
            ("b.rs".to_owned(), WriteMode::Overwrite, "B\n".to_owned()),
            ("c.rs".to_owned(), WriteMode::FromManifest, "C\n".to_owned()),
        ]
    );
    assert_eq!(r.coverage.actions, 6);
    assert_eq!(r.coverage.manifest, 3);
}

#[test]
fn a_patch_absorbs_its_ceremony() {
    let lead = "File `src/a.rs` (exact diff; save the block as `$env:TEMP\\p (1).patch`, outside \
                the repository, then apply it):";
    let r = ok(&plan(
        "- Modify `src/a.rs`",
        &format!("{lead}\n\n{PATCH}\n\n{CEREMONY}\n"),
    ));
    assert_eq!(r.actions.len(), 1);
    let ActionKind::ApplyPatch {
        path, pre, post, ..
    } = &r.actions[0].kind
    else {
        panic!("{:?}", r.actions[0].kind);
    };
    assert_eq!(path.as_str(), "src/a.rs");
    assert_eq!(pre.map(|i| i.to_string()).as_deref(), Some("1111111"));
    assert_eq!(post.map(|i| i.to_string()).as_deref(), Some("2222222"));
    assert_eq!(r.coverage.absorbed.len(), 1);
    assert_eq!(
        &*r.coverage.absorbed[0].recognizer.0,
        "ceremony#git-apply-temp"
    );
}

#[test]
fn a_ceremony_not_after_a_patch_is_a_script() {
    let r = ok(&plan("- Modify `a.rs`", &format!("{CEREMONY}\n")));
    assert!(matches!(r.actions[0].kind, ActionKind::RunScript(_)));
    assert!(r.coverage.absorbed.is_empty());
}

#[test]
fn broken_file_blocks_are_errors() {
    let files = "- Modify `src/a.rs`";
    let lead = "File `src/a.rs` (exact diff; save it):";
    assert_eq!(
        errors(&plan(
            files,
            "File `src/a.rs` (partial contents):\n\n```rust\nx\n```\n"
        )),
        ["unknown-file-op"]
    );
    assert_eq!(
        errors(&plan(
            files,
            "File `src/a.rs` (full contents):\n\nForgotten.\n"
        )),
        ["missing-block"]
    );
    let other = PATCH.replace("src/a.rs", "src/b.rs");
    assert_eq!(
        errors(&plan(files, &format!("{lead}\n\n{other}\n"))),
        ["patch-path"]
    );
    assert_eq!(
        errors(&plan(
            files,
            &format!("{lead}\n\n```diff\nnot a patch\n```\n")
        )),
        ["patch-shape"]
    );
    assert_eq!(
        errors(&plan(
            files,
            "File `../a.rs` (full contents):\n\n```rust\nx\n```\n"
        )),
        ["bad-path"]
    );
}

#[test]
fn deletes_and_generated_files() {
    let step = "Delete `src/old.rs`.\n\n\
                `docs/a.txt` is generated by step 2's first command, never written by hand.\n\n\
                `Cargo.lock` changes: it is regenerated by cargo in the next step. Do not edit it \
                by hand; commit it.\n\n\
                Restore `web/lock.json` from the verified scratch commit `abc` (never written by \
                hand; step 2 checks it):\n\n```powershell\ngit restore --source=abc -- web/lock.json\n```\n";
    let r = ok(&plan("- Delete `src/old.rs`", step));
    let kinds: Vec<String> = r
        .actions
        .iter()
        .map(|a| match &a.kind {
            ActionKind::Delete { path, via_git } => format!("delete {path} {via_git}"),
            ActionKind::Generated { path } => format!("generated {path}"),
            ActionKind::RunScript(s) => format!("script {:?}", s.intent),
            other => format!("{other:?}"),
        })
        .collect();
    assert_eq!(
        kinds,
        [
            "delete src/old.rs false",
            "generated docs/a.txt",
            "generated Cargo.lock",
            "generated web/lock.json",
            "script Verify",
        ]
    );
}

#[test]
fn script_intents() {
    let step = "```powershell\ncargo test; $gate = $LASTEXITCODE\n```\n\n\
                ```bash\ncargo build\n```\n\n\
                ```powershell\nif ($gate -eq 0) {\n  git add -- a\n  if ($LASTEXITCODE -eq 0) { git commit -m \"x\" }\n}\n```\n";
    let r = ok(&plan("- Modify `a`", step));
    let shapes: Vec<(Shell, ScriptIntent, bool)> = r
        .actions
        .iter()
        .map(|a| {
            let commit = matches!(a.kind, ActionKind::Commit(_));
            (script(a).shell, script(a).intent, commit)
        })
        .collect();
    assert_eq!(
        shapes,
        [
            (Shell::Pwsh, ScriptIntent::Gate, false),
            (Shell::Bash, ScriptIntent::Verify, false),
            (Shell::Pwsh, ScriptIntent::Other, true),
        ]
    );
}

#[test]
fn expected_blocks_amend_their_script() {
    let step = "```powershell\ncargo test\n```\n\n\
                Expected (exit code 0; summary lines):\n\n```text\nok\n```\n\n\
                ```powershell\nrg x\n```\n\n\
                Expected (exit code 1; summary lines):\n\n```text\n```\n";
    let r = ok(&plan("- Modify `a`", step));
    let expects: Vec<(ExitExpect, Option<String>)> = r
        .actions
        .iter()
        .map(|a| {
            let e = script(a)
                .expect
                .clone()
                .unwrap_or_else(|| panic!("no expectation"));
            (e.exit, e.lines.map(|l| r.doc.text(&l).to_owned()))
        })
        .collect();
    assert_eq!(
        expects,
        [
            (ExitExpect::Success, Some("ok\n".to_owned())),
            (ExitExpect::Code(1), Some(String::new())),
        ]
    );
    assert_eq!(r.coverage.actions, 6);
}

#[test]
fn broken_expectations_are_errors() {
    let files = "- Modify `a`";
    let script = "```powershell\ncargo test\n```\n\n";
    let block = "Expected (exit code 0; summary lines):\n\n```text\nok\n```\n";
    assert_eq!(
        errors(&plan(
            files,
            &format!("{script}Expected (summary lines):\n\n```text\nok\n```\n")
        )),
        ["expected-spec"]
    );
    assert_eq!(
        errors(&plan(
            files,
            &format!("{script}Expected (exit code 0):\n\n```powershell\nx\n```\n")
        )),
        ["missing-block"]
    );
    assert_eq!(errors(&plan(files, block)), ["expected-without-script"]);
    assert_eq!(
        errors(&plan(
            files,
            &format!("{script}{block}\nExpected: all pass.\n")
        )),
        ["expected-twice"]
    );
}

#[test]
fn prose_expectations_are_first_match() {
    let cases = [
        (
            "Expected: the commit, then no output from `git status --porcelain`.",
            ExitExpect::Unknown,
        ),
        ("Expected: all tests pass.", ExitExpect::Success),
        ("Expected: a build error.", ExitExpect::Failure),
        ("Expected: something else.", ExitExpect::Unknown),
    ];
    for (sentence, exit) in cases {
        let r = ok(&plan(
            "- Modify `a`",
            &format!("```powershell\nx\n```\n\n{sentence}\n"),
        ));
        let e = script(&r.actions[0])
            .expect
            .clone()
            .unwrap_or_else(|| panic!("{sentence}"));
        assert_eq!(e.exit, exit, "{sentence}");
        let prose = e.prose.map(|p| r.doc.text(&p).to_owned());
        assert_eq!(prose.as_deref(), sentence.strip_prefix("Expected: "));
    }
}

#[test]
fn the_fallback_accounts_for_every_other_block() {
    let step = "Plain prose.\n\nRun it by hand.\n\n```rust\nfn x() {}\n```\n\n```text\nout\n```\n\n\
                ```\nbare\n```\n\n<!-- a comment -->\n\n| a |\n|---|\n| 1 |\n";
    let r = ok(&plan("- Modify `a`", step));
    let kinds: Vec<String> = r
        .actions
        .iter()
        .map(|a| match &a.kind {
            ActionKind::Note { .. } => "note".to_owned(),
            ActionKind::Manual { reason, .. } => format!("manual {reason:?}"),
            other => format!("{other:?}"),
        })
        .collect();
    assert_eq!(
        kinds,
        [
            "note",
            "manual Marked",
            "manual ForeignLanguage(\"rust\")",
            "note",
            "manual Unrecognized",
            "note",
        ]
    );
    assert_eq!(r.coverage.dropped, 1);
    assert_eq!((r.coverage.notes, r.coverage.manual), (3, 3));
    assert!(matches!(
        &r.actions[1].kind,
        ActionKind::Manual {
            reason: ManualReason::Marked,
            ..
        }
    ));
}

#[test]
fn kind_parameters_are_validated_when_the_spec_loads() {
    let base = std::fs::read_to_string(concat!(
        env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"),
        "/../../formats/assay.toml"
    ))
    .unwrap_or_else(|e| panic!("{e}"));
    let base = base.replace("id = \"assay\"", "id = \"x\"");
    let refused = |extra: &str| {
        let mut registry = Registry::with_builtins().unwrap_or_else(|e| panic!("{e}"));
        registry
            .add_spec("x", &format!("{base}\n[[recognizer]]\n{extra}\n"))
            .err()
            .map(|e| e.to_string())
    };
    assert_eq!(
        refused("kind = \"delete\"\nid = \"d\"\nlead = 'x'\nextra = 1"),
        Some("format `x`: recognizer `delete`: lead: the regex has no `path` capture".to_owned())
    );
    assert_eq!(
        refused("kind = \"delete\"\nid = \"d\"\nlead = '(?P<path>x)'\nextra = 1"),
        Some("format `x`: recognizer `delete`: unknown parameter `extra`".to_owned())
    );
    assert!(refused("kind = \"file-block\"\nid = \"f\"\nlead = '(?P<path>x)(?P<op>y)'\nops = { y = \"move\" }").is_some());
    assert!(
        refused("kind = \"script\"\nid = \"s\"\nintents = [{ intent = \"deploy\", body = 'x' }]")
            .is_some()
    );
    assert!(refused("kind = \"expected-block\"\nid = \"e\"\nlead = '(?P<spec>x)'\nexit = [{ spec = 'x', exit = \"code\" }]").is_some());
    assert!(refused("kind = \"ceremony\"\nid = \"c\"\nafter = \"script\"\nbody = 'x'").is_some());
    assert_eq!(
        refused("kind = \"delete\"\nid = \"d\"\nlead = '(?P<path>x)'"),
        None
    );
}

File `crates/planrun-parse/src/lib.rs` (full contents):

```rust
//! Markdown plan → typed [`planrun_model::Plan`] (docs/architecture.md §4–§5).
//!
//! Pipeline: read and normalize ([`source`]) → block stream ([`document`]) → pick a format
//! ([`registry`]) → outline: tasks, manifests, steps ([`format`], [`outline`]) → recognizers turn
//! each step's blocks into actions ([`recognize`], [`recognizer`], the kinds in `kinds`) →
//! static checks ([`check`]).
//!
//! This crate never touches the repository and never runs anything (decision D2). Checks that
//! need the repository (preimage blob ids against the files on disk) live in planrun-exec.
//!
//! Extension points, in the order a new plan style should try them (docs/extending.md):
//! 1. a format spec in TOML that reuses the built-in recognizer kinds ([`spec`]);
//! 2. a new [`recognizer::RecognizerFactory`] for a new idiom;
//! 3. a hand-written [`format::Format`] when the outline is not heading-shaped.

pub mod check;
pub mod diagnostic;
pub mod document;
pub mod format;
mod kinds;
pub mod outline;
pub mod patch;
pub mod recognize;
pub mod recognizer;
pub mod registry;
pub mod source;
pub mod spec;

pub use diagnostic::{Diagnostic, Diagnostics, Severity};
pub use recognize::TaskCoverage;
pub use registry::Registry;

use planrun_model::Plan;

use crate::document::Document;
use crate::format::{FormatSettings, Outline};

/// A plan that parsed and checked without errors, with its warnings and notes and its coverage
/// report.
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct Parsed {
    /// The plan.
    pub plan: Plan,
    /// Warnings and notes, in source order.
    pub diagnostics: Diagnostics,
    /// Per task, where its blocks went.
    pub coverage: Vec<TaskCoverage>,
}

/// Parses and checks `bytes` as a plan named `name`, with the formats in `registry`.
///
/// `format` forces a format by id; otherwise the one with the highest detection score wins, and
/// a tie or a score below the registry's threshold is an error (never a guess).
///
/// A plan is returned only when there is no error (`Never execute a partially parsed plan`,
/// decision D3); otherwise every diagnostic comes back, in source order. Check rules run only
/// when the outline and recognition had no errors.
pub fn parse_plan(
    registry: &Registry,
    name: &str,
    bytes: &[u8],
    format: Option<&str>,
) -> Result<Parsed, Diagnostics> {
    let source = source::load(name, bytes).map_err(Diagnostics::single)?;
    let doc = Document::build(source)?;
    let format = registry.select(&doc, format).map_err(Diagnostics::single)?;
    let outline = format.outline(&doc)?;
    let (tasks, coverage) = recognize::recognize(format.as_ref(), &doc, &outline)?;
    let mut found = outside_notes(&doc, format.settings(), &outline);
    let plan = Plan {
        source: doc.source,
        format: format.id().clone(),
        meta: outline.meta,
        tasks,
    };
    for rule in check::default_rules() {
        rule.check(&plan, &mut found);
    }
    let mut diagnostics = Diagnostics(found);
    diagnostics.sort();
    if diagnostics.has_errors() {
        return Err(diagnostics);
    }
    Ok(Parsed {
        plan,
        diagnostics,
        coverage,
    })
}

/// Manual markers outside the tasks: before task 1 (read them first) and after the tasks (not
/// run).
fn outside_notes(doc: &Document, settings: &FormatSettings, outline: &Outline) -> Vec<Diagnostic> {
    let before = outline.meta.preamble.iter().map(|text| {
        Diagnostic::note(
            "manual-outside-tasks",
            Some(text.span()),
            "read this before task 1: it is an instruction for you",
        )
    });
    let after = outline
        .after_tasks
        .map(|range| outline::marked(doc, settings, range))
        .unwrap_or_default();
    let after = after.into_iter().map(|text| {
        Diagnostic::note(
            "manual-outside-tasks",
            Some(text.span()),
            "after the tasks: planrun does not run this",
        )
    });
    before.chain(after).collect()
}

```rust
//! Reading a git patch as text: its file header (paths, `index` line) and the shape of its
//! hunks. Nothing is applied here; `git apply` does that in planrun-exec (decision D20).

use planrun_model::AbbrevBlobId;

/// The file header of a one-file git patch.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct PatchHeader {
    /// Every path the header names (`diff --git a/… b/…`, `--- a/…`, `+++ b/…`), `/dev/null`
    /// left out.
    pub paths: Vec<String>,
    /// The preimage blob id of the `index` line.
    pub pre: Option<AbbrevBlobId>,
    /// The postimage blob id of the `index` line.
    pub post: Option<AbbrevBlobId>,
}

/// Reads the header of `patch`. Refused: no `diff --git` or `---`/`+++` header, more than one
/// file, an `index` line that does not parse.
pub fn header(patch: &str) -> Result<PatchHeader, String> {
    let mut header = PatchHeader {
        paths: Vec::new(),
        pre: None,
        post: None,
    };
    let mut files = 0;
    let mut markers = 0;
    for line in patch.lines() {
        if let Some(rest) = line.strip_prefix("diff --git ") {
            files += 1;
            if files > 1 {
                return Err("the patch changes more than one file".to_owned());
            }
            header.paths.extend(git_paths(rest)?);
            continue;
        }
        if line.starts_with("@@") {
            continue;
        }
        if let Some(ids) = line.strip_prefix("index ") {
            if markers == 0 {
                (header.pre, header.post) = index_ids(ids)?;
            }
            continue;
        }
        let side = line
            .strip_prefix("--- ")
            .map(|p| (p, "a/"))
            .or_else(|| line.strip_prefix("+++ ").map(|p| (p, "b/")));
        let Some((path, prefix)) = side.filter(|_| markers < 2) else {
            continue;
        };
        markers += 1;
        if path == "/dev/null" {
            continue;
        }
        let Some(path) = path.strip_prefix(prefix) else {
            return Err(format!("`{line}` does not name an `{prefix}` path"));
        };
        header.paths.push(path.to_owned());
    }
    if files == 0 && markers < 2 {
        return Err("not a git patch: no `diff --git` line and no `---`/`+++` pair".to_owned());
    }
    Ok(header)
}

fn git_paths(rest: &str) -> Result<[String; 2], String> {
    let parsed = rest
        .strip_prefix("a/")
        .and_then(|r| r.rsplit_once(" b/"))
        .map(|(a, b)| [a.to_owned(), b.to_owned()]);
    parsed.ok_or_else(|| format!("`diff --git {rest}` does not name `a/…` and `b/…` paths"))
}

fn index_ids(ids: &str) -> Result<(Option<AbbrevBlobId>, Option<AbbrevBlobId>), String> {
    let range = ids.split_whitespace().next().unwrap_or("");
    let Some((pre, post)) = range.split_once("..") else {
        return Err(format!("`index {ids}` is not `<pre>..<post>`"));
    };
    let parse = |id: &str| AbbrevBlobId::parse(id).map_err(|e| e.to_string());
    Ok((Some(parse(pre)?), Some(parse(post)?)))
}

/// Checks the hunks of `patch`: at least one, and each hunk's body has exactly the line counts
/// its `@@ -a,b +c,d @@` header gives. An empty line counts as context, as `git apply` reads it.
/// Returns the number of hunks.
pub fn check_hunks(patch: &str) -> Result<usize, String> {
    let mut lines = patch.lines().enumerate().peekable();
    while lines.next_if(|(_, l)| !l.starts_with("@@")).is_some() {}
    let mut hunks = 0;
    while let Some((n, line)) = lines.next() {
        if line.starts_with('\\') {
            continue;
        }
        let Some((mut old, mut new)) = hunk_counts(line) else {
            return Err(format!(
                "patch line {}: `{line}` is not a hunk header",
                n + 1
            ));
        };
        hunks += 1;
        while old > 0 || new > 0 {
            let Some((m, body)) = lines.next() else {
                return Err(format!(
                    "the hunk at patch line {} ends early: {old} old and {new} new lines missing",
                    n + 1
                ));
            };
            let (takes_old, takes_new) = match body.chars().next() {
                None | Some(' ') => (1, 1),
                Some('-') => (1, 0),
                Some('+') => (0, 1),
                Some('\\') => (0, 0),
                Some(_) => {
                    return Err(format!("patch line {}: `{body}` is not a hunk line", m + 1));
                }
            };
            if takes_old > old || takes_new > new {
                return Err(format!(
                    "the hunk at patch line {} has more lines than its header says",
                    n + 1
                ));
            }
            old -= takes_old;
            new -= takes_new;
        }
    }
    if hunks == 0 {
        return Err("the patch has no hunk".to_owned());
    }
    Ok(hunks)
}

/// `@@ -a[,b] +c[,d] @@…` → (b, d), a missing count being 1.
fn hunk_counts(line: &str) -> Option<(u32, u32)> {
    let rest = line.strip_prefix("@@ -")?;
    let (ranges, _) = rest.split_once(" @@")?;
    let (old, new) = ranges.split_once(" +")?;
    Some((count(old)?, count(new)?))
}

fn count(range: &str) -> Option<u32> {
    match range.split_once(',') {
        Some((start, len)) => {
            start.parse::<u32>().ok()?;
            len.parse().ok()
        }
        None => range.parse::<u32>().ok().map(|_| 1),
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    const PATCH: &str = "diff --git a/src/lib.rs b/src/lib.rs
index aa80cf1..97bd92a 100644
--- a/src/lib.rs
+++ b/src/lib.rs
@@ -1,3 +1,4 @@ fn x
 a

+b
-c
+d
";

    #[test]
    fn the_header_names_paths_and_blob_ids() {
        let h = header(PATCH).unwrap_or_else(|e| panic!("{e}"));
        assert_eq!(h.paths, ["src/lib.rs"; 4]);
        assert_eq!(h.pre.map(|i| i.to_string()).as_deref(), Some("aa80cf1"));
        assert_eq!(h.post.map(|i| i.to_string()).as_deref(), Some("97bd92a"));
        assert_eq!(check_hunks(PATCH), Ok(1));
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_new_file_names_dev_null() {
        let p = "diff --git a/n.rs b/n.rs\nnew file mode 100644\nindex 0000000..1111111\n\
                 --- /dev/null\n+++ b/n.rs\n@@ -0,0 +1 @@\n+x\n";
        let h = header(p).unwrap_or_else(|e| panic!("{e}"));
        assert_eq!(h.paths, ["n.rs", "n.rs", "n.rs"]);
        assert!(h.pre.is_some_and(|i| i.is_absent()));
        assert_eq!(check_hunks(p), Ok(1));
    }

    #[test]
    fn bad_headers_are_refused() {
        assert!(header("just text\n").is_err());
        assert!(header(&format!("{PATCH}diff --git a/b b/b\n")).is_err());
        assert!(header("diff --git a/x b/x\nindex zz..yy\n").is_err());
    }

    #[test]
    fn hunk_counts_must_match() {
        let short = "--- a/a\n+++ b/a\n@@ -1,2 +1 @@\n-a\n+b\n";
        assert!(check_hunks(short).is_err());
        let long = "--- a/a\n+++ b/a\n@@ -1 +1 @@\n-a\n+b\n+c\n";
        assert!(check_hunks(long).is_err());
        assert!(check_hunks("--- a/a\n+++ b/a\n").is_err());
        let two = "--- a/a\n+++ b/a\n@@ -1 +1 @@\n-a\n+b\n\\ No newline at end of file\n@@ -5 +5 @@\n-x\n+y\n";
        assert_eq!(check_hunks(two), Ok(2));
    }
}
