File `crates/planrun-parse/src/recognize.rs` (full contents):

```rust
//! Recognition (docs/architecture.md §4.4): for each step, a cursor walks its blocks and the
//! format's recognizers are tried in order; the first that answers anything but `No` wins. A
//! block no recognizer takes goes to the built-in fallback, so every block is accounted for
//! (decision D21).

use planrun_model::{
    Action, ActionKind, ManualReason, RecognizerId, Span, Step, Task, TaskId, TextRef,
};

use crate::diagnostic::{Diagnostic, Diagnostics};
use crate::document::{Block, Document};
use crate::format::{Format, Outline, OutlineStep, OutlineTask};
use crate::recognizer::{Cursor, Recognized, StepContext};

/// Where one task's blocks went (the coverage report, docs/architecture.md §4.5).
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct TaskCoverage {
    /// The task.
    pub task: TaskId,
    /// Blocks that became actions, expectations included.
    pub actions: usize,
    /// Blocks absorbed without an action (the patch ceremony), with where they are.
    pub absorbed: Vec<Absorbed>,
    /// The manifest's blocks.
    pub manifest: usize,
    /// Blocks that became `Note`.
    pub notes: usize,
    /// Blocks that became `Manual`.
    pub manual: usize,
    /// HTML comments, dropped.
    pub dropped: usize,
}

impl TaskCoverage {
    /// Every block of the task's steps and manifest.
    pub fn blocks(&self) -> usize {
        self.actions + self.absorbed.len() + self.manifest + self.notes + self.manual + self.dropped
    }
}

/// One absorbed block.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct Absorbed {
    /// The recognizer that absorbed it.
    pub recognizer: RecognizerId,
    /// The block.
    pub span: Span,
}

/// The recognizer id of the built-in fallback.
pub const FALLBACK: &str = "fallback";

/// The tasks of `outline` with their actions, and the coverage of each. Every `Malformed` is
/// collected; any means no tasks.
pub fn recognize(
    format: &dyn Format,
    doc: &Document,
    outline: &Outline,
) -> Result<(Vec<Task>, Vec<TaskCoverage>), Diagnostics> {
    let mut errors = Vec::new();
    let mut tasks = Vec::with_capacity(outline.tasks.len());
    let mut coverage = Vec::with_capacity(outline.tasks.len());
    for task in &outline.tasks {
        let mut cov = TaskCoverage {
            task: task.id.clone(),
            actions: 0,
            absorbed: Vec::new(),
            manifest: task.manifest_blocks.map_or(0, |m| m.len()),
            notes: 0,
            manual: 0,
            dropped: 0,
        };
        let steps = task
            .steps
            .iter()
            .map(|s| step(format, doc, task, s, &mut cov, &mut errors))
            .collect();
        tasks.push(Task {
            id: task.id.clone(),
            title: task.title.clone(),
            span: task.span,
            manifest: task.manifest.clone(),
            steps,
        });
        coverage.push(cov);
    }
    if !errors.is_empty() {
        return Err(Diagnostics(errors));
    }
    Ok((tasks, coverage))
}

fn step(
    format: &dyn Format,
    doc: &Document,
    task: &OutlineTask,
    outline: &OutlineStep,
    cov: &mut TaskCoverage,
    errors: &mut Vec<Diagnostic>,
) -> Step {
    let mut cursor = Cursor::with_skip(doc, outline.blocks, task.manifest_blocks);
    let mut actions: Vec<Action> = Vec::new();
    while let Some(at) = cursor.peek(0).map(|b| b.span) {
        let before = cursor.consumed();
        let cx = StepContext {
            doc,
            task: &task.id,
            manifest: &task.manifest,
            settings: format.settings(),
            previous: &actions,
        };
        let (origin, outcome) = first_match(format, &cx, &mut cursor);
        let consumed = cursor.consumed() - before;
        match outcome {
            Recognized::No => {
                if let Some(action) = fallback(&cx, &mut cursor, cov) {
                    actions.push(action);
                }
            }
            Recognized::Malformed(d) => {
                errors.push(d);
                cursor.advance(1);
            }
            _ if consumed == 0 => {
                errors.push(Diagnostic::error(
                    "recognizer-stalled",
                    Some(at),
                    format!("recognizer `{origin}` matched without consuming a block"),
                ));
                cursor.advance(1);
            }
            Recognized::Yes(new) if new.is_empty() => cov.absorbed.push(Absorbed {
                recognizer: RecognizerId(origin.into()),
                span: at,
            }),
            Recognized::Yes(new) => {
                cov.actions += consumed;
                actions.extend(new);
            }
            Recognized::Amend(action) => {
                cov.actions += consumed;
                actions.pop();
                actions.push(action);
            }
        }
    }
    Step {
        number: outline.number,
        title: outline.title.clone(),
        span: outline.span,
        actions,
    }
}

/// The first recognizer that answers anything but `No`, with its id.
fn first_match<'f>(
    format: &'f dyn Format,
    cx: &StepContext<'_>,
    cursor: &mut Cursor<'_>,
) -> (&'f str, Recognized) {
    for recognizer in format.recognizers() {
        let outcome = recognizer.recognize(cx, cursor);
        if !matches!(outcome, Recognized::No) {
            return (recognizer.id(), outcome);
        }
    }
    (FALLBACK, Recognized::No)
}

/// The built-in fallback: consumes one block. Prose becomes `Note` (or `Manual(Marked)`), a
/// fence `Manual`, a `text` fence `Note`; an HTML comment is dropped.
fn fallback(
    cx: &StepContext<'_>,
    cursor: &mut Cursor<'_>,
    cov: &mut TaskCoverage,
) -> Option<Action> {
    let located = cursor.peek(0)?;
    cursor.advance(1);
    let whole = TextRef::Span(located.span);
    let prose = |text: &TextRef| {
        if cx.settings.is_marked(cx.doc.text(text)) {
            ActionKind::Manual {
                reason: ManualReason::Marked,
                text: text.clone(),
            }
        } else {
            ActionKind::Note { text: text.clone() }
        }
    };
    let kind = match &located.block {
        Block::Paragraph { text }
        | Block::ListItem { text, .. }
        | Block::Quote { text }
        | Block::Heading { text, .. } => prose(text),
        Block::Table { text } => ActionKind::Note { text: text.clone() },
        Block::Rule => ActionKind::Note { text: whole },
        Block::Html { text } if cx.doc.text(text).trim_start().starts_with("<!--") => {
            cov.dropped += 1;
            return None;
        }
        Block::Html { text } => ActionKind::Note { text: text.clone() },
        Block::Fence { .. } => fence(cx, &located.block, whole),
    };
    match kind {
        ActionKind::Manual { .. } => cov.manual += 1,
        _ => cov.notes += 1,
    }
    Some(Action {
        kind,
        span: located.span,
        origin: RecognizerId(FALLBACK.into()),
    })
}

fn fence(cx: &StepContext<'_>, block: &Block, whole: TextRef) -> ActionKind {
    let language = block.fence_language(cx.doc).unwrap_or("");
    let reason = match language {
        "text" => return ActionKind::Note { text: whole },
        "" => ManualReason::Unrecognized,
        shell if cx.settings.shell_for(shell).is_some() => ManualReason::Unrecognized,
        other => ManualReason::ForeignLanguage(other.into()),
    };
    ActionKind::Manual {
        reason,
        text: whole,
    }
}
```

```rust
//! Recognizers: small, composable matchers that turn a run of blocks into actions
//! (docs/architecture.md §4.4). A format is an ordered list of recognizer instances.

use std::sync::Arc;

use planrun_model::{Action, Manifest, TaskId};

use crate::diagnostic::Diagnostic;
use crate::document::{BlockRange, Document, Located};
use crate::format::FormatSettings;

/// What a recognizer may read besides the blocks.
pub struct StepContext<'a> {
    /// The document.
    pub doc: &'a Document,
    /// The task being recognized.
    pub task: &'a TaskId,
    /// Its manifest (a full-contents write consults it for create vs overwrite).
    pub manifest: &'a Manifest,
    /// Format settings.
    pub settings: &'a FormatSettings,
    /// Actions already produced in this step: an expectation attaches to the script before it;
    /// the patch ceremony is absorbed only right after a patch.
    pub previous: &'a [Action],
}

/// A position in a step's blocks. A recognizer that matches advances it past what it consumed.
/// Blocks in the `skip` range (the task's manifest) are invisible to it.
pub struct Cursor<'a> {
    blocks: &'a [Located],
    range: BlockRange,
    skip: Option<BlockRange>,
    position: usize,
    consumed: usize,
}

impl<'a> Cursor<'a> {
    /// A cursor at the start of `range`.
    pub fn new(doc: &'a Document, range: BlockRange) -> Self {
        Self::with_skip(doc, range, None)
    }

    /// A cursor at the start of `range` that never sees the blocks of `skip`.
    pub fn with_skip(doc: &'a Document, range: BlockRange, skip: Option<BlockRange>) -> Self {
        let mut cursor = Self {
            blocks: &doc.blocks,
            range,
            skip,
            position: range.start,
            consumed: 0,
        };
        cursor.position = cursor.settle(range.start);
        cursor
    }

    /// `index`, or the first index after the skipped range when it falls inside it.
    fn settle(&self, index: usize) -> usize {
        match self.skip {
            Some(skip) if skip.contains(index) => skip.end,
            _ => index,
        }
    }

    /// The block `offset` positions ahead, if it is still in the step.
    pub fn peek(&self, offset: usize) -> Option<&'a Located> {
        let mut index = self.position;
        for _ in 0..offset {
            index = self.settle(index + 1);
        }
        (index < self.range.end).then(|| &self.blocks[index])
    }

    /// Consumes `count` blocks.
    pub fn advance(&mut self, count: usize) {
        for _ in 0..count {
            if self.is_done() {
                return;
            }
            self.position = self.settle(self.position + 1);
            self.consumed += 1;
        }
    }

    /// True when every block of the step is consumed.
    pub fn is_done(&self) -> bool {
        self.position >= self.range.end
    }

    /// The current position (for coverage and diagnostics).
    pub fn position(&self) -> usize {
        self.position
    }

    /// How many blocks have been consumed so far.
    pub fn consumed(&self) -> usize {
        self.consumed
    }
}

/// The outcome of one recognizer at one position.
pub enum Recognized {
    /// Not this recognizer's idiom; the cursor must be unchanged.
    No,
    /// Matched: the cursor is advanced; these actions are appended (may be empty, e.g. the
    /// absorbed patch ceremony).
    Yes(Vec<Action>),
    /// The idiom matched but is malformed (lead sentence without its fence, a patch whose
    /// paths disagree with the lead): a parse error, never a fallback to `Manual`.
    Malformed(Diagnostic),
    /// Matched an earlier action instead of producing one (an `Expected` block): replace the
    /// last action with this one.
    Amend(Action),
}

/// One configured matcher. Must be pure: no I/O, no state between calls.
pub trait Recognizer: Send + Sync {
    /// Instance id for coverage and `planrun show` (`file-block#full-contents`).
    fn id(&self) -> &str;

    /// Tries to match at the cursor.
    fn recognize(&self, cx: &StepContext<'_>, cursor: &mut Cursor<'_>) -> Recognized;
}

/// Builds recognizer instances of one kind from the `[[recognizer]]` tables of a format spec.
/// Registered by kind name in the [`crate::Registry`]; this is the code-level plugin point.
pub trait RecognizerFactory: Send + Sync {
    /// The kind name used in specs (`file-block`).
    fn kind(&self) -> &'static str;

    /// One instance from its spec table (`params` excludes `kind`). Invalid params are an
    /// error when the registry loads the format, never at parse time.
    fn build(&self, params: &toml::Table) -> Result<Arc<dyn Recognizer>, String>;
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use crate::source;

    #[test]
    fn the_cursor_never_sees_the_skipped_blocks() {
        let text = "a\n\nb\n\nc\n\nd\n";
        let source = source::load("t", text.as_bytes()).unwrap_or_else(|e| panic!("{e:?}"));
        let doc = Document::build(source).unwrap_or_else(|e| panic!("{e:?}"));
        let all = BlockRange { start: 0, end: 4 };
        let skip = Some(BlockRange { start: 1, end: 3 });
        let mut cursor = Cursor::with_skip(&doc, all, skip);
        let at = |c: &Cursor<'_>, k| c.peek(k).map(|b| doc.source.slice(b.span).to_owned());
        assert_eq!(at(&cursor, 0).as_deref(), Some("a"));
        assert_eq!(at(&cursor, 1).as_deref(), Some("d"));
        assert_eq!(at(&cursor, 2), None);
        cursor.advance(1);
        assert_eq!(cursor.position(), 3);
        cursor.advance(5);
        assert!(cursor.is_done());
        assert_eq!(cursor.consumed(), 2);

        let from_skip = Cursor::with_skip(&doc, BlockRange { start: 1, end: 4 }, skip);
        assert_eq!(from_skip.position(), 3);
    }
}
```

File `crates/planrun-parse/src/registry.rs` (full contents):

```rust
//! The registry: recognizer factories by kind, and the formats built from specs with them.
//!
//! Composition root of parsing. Built-ins register in [`Registry::with_builtins`]; user formats
//! are added from TOML (`.planrun/formats/*.toml` in the repository, then the user config
//! directory) with [`Registry::add_spec`]. A spec that names an unknown recognizer kind, has an
//! invalid regex or duplicates a format id fails here, before any plan is read.

use std::collections::HashMap;
use std::sync::Arc;

use crate::diagnostic::Diagnostic;
use crate::document::Document;
use crate::format::Format;
use crate::kinds;
use crate::recognizer::RecognizerFactory;
use crate::spec::{FormatSpec, SpecFormat};

/// Recognizer factories and formats.
pub struct Registry {
    factories: HashMap<&'static str, Arc<dyn RecognizerFactory>>,
    formats: Vec<Arc<dyn Format>>,
    /// Detection scores below this never select a format.
    pub min_score: u8,
}

/// Why a factory, spec or format was refused.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, thiserror::Error)]
pub enum RegistryError {
    /// Two factories with one kind.
    #[error("recognizer kind `{0}` is registered twice")]
    DuplicateKind(String),
    /// Two formats with one id.
    #[error("format `{0}` is registered twice")]
    DuplicateFormat(String),
    /// A spec names a kind no factory provides.
    #[error("format `{format}`: unknown recognizer kind `{kind}`")]
    UnknownKind {
        /// The format.
        format: String,
        /// The kind.
        kind: String,
    },
    /// A spec does not parse or a factory refused its parameters.
    #[error("format `{format}`: {message}")]
    InvalidSpec {
        /// The format (or the file name when the id is unreadable).
        format: String,
        /// What is wrong.
        message: String,
    },
}

impl Default for Registry {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self::new()
    }
}

impl Registry {
    /// An empty registry with the default threshold (50).
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self {
            factories: HashMap::new(),
            formats: Vec::new(),
            min_score: 50,
        }
    }

    /// The built-in recognizer kinds and formats (Phase 1: the `assay` format and the kinds it
    /// uses; Phase 2: the other three).
    pub fn with_builtins() -> Result<Self, RegistryError> {
        let mut registry = Self::new();
        for factory in kinds::builtin_factories() {
            registry.add_factory(factory)?;
        }
        registry.add_spec("assay", include_str!("../../../formats/assay.toml"))?;
        Ok(registry)
    }

    /// Registers a recognizer factory.
    pub fn add_factory(
        &mut self,
        factory: Arc<dyn RecognizerFactory>,
    ) -> Result<(), RegistryError> {
        let kind = factory.kind();
        if self.factories.insert(kind, factory).is_some() {
            return Err(RegistryError::DuplicateKind(kind.to_owned()));
        }
        Ok(())
    }

    /// Registers a hand-written format.
    pub fn add_format(&mut self, format: Arc<dyn Format>) -> Result<(), RegistryError> {
        if self.formats.iter().any(|f| f.id() == format.id()) {
            return Err(RegistryError::DuplicateFormat(format.id().0.to_string()));
        }
        self.formats.push(format);
        Ok(())
    }

    /// Builds a format from TOML spec text and registers it. `origin` is the spec's file stem,
    /// which must equal its `id` (docs/extending.md §2).
    pub fn add_spec(&mut self, origin: &str, toml_text: &str) -> Result<(), RegistryError> {
        let invalid = |message: String| RegistryError::InvalidSpec {
            format: origin.to_owned(),
            message,
        };
        let spec: FormatSpec = toml::from_str(toml_text).map_err(|e| invalid(e.to_string()))?;
        if spec.id != origin {
            return Err(invalid(format!(
                "the id `{}` is not the file name `{origin}`",
                spec.id
            )));
        }
        let unknown = spec
            .recognizers
            .iter()
            .filter_map(|t| t.get("kind").and_then(|k| k.as_str()))
            .find(|kind| !self.factories.contains_key(kind));
        if let Some(kind) = unknown {
            return Err(RegistryError::UnknownKind {
                format: origin.to_owned(),
                kind: kind.to_owned(),
            });
        }
        let format =
            SpecFormat::compile(spec, |kind| self.factories.get(kind).cloned()).map_err(invalid)?;
        self.add_format(Arc::new(format))
    }

    /// The factory for `kind`.
    pub fn factory(&self, kind: &str) -> Option<&Arc<dyn RecognizerFactory>> {
        self.factories.get(kind)
    }

    /// All formats, in registration order.
    pub fn formats(&self) -> &[Arc<dyn Format>] {
        &self.formats
    }

    /// The format for `doc`: the one named by `forced` (`unknown-format` when none has that
    /// id), else the single highest detection score at or above [`Registry::min_score`]
    /// (`no-format` when none reaches it, `ambiguous-format` on a tie). Never a guess.
    pub fn select(
        &self,
        doc: &Document,
        forced: Option<&str>,
    ) -> Result<Arc<dyn Format>, Diagnostic> {
        if let Some(id) = forced {
            return self
                .formats
                .iter()
                .find(|f| &*f.id().0 == id)
                .cloned()
                .ok_or_else(|| {
                    Diagnostic::error("unknown-format", None, format!("no format has id `{id}`"))
                });
        }
        let mut scored: Vec<(u8, &Arc<dyn Format>)> = self
            .formats
            .iter()
            .map(|f| (f.detect(doc), f))
            .filter(|(score, _)| *score >= self.min_score)
            .collect();
        scored.sort_by_key(|(score, _)| std::cmp::Reverse(*score));
        match scored.as_slice() {
            [] => Err(Diagnostic::error(
                "no-format",
                None,
                format!(
                    "no format recognizes this plan (no detection score reaches {}); name one \
                     with --format",
                    self.min_score
                ),
            )),
            [(a, first), (b, second), ..] if a == b => Err(Diagnostic::error(
                "ambiguous-format",
                None,
                format!(
                    "formats `{}` and `{}` both score {a}; name one with --format",
                    first.id().0,
                    second.id().0
                ),
            )),
            [(_, first), ..] => Ok(Arc::clone(first)),
        }
    }
}

mod tests;
```

```rust
//! The synthetic assay plan parses with every block accounted for: actions, the absorbed
//! ceremonies, the manifest, and Notes for the goal and the prose between blocks.

use std::path::Path;

use planrun_model::{ActionKind, ExitExpect, ScriptIntent, WriteMode};
use planrun_parse::{Registry, parse_plan};

fn parse() -> planrun_parse::Parsed {
    let path =
        Path::new(env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR")).join("../../tests/corpus/synthetic/assay-style.md");
    let bytes = std::fs::read(&path).unwrap_or_else(|e| panic!("{}: {e}", path.display()));
    let registry = Registry::with_builtins().unwrap_or_else(|e| panic!("{e}"));
    parse_plan(&registry, "assay-style.md", &bytes, None)
        .unwrap_or_else(|d| panic!("{:?}", d.error_codes()))
}

/// One short label per action.
fn label(kind: &ActionKind) -> String {
    match kind {
        ActionKind::WriteFile { path, mode, .. } => format!("write {path} {mode:?}"),
        ActionKind::ApplyPatch {
            path, pre, post, ..
        } => {
            let id = |i: &Option<planrun_model::AbbrevBlobId>| {
                i.map(|i| i.to_string()).unwrap_or_default()
            };
            format!("patch {path} {}..{}", id(pre), id(post))
        }
        ActionKind::Delete { path, .. } => format!("delete {path}"),
        ActionKind::Generated { path } => format!("generated {path}"),
        ActionKind::RunScript(s) => {
            let exit = s.expect.as_ref().map_or(ExitExpect::Unknown, |e| e.exit);
            format!("run {:?} {exit:?}", s.intent)
        }
        ActionKind::Commit(s) => {
            let exit = s.expect.as_ref().map_or(ExitExpect::Unknown, |e| e.exit);
            format!("commit {exit:?}")
        }
        ActionKind::Note { .. } => "note".to_owned(),
        ActionKind::Manual { reason, .. } => format!("manual {reason:?}"),
        ActionKind::ReplaceExact { path, .. } => format!("replace {path}"),
    }
}

#[test]
fn the_assay_plan_is_fully_recognized() {
    let parsed = parse();
    assert_eq!(&*parsed.plan.format.0, "assay");
    assert_eq!(parsed.plan.meta.phase.as_deref(), Some("P1"));
    assert!(parsed.diagnostics.0.is_empty(), "{:?}", parsed.diagnostics);

    let tasks: Vec<(String, Vec<Vec<String>>)> = parsed
        .plan
        .tasks
        .iter()
        .map(|t| {
            let steps = t
                .steps
                .iter()
                .map(|s| s.actions.iter().map(|a| label(&a.kind)).collect())
                .collect();
            (t.id.raw.to_string(), steps)
        })
        .collect();
    let strings = |items: &[&str]| items.iter().map(|s| (*s).to_owned()).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    assert_eq!(
        tasks,
        [
            (
                "P1-T1".to_owned(),
                vec![
                    strings(&["note"]),
                    strings(&[
                        "write src/greet.rs Create",
                        "patch src/lib.rs aa80cf1..97bd92a"
                    ]),
                    strings(&["note", "run Verify Success", "note", "run Gate Success"]),
                    strings(&["note", "commit Unknown"]),
                ]
            ),
            (
                "P1-T2".to_owned(),
                vec![
                    strings(&["note", "generated docs/modules.txt"]),
                    strings(&[
                        "patch src/lib.rs 97bd92a..6b269fc",
                        "delete src/old.rs",
                        "generated docs/modules.txt",
                    ]),
                    strings(&["note", "run Verify Success", "note", "run Gate Success"]),
                    strings(&["note", "commit Unknown"]),
                ]
            ),
        ]
    );
}

#[test]
fn coverage_has_no_manual_block_and_lists_the_absorbed_ceremonies() {
    let parsed = parse();
    for c in &parsed.coverage {
        assert_eq!(c.manual, 0, "{}", c.task);
        assert_eq!(c.absorbed.len(), 1, "{}", c.task);
        assert_eq!(&*c.absorbed[0].recognizer.0, "ceremony#git-apply-temp");
        assert_eq!(c.notes, 4, "{}", c.task);
    }
    let lines: Vec<u32> = parsed
        .coverage
        .iter()
        .map(|c| parsed.plan.source.line_col(c.absorbed[0].span.start).line)
        .collect();
    assert_eq!(lines, [74, 154]);
}

#[test]
fn write_modes_and_intents_come_from_the_plan() {
    let parsed = parse();
    let first = &parsed.plan.tasks[0].steps[1].actions[0];
    assert!(matches!(
        first.kind,
        ActionKind::WriteFile {
            mode: WriteMode::Create,
            ..
        }
    ));
    let commit = &parsed.plan.tasks[0].steps[3].actions[1];
    let ActionKind::Commit(script) = &commit.kind else {
        panic!("{:?}", commit.kind);
    };
    assert_eq!(script.intent, ScriptIntent::Other);
    let prose = script.expect.as_ref().and_then(|e| e.prose.as_ref());
    assert_eq!(
        prose.map(|p| parsed.plan.text(p)),
        Some("the commit, then no output from `git status --porcelain`.")
    );
}
```

```powershell
  }
  $differ = @($verified.Keys | Where-Object { (git rev-parse ":$_") -ne $verified[$_] })
  if ($differ.Count -eq 0) { git commit -m "P1-T4: recognition and the assay kinds" }
  else { "staged files differ from the verified tree: $($differ -join ', ')" }
} else { "the gate failed (exit code $gate): fix the task before committing" }
git status --porcelain
```

Expected: the commit, then no output from `git status --porcelain`. A line naming files instead of the commit means those files differ from the verified tree.

### P1-T5: checks, invalid corpus, local corpus

**Goal:** The six check rules (`task-order` with `duplicate-task-id`, `step-order`, `manifest`, `patch-shape`, `blob-chain`, `commit`), the invalid-corpus test with ten new files (one per new code a plan can produce), and the ignored local-corpus test.

**Files**

- Modify `Cargo.lock`
- Modify `crates/planrun-parse/Cargo.toml`

`sha1_smol` moves into `planrun-parse` for the blob id of a created file (`blob-chain`); D9 already says so since P1-T1. The local-corpus test lists the four plans no built-in format detects yet as skipped and fails on any other error.

File `crates/planrun-parse/src/check.rs` (full contents):

```rust
//! Static checks over a recognized plan (docs/architecture.md §4.5). Pure: plan text only.
//!
//! Each rule is a [`Rule`]; [`default_rules`] is the set `planrun check` runs. A rule reports
//! with stable codes; the invalid corpus pins them.

use std::collections::HashMap;

use planrun_model::{
    AbbrevBlobId, Action, ActionKind, ChangeKind, Plan, RepoPath, Span, Task, WriteMode,
};

use crate::diagnostic::Diagnostic;
use crate::patch;

/// One static check.
pub trait Rule: Send + Sync {
    /// Its id (`manifest`, `blob-chain`).
    fn id(&self) -> &'static str;

    /// Appends its findings for `plan`.
    fn check(&self, plan: &Plan, out: &mut Vec<Diagnostic>);
}

/// The rules of `planrun check`, in reporting order:
///
/// - `task-order`: a repeated task id is `duplicate-task-id`; among the other tasks, ordinals
///   strictly increase (`task-order`).
/// - `step-order`: written step numbers count up from 1 in each task.
/// - `manifest`: every file action's path is declared (`manifest-undeclared`); every declared
///   Create/Modify/Rewrite/Delete is touched by an action or a `Generated`
///   (`manifest-untouched`); a `WriteFile { FromManifest }` on a declared path resolves to Create
///   or Overwrite (`manifest-write-mode`). A task without a manifest block is skipped.
/// - `patch-shape`: a patch has at least one hunk and its hunks' line counts match their bodies.
///   (Its paths and `index` line are checked when it is recognized: `patch-path`, `patch-shape`.)
/// - `blob-chain`: per path, each patch's preimage equals the postimage of the last earlier
///   patch on that path (when both are known); a Create's later patch starts from the created
///   content's blob id; a Delete ends the chain.
/// - `commit`: at most one Commit per task (`commit-twice`), and it is the task's last runnable
///   action (`commit-not-last`).
///
/// Rules run only on a plan whose outline and recognition had no errors. The coverage report is
/// not a rule: `parse_plan` returns it beside the plan.
pub fn default_rules() -> Vec<Box<dyn Rule>> {
    vec![
        Box::new(TaskOrder),
        Box::new(StepOrder),
        Box::new(ManifestRule),
        Box::new(PatchShape),
        Box::new(BlobChain),
        Box::new(CommitRule),
    ]
}

fn actions(task: &Task) -> impl Iterator<Item = &Action> {
    task.steps.iter().flat_map(|s| s.actions.iter())
}

/// The task's heading line.
fn heading(task: &Task) -> Span {
    Span::new(task.span.start, task.title.span().end.max(task.span.start))
}

/// The path a file action changes.
fn file_path(kind: &ActionKind) -> Option<&RepoPath> {
    match kind {
        ActionKind::WriteFile { path, .. }
        | ActionKind::ApplyPatch { path, .. }
        | ActionKind::ReplaceExact { path, .. }
        | ActionKind::Delete { path, .. }
        | ActionKind::Generated { path } => Some(path),
        _ => None,
    }
}

/// `task-order`.
pub struct TaskOrder;

impl Rule for TaskOrder {
    fn id(&self) -> &'static str {
        "task-order"
    }

    fn check(&self, plan: &Plan, out: &mut Vec<Diagnostic>) {
        let mut seen: HashMap<&str, Span> = HashMap::new();
        let mut last: Option<&Task> = None;
        for task in &plan.tasks {
            if let Some(first) = seen.get(&*task.id.raw) {
                out.push(
                    Diagnostic::error(
                        "duplicate-task-id",
                        Some(heading(task)),
                        format!("task `{}` is defined twice", task.id),
                    )
                    .with_related(*first, "first defined here"),
                );
                continue;
            }
            seen.insert(&task.id.raw, heading(task));
            if let Some(prev) = last.filter(|p| task.id.ordinal <= p.id.ordinal) {
                out.push(
                    Diagnostic::error(
                        "task-order",
                        Some(heading(task)),
                        format!("task `{}` comes after task `{}`", task.id, prev.id),
                    )
                    .with_related(heading(prev), "the earlier task"),
                );
            }
            last = Some(task);
        }
    }
}

/// `step-order`.
pub struct StepOrder;

impl Rule for StepOrder {
    fn id(&self) -> &'static str {
        "step-order"
    }

    fn check(&self, plan: &Plan, out: &mut Vec<Diagnostic>) {
        for task in &plan.tasks {
            let numbered = task.steps.iter().filter_map(|s| s.number.map(|n| (n, s)));
            let wrong = numbered
                .zip(1u32..)
                .find(|((n, _), expected)| n != expected);
            let Some(((n, step), expected)) = wrong else {
                continue;
            };
            let at = Span::new(
                step.span.start,
                step.title.as_ref().map_or(step.span.end, |t| t.span().end),
            );
            out.push(Diagnostic::error(
                "step-order",
                Some(at),
                format!(
                    "step {n} where step {expected} comes next in task `{}`",
                    task.id
                ),
            ));
        }
    }
}

/// `manifest`.
pub struct ManifestRule;

impl Rule for ManifestRule {
    fn id(&self) -> &'static str {
        "manifest"
    }

    fn check(&self, plan: &Plan, out: &mut Vec<Diagnostic>) {
        for task in plan.tasks.iter().filter(|t| t.manifest.span.is_some()) {
            check_manifest(task, out);
        }
    }
}

fn check_manifest(task: &Task, out: &mut Vec<Diagnostic>) {
    let manifest = task.manifest.span.unwrap_or(task.span);
    let declared = |path: &RepoPath| task.manifest.entries.iter().find(|e| &e.path == path);
    let mut touched: Vec<&RepoPath> = Vec::new();
    for action in actions(task) {
        let Some(path) = file_path(&action.kind) else {
            continue;
        };
        if touched.contains(&path) {
            continue;
        }
        touched.push(path);
        let Some(entry) = declared(path) else {
            out.push(
                Diagnostic::error(
                    "manifest-undeclared",
                    Some(action.span),
                    format!("`{path}` is changed here but not declared in the task's files"),
                )
                .with_related(manifest, "the task's files"),
            );
            continue;
        };
        let unresolved = matches!(
            action.kind,
            ActionKind::WriteFile {
                mode: WriteMode::FromManifest,
                ..
            }
        );
        if unresolved {
            out.push(
                Diagnostic::error(
                    "manifest-write-mode",
                    Some(action.span),
                    format!(
                        "full contents for `{path}`, declared {:?}: neither create nor overwrite",
                        entry.change
                    ),
                )
                .with_related(entry.span, "declared here"),
            );
        }
    }
    let must_touch = |c: ChangeKind| !matches!(c, ChangeKind::Test);
    for entry in &task.manifest.entries {
        if must_touch(entry.change) && !touched.contains(&&entry.path) {
            out.push(Diagnostic::error(
                "manifest-untouched",
                Some(entry.span),
                format!(
                    "`{}` is declared, and no action of the task changes it",
                    entry.path
                ),
            ));
        }
    }
}

/// `patch-shape`.
pub struct PatchShape;

impl Rule for PatchShape {
    fn id(&self) -> &'static str {
        "patch-shape"
    }

    fn check(&self, plan: &Plan, out: &mut Vec<Diagnostic>) {
        for action in plan.tasks.iter().flat_map(actions) {
            let ActionKind::ApplyPatch { patch, .. } = &action.kind else {
                continue;
            };
            if let Err(e) = patch::check_hunks(plan.text(patch)) {
                out.push(Diagnostic::error("patch-shape", Some(action.span), e));
            }
        }
    }
}

/// What is known about a file's content at a point of the plan.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy)]
enum Known {
    /// The blob id of content the plan wrote in full.
    Full([u8; 20]),
    /// The postimage of a patch.
    Abbrev(AbbrevBlobId),
}

impl Known {
    fn agrees(&self, pre: &AbbrevBlobId) -> bool {
        match self {
            Known::Full(id) => pre.matches(id),
            Known::Abbrev(post) => {
                let (a, b) = (post.to_string(), pre.to_string());
                a.starts_with(&b) || b.starts_with(&a)
            }
        }
    }

    fn hex(&self) -> String {
        match self {
            Known::Full(id) => id.iter().map(|b| format!("{b:02x}")).collect(),
            Known::Abbrev(id) => id.to_string(),
        }
    }
}

/// The git blob id of `content`.
pub fn blob_id(content: &str) -> [u8; 20] {
    let mut sha = sha1_smol::Sha1::new();
    sha.update(format!("blob {}\0", content.len()).as_bytes());
    sha.update(content.as_bytes());
    sha.digest().bytes()
}

/// `blob-chain`.
pub struct BlobChain;

impl Rule for BlobChain {
    fn id(&self) -> &'static str {
        "blob-chain"
    }

    fn check(&self, plan: &Plan, out: &mut Vec<Diagnostic>) {
        let mut known: HashMap<&RepoPath, (Known, Span)> = HashMap::new();
        for action in plan.tasks.iter().flat_map(actions) {
            match &action.kind {
                ActionKind::WriteFile { path, body, .. } => {
                    known.insert(path, (Known::Full(blob_id(plan.text(body))), action.span));
                }
                ActionKind::ApplyPatch {
                    path, pre, post, ..
                } => {
                    let broken = pre
                        .zip(known.get(path))
                        .filter(|(pre, (s, _))| !s.agrees(pre));
                    if let Some((pre, (state, before))) = broken {
                        out.push(chain_error(path, &pre, state, action.span, *before));
                    }
                    match post {
                        Some(post) => known.insert(path, (Known::Abbrev(*post), action.span)),
                        None => known.remove(path),
                    };
                }
                ActionKind::ReplaceExact { path, .. }
                | ActionKind::Delete { path, .. }
                | ActionKind::Generated { path } => {
                    known.remove(path);
                }
                _ => {}
            }
        }
    }
}

fn chain_error(
    path: &RepoPath,
    pre: &AbbrevBlobId,
    state: &Known,
    at: Span,
    before: Span,
) -> Diagnostic {
    Diagnostic::error(
        "blob-chain",
        Some(at),
        format!(
            "this patch of `{path}` starts from {pre}, but the plan's last change to it leaves {}",
            state.hex()
        ),
    )
    .with_related(before, "the last change")
}

/// `commit`.
pub struct CommitRule;

impl Rule for CommitRule {
    fn id(&self) -> &'static str {
        "commit"
    }

    fn check(&self, plan: &Plan, out: &mut Vec<Diagnostic>) {
        for task in &plan.tasks {
            check_commit(task, out);
        }
    }
}

fn check_commit(task: &Task, out: &mut Vec<Diagnostic>) {
    let mut commit: Option<Span> = None;
    for action in actions(task) {
        let is_commit = matches!(action.kind, ActionKind::Commit(_));
        let runnable = !matches!(
            action.kind,
            ActionKind::Note { .. } | ActionKind::Manual { .. }
        );
        match commit {
            Some(first) if is_commit => out.push(
                Diagnostic::error(
                    "commit-twice",
                    Some(action.span),
                    format!("a second commit in task `{}`", task.id),
                )
                .with_related(first, "the first commit"),
            ),
            Some(first) if runnable => out.push(
                Diagnostic::error(
                    "commit-not-last",
                    Some(action.span),
                    format!("this runs after the commit of task `{}`", task.id),
                )
                .with_related(first, "the commit"),
            ),
            None if is_commit => commit = Some(action.span),
            _ => {}
        }
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn blob_ids_are_gits() {
        let hex: String = blob_id("").iter().map(|b| format!("{b:02x}")).collect();
        assert_eq!(hex, "e69de29bb2d1d6434b8b29ae775ad8c2e48c5391");
        let hello: String = blob_id("hello\n")
            .iter()
            .map(|b| format!("{b:02x}"))
            .collect();
        assert_eq!(hello, "ce013625030ba8dba906f756967f9e9ca394464a");
    }

    #[test]
    fn abbreviations_agree_by_prefix() {
        let id = |s: &str| AbbrevBlobId::parse(s).unwrap_or_else(|e| panic!("{e}"));
        assert!(Known::Abbrev(id("97bd92a")).agrees(&id("97bd92a0")));
        assert!(!Known::Abbrev(id("97bd92a")).agrees(&id("97bd92b")));
        assert!(Known::Full(blob_id("hello\n")).agrees(&id("ce01362")));
    }
}
```

File `crates/planrun-parse/tests/invalid_corpus.rs` (full contents):

```rust
//! Every plan in `tests/corpus/invalid` fails with exactly the error code on its first line
//! (`<!-- expect: <code> -->`, tests/corpus/invalid/README.md). Codes are pinned, messages are
//! not.

use std::collections::BTreeSet;
use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};

use planrun_parse::{Registry, parse_plan};

fn invalid_plans() -> Vec<PathBuf> {
    let dir = Path::new(env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR")).join("../../tests/corpus/invalid");
    let mut plans: Vec<PathBuf> = std::fs::read_dir(&dir)
        .unwrap_or_else(|e| panic!("{}: {e}", dir.display()))
        .map(|e| e.unwrap_or_else(|e| panic!("{e}")).path())
        .filter(|p| p.extension().is_some_and(|x| x == "md"))
        .filter(|p| !p.ends_with("README.md"))
        .collect();
    plans.sort();
    plans
}

fn expected_code(text: &str) -> Option<&str> {
    text.lines()
        .next()?
        .strip_prefix("<!-- expect: ")?
        .strip_suffix(" -->")
}

#[test]
fn every_invalid_plan_yields_exactly_its_code() {
    let registry = Registry::with_builtins().unwrap_or_else(|e| panic!("{e}"));
    let plans = invalid_plans();
    assert!(plans.len() >= 20, "only {} invalid plans", plans.len());
    let mut wrong = Vec::new();
    for path in &plans {
        let bytes = std::fs::read(path).unwrap_or_else(|e| panic!("{}: {e}", path.display()));
        let text = String::from_utf8_lossy(&bytes);
        let name = path.file_name().and_then(|n| n.to_str()).unwrap_or("");
        let Some(expected) = expected_code(&text) else {
            wrong.push(format!("{name}: no `<!-- expect: … -->` first line"));
            continue;
        };
        let codes: BTreeSet<&str> = match parse_plan(&registry, name, &bytes, None) {
            Ok(_) => BTreeSet::new(),
            Err(d) => d.error_codes().into_iter().collect(),
        };
        if codes != BTreeSet::from([expected]) {
            wrong.push(format!("{name}: expected {{{expected}}}, got {codes:?}"));
        }
    }
    assert!(wrong.is_empty(), "\n{}", wrong.join("\n"));
}

#[test]
fn the_file_name_is_the_code() {
    for path in invalid_plans() {
        let text = std::fs::read_to_string(&path).unwrap_or_else(|e| panic!("{e}"));
        let stem = path.file_stem().and_then(|s| s.to_str());
        assert_eq!(expected_code(&text), stem, "{}", path.display());
    }
}
```

File `crates/planrun-parse/tests/local_corpus.rs` (full contents):

```rust
//! The real plans in `tests/corpus/local` (git-ignored, decision D16). Ignored by default, so CI
//! and fresh clones stay green: `cargo test -- --ignored local_corpus`.
//!
//! Every plan must parse with zero errors, except plans no built-in format detects yet (the
//! three formats of P2), which are listed as skipped. A coverage line per task is printed; run
//! with `--nocapture` to see it.

use std::path::Path;

use planrun_parse::{Registry, parse_plan};

#[test]
#[ignore = "needs the private plans in tests/corpus/local"]
fn local_corpus() {
    let dir = Path::new(env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR")).join("../../tests/corpus/local");
    let registry = Registry::with_builtins().unwrap_or_else(|e| panic!("{e}"));
    let mut paths: Vec<_> = std::fs::read_dir(&dir)
        .unwrap_or_else(|e| panic!("{}: {e}", dir.display()))
        .map(|e| e.unwrap_or_else(|e| panic!("{e}")).path())
        .filter(|p| p.extension().is_some_and(|x| x == "md") && !p.ends_with("README.md"))
        .collect();
    paths.sort();
    let mut failed = Vec::new();
    let mut parsed = 0;
    for path in &paths {
        let name = path.file_name().and_then(|n| n.to_str()).unwrap_or("");
        let bytes = std::fs::read(path).unwrap_or_else(|e| panic!("{}: {e}", path.display()));
        let result = match parse_plan(&registry, name, &bytes, None) {
            Ok(result) => result,
            Err(d) if d.error_codes() == ["no-format"] => {
                println!("{name}: skipped, no built-in format detects it yet");
                continue;
            }
            Err(d) => {
                failed.push(format!("{name}: {:?}", d.error_codes()));
                continue;
            }
        };
        parsed += 1;
        println!(
            "{name}: {} ({} tasks)",
            result.plan.format.0,
            result.plan.tasks.len()
        );
        for c in &result.coverage {
            println!(
                "  {:<12} blocks {:>3}  actions {:>3}  absorbed {:>2}  manifest {:>2}  notes {:>2}  manual {:>2}",
                c.task.raw,
                c.blocks(),
                c.actions,
                c.absorbed.len(),
                c.manifest,
                c.notes,
                c.manual
            );
        }
    }
    assert!(failed.is_empty(), "\n{}", failed.join("\n"));
    println!("{parsed} parsed, {} skipped", paths.len() - parsed);
}
```

File `README.md` (full contents):

````markdown
# planrun

Executes structured Markdown implementation plans against a git repository, task by task and step
by step, the way you would by hand: write the files the plan gives, apply its exact diffs, run its
commands, check their expected output, commit when the gate passes. Anything it cannot do
mechanically it shows and waits for. It is not an agent.

```
planrun check plan.md          parse, check, coverage report; changes nothing
planrun show  plan.md P1-T2    the typed form of a task
planrun run   plan.md          execute interactively (--dry-run: change nothing)
planrun resume plan.md         continue after an interruption, re-checking the disk
```

Status: P1. `check` and `show` parse and check plans in the `assay` format; `run` comes in P5. See
[docs/roadmap.md](docs/roadmap.md).

## Documents

| | |
|---|---|
| [CLAUDE.md](CLAUDE.md) | rules for working on this code |
| [docs/architecture.md](docs/architecture.md) | how it works |
| [docs/plan-grammar.md](docs/plan-grammar.md) | the plan formats it reads |
| [docs/extending.md](docs/extending.md) | adding a plan format |
| [docs/decisions.md](docs/decisions.md) | decisions D1–D25 |
| [docs/roadmap.md](docs/roadmap.md) | phases P1–P6 |
| [docs/plans/](docs/plans/) | phase plans (assay format) |

## Build

```powershell
cargo build
cargo test
```

Rust 2024 edition, stable toolchain. Windows with PowerShell 7 is the primary platform; Linux works.
````

File `crates/planrun-cli/src/main.rs` (full contents):

```rust
//! The `planrun` binary (docs/architecture.md §7).

mod render;
mod view;

use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};
use std::process::ExitCode;

use clap::{Parser, Subcommand};
use planrun_parse::{Diagnostics, Parsed, Registry, parse_plan, source};

use crate::view::{Detail, Viewer};

/// Executes structured implementation plans against a git repository, task by task.
#[derive(Debug, Parser)]
#[command(name = "planrun", version)]
struct Cli {
    #[command(subcommand)]
    command: Command,
}

#[derive(Debug, Subcommand)]
enum Command {
    /// Parse and check a plan; print diagnostics and the coverage report. Writes nothing.
    Check {
        /// The plan file.
        plan: PathBuf,
        /// Force a format id instead of detecting one.
        #[arg(long)]
        format: Option<String>,
    },
    /// Print the typed plan, or one task (`P1-T2`) or step (`P1-T2/1`, `P1-T2/intro`).
    Show {
        /// The plan file.
        plan: PathBuf,
        /// A task or task/step.
        target: Option<String>,
        /// Force a format id.
        #[arg(long)]
        format: Option<String>,
    },
    /// Execute a plan (Phase 5).
    Run {
        /// The plan file.
        plan: PathBuf,
        /// Parse, check and validate against a virtual tree; change nothing, run nothing.
        #[arg(long)]
        dry_run: bool,
    },
}

/// Exit codes: 0 fine, 1 the plan has errors, 2 a usage or I/O problem.
const OK: u8 = 0;
const PLAN_ERRORS: u8 = 1;
const USAGE: u8 = 2;

fn main() -> ExitCode {
    let cli = Cli::parse();
    let mut out = String::new();
    let code = match &cli.command {
        Command::Check { plan, format } => check(plan, format.as_deref(), &mut out),
        Command::Show {
            plan,
            target,
            format,
        } => show(plan, target.as_deref(), format.as_deref(), &mut out),
        Command::Run { .. } => {
            out.push_str("planrun: run is not implemented yet (docs/roadmap.md, P5)\n");
            USAGE
        }
    };
    if code == OK {
        print!("{out}");
    } else {
        eprint!("{out}");
    }
    ExitCode::from(code)
}

/// A plan read and parsed, or the exit code when it was not.
enum Loaded {
    Parsed(Box<Parsed>),
    Failed(u8),
}

fn load(path: &Path, format: Option<&str>, out: &mut String) -> Loaded {
    let name = path.display().to_string();
    let bytes = match std::fs::read(path) {
        Ok(bytes) => bytes,
        Err(e) => {
            out.push_str(&format!("planrun: {name}: {e}\n"));
            return Loaded::Failed(USAGE);
        }
    };
    let registry = match Registry::with_builtins() {
        Ok(registry) => registry,
        Err(e) => {
            out.push_str(&format!("planrun: the built-in formats: {e}\n"));
            return Loaded::Failed(USAGE);
        }
    };
    match parse_plan(&registry, &name, &bytes, format) {
        Ok(parsed) => Loaded::Parsed(Box::new(parsed)),
        Err(diagnostics) => {
            failed(&name, &bytes, &diagnostics, out);
            Loaded::Failed(PLAN_ERRORS)
        }
    }
}

/// Renders the diagnostics of a plan that did not parse, against its normalized text.
fn failed(name: &str, bytes: &[u8], diagnostics: &Diagnostics, out: &mut String) {
    let text = source::load(name, bytes)
        .map(|s| s.text().to_owned())
        .unwrap_or_default();
    render::diagnostics(name, &text, &diagnostics.0, out);
    let errors = diagnostics.error_codes().len();
    let plural = if errors == 1 { "" } else { "s" };
    out.push_str(&format!("planrun: {name}: {errors} error{plural}\n"));
}

fn check(path: &Path, format: Option<&str>, out: &mut String) -> u8 {
    let parsed = match load(path, format, out) {
        Loaded::Parsed(parsed) => parsed,
        Loaded::Failed(code) => return code,
    };
    let plan = &parsed.plan;
    let name = plan.source.name.to_string();
    render::diagnostics(&name, plan.source.text(), &parsed.diagnostics.0, out);
    render::coverage(plan, &parsed.coverage, out);
    out.push_str(&format!(
        "planrun: {name}: ok ({}, {} tasks)\n",
        plan.format.0,
        plan.tasks.len()
    ));
    OK
}

fn show(path: &Path, target: Option<&str>, format: Option<&str>, out: &mut String) -> u8 {
    let parsed = match load(path, format, out) {
        Loaded::Parsed(parsed) => parsed,
        Loaded::Failed(code) => return code,
    };
    let viewer = Viewer::new(&parsed.plan);
    let Some(target) = target else {
        view::plan_text(&viewer.plan(), out);
        return OK;
    };
    let (task_id, step) = match target.split_once('/') {
        Some((task, step)) => (task, Some(step)),
        None => (target, None),
    };
    let Some(task) = parsed.plan.tasks.iter().find(|t| &*t.id.raw == task_id) else {
        out.push_str(&format!("planrun: the plan has no task `{task_id}`\n"));
        return USAGE;
    };
    let Some(step) = step else {
        view::task_text(&viewer.task(task), Detail::Summary, out);
        return OK;
    };
    let found = task.steps.iter().find(|s| match s.number {
        Some(n) => n.to_string() == step,
        None => step == "intro",
    });
    let Some(found) = found else {
        out.push_str(&format!("planrun: task `{task_id}` has no step `{step}`\n"));
        return USAGE;
    };
    view::step_text(&viewer.step(found), Detail::Full, out);
    OK
}
```

File `crates/planrun-cli/src/render.rs` (full contents):

```rust
//! Diagnostics with source context (annotate-snippets, plain: no colour, stable for snapshots)
//! and the coverage report.

use annotate_snippets::{AnnotationKind, Group, Level, Renderer, Snippet};
use planrun_model::{ActionKind, Plan};
use planrun_parse::{Diagnostic, Severity, TaskCoverage};

/// One diagnostic against `text` (the normalized plan), with `name` as its path:
/// `error[code]: message`, then `--> name:line:col`, the source line and a caret.
pub fn diagnostic(name: &str, text: &str, d: &Diagnostic) -> String {
    let level = match d.severity {
        Severity::Error => Level::ERROR,
        Severity::Warning => Level::WARNING,
        Severity::Note => Level::NOTE,
    };
    let title = level.primary_title(d.message.as_str()).id(d.code);
    let renderer = Renderer::plain();
    let Some(span) = d.span else {
        return renderer.render(&[Group::with_title(title)]);
    };
    let mut snippet = Snippet::source(text)
        .path(name)
        .annotation(AnnotationKind::Primary.span(span.start as usize..span.end as usize));
    for (related, label) in &d.related {
        snippet = snippet.annotation(
            AnnotationKind::Context
                .span(related.start as usize..related.end as usize)
                .label(label.as_str()),
        );
    }
    renderer.render(&[title.element(snippet)])
}

/// Every diagnostic, one after another, each followed by a blank line.
pub fn diagnostics(name: &str, text: &str, all: &[Diagnostic], out: &mut String) {
    for d in all {
        out.push_str(&diagnostic(name, text, d));
        out.push_str("\n\n");
    }
}

/// The coverage table, the absorbed blocks and the Manual actions of `plan`.
pub fn coverage(plan: &Plan, coverage: &[TaskCoverage], out: &mut String) {
    out.push_str("coverage\n");
    out.push_str("  task          blocks  actions  absorbed  manifest  notes  manual\n");
    for c in coverage {
        out.push_str(&format!(
            "  {:<12}  {:>6}  {:>7}  {:>8}  {:>8}  {:>5}  {:>6}\n",
            c.task.raw,
            c.blocks(),
            c.actions,
            c.absorbed.len(),
            c.manifest,
            c.notes,
            c.manual
        ));
    }
    let line = |at: u32| plan.source.line_col(at).line;
    let absorbed: Vec<String> = coverage
        .iter()
        .flat_map(|c| c.absorbed.iter().map(move |a| (c, a)))
        .map(|(c, a)| {
            format!(
                "  {:<12}  L{:<5} {}\n",
                c.task.raw,
                line(a.span.start),
                a.recognizer.0
            )
        })
        .collect();
    if !absorbed.is_empty() {
        out.push_str("absorbed (never run)\n");
        absorbed.iter().for_each(|l| out.push_str(l));
    }
    let manual: Vec<String> = plan
        .tasks
        .iter()
        .flat_map(|t| {
            t.steps
                .iter()
                .flat_map(move |s| s.actions.iter().map(move |a| (t, a)))
        })
        .filter_map(|(t, a)| match &a.kind {
            ActionKind::Manual { reason, text } => Some(format!(
                "  {:<12}  L{:<5} {reason:?}: {}\n",
                t.id.raw,
                line(a.span.start),
                first_line(plan.text(text))
            )),
            _ => None,
        })
        .collect();
    if !manual.is_empty() {
        out.push_str("manual (planrun waits for you)\n");
        manual.iter().for_each(|l| out.push_str(l));
    }
}

fn first_line(text: &str) -> String {
    let line = text.lines().next().unwrap_or("");
    let short: String = line.chars().take(60).collect();
    if short.len() < line.len() {
        format!("{short}…")
    } else {
        short
    }
}
```

A tiny fixture crate for the planrun corpus.\n \n+pub mod greet;\n pub mod old;\n \n /// The greeting for `name`.\n pub fn greeting(name: &str) -> String {\n-    old::legacy_greeting(name)\n+    greet::greeting(name)\n }\n"
```text
      - step: 2 — verify

          - at: L81

          - at: L110
```

File `crates/planrun-cli/src/view.rs` (full contents):

```rust
//! The resolved view of a plan: the IR with every `TextRef` turned into text and every span into
//! a line (`L37`). `planrun show` prints it; the IR snapshot is its YAML.

use planrun_model::{
    Action, ActionKind, ExitExpect, Expectation, ManifestEntry, Plan, Script, Span, Step, Task,
    TextRef,
};
use serde::Serialize;

/// A plan.
#[derive(Debug, Serialize)]
pub struct PlanView {
    /// The plan's name as given.
    pub plan: String,
    /// The format id.
    pub format: String,
    /// The first level-1 heading.
    pub title: Option<String>,
    /// The phase.
    pub phase: Option<String>,
    /// Manual-marked text before task 1.
    pub preamble: Vec<String>,
    /// The tasks.
    pub tasks: Vec<TaskView>,
    /// Where the text after the tasks starts (never run).
    pub after_tasks: Option<String>,
}

/// A task.
#[derive(Debug, Serialize)]
pub struct TaskView {
    /// `P1-T1`.
    pub id: String,
    /// Its title.
    pub title: String,
    /// Its heading's line.
    pub at: String,
    /// The manifest, one `Change path` per entry.
    pub files: Vec<String>,
    /// The steps.
    pub steps: Vec<StepView>,
}

/// A step.
#[derive(Debug, Serialize)]
pub struct StepView {
    /// `1 — write the files`, or `intro` for the blocks before the first step.
    pub step: String,
    /// Its first line.
    pub at: String,
    /// The actions.
    pub actions: Vec<ActionView>,
}

/// An action.
#[derive(Debug, Serialize)]
pub struct ActionView {
    /// Its first line.
    pub at: String,
    /// The recognizer that produced it.
    pub by: String,
    /// What it does.
    #[serde(flatten)]
    pub what: What,
}

/// What an action does, resolved.
#[derive(Debug, Serialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "kebab-case")]
pub enum What {
    /// `WriteFile`.
    WriteFile {
        /// Target.
        path: String,
        /// Create / Overwrite / FromManifest.
        mode: String,
        /// The contents.
        body: String,
    },
    /// `ApplyPatch`.
    ApplyPatch {
        /// Target.
        path: String,
        /// Preimage blob id.
        pre: Option<String>,
        /// Postimage blob id.
        post: Option<String>,
        /// The patch.
        patch: String,
    },
    /// `ReplaceExact`.
    ReplaceExact {
        /// Target.
        path: String,
        /// The text to find.
        before: String,
        /// Its replacement.
        after: String,
    },
    /// `Delete`.
    Delete {
        /// Target.
        path: String,
        /// Through `git rm`.
        via_git: bool,
    },
    /// `Generated`.
    Generated {
        /// The generated file.
        path: String,
    },
    /// `RunScript`.
    Run(ScriptView),
    /// `Commit`.
    Commit(ScriptView),
    /// `Note`.
    Note {
        /// The text.
        text: String,
    },
    /// `Manual`.
    Manual {
        /// Why.
        reason: String,
        /// The text.
        text: String,
    },
}

/// A script.
#[derive(Debug, Serialize)]
pub struct ScriptView {
    /// pwsh / bash.
    pub shell: String,
    /// Verify / Gate / …
    pub intent: String,
    /// The script.
    pub body: String,
    /// What the plan expects.
    pub expect: Option<ExpectView>,
}

/// An expectation.
#[derive(Debug, Serialize)]
pub struct ExpectView {
    /// `exit 0`, `exit 3`, `fails`, `not checked`.
    pub exit: String,
    /// Summary lines.
    pub lines: Option<String>,
    /// The sentence.
    pub prose: Option<String>,
}

/// Builds the views of one plan.
pub struct Viewer<'a> {
    plan: &'a Plan,
}

impl<'a> Viewer<'a> {
    /// A viewer of `plan`.
    pub fn new(plan: &'a Plan) -> Self {
        Self { plan }
    }

    fn text(&self, text: &TextRef) -> String {
        self.plan.text(text).to_owned()
    }

    fn at(&self, span: Span) -> String {
        format!("L{}", self.plan.source.line_col(span.start).line)
    }

    /// The whole plan.
    pub fn plan(&self) -> PlanView {
        let meta = &self.plan.meta;
        PlanView {
            plan: self.plan.source.name.to_string(),
            format: self.plan.format.0.to_string(),
            title: meta.title.as_ref().map(|t| self.text(t)),
            phase: meta.phase.as_ref().map(|p| p.to_string()),
            preamble: meta.preamble.iter().map(|t| self.text(t)).collect(),
            tasks: self.plan.tasks.iter().map(|t| self.task(t)).collect(),
            after_tasks: meta.after_tasks.map(|s| self.at(s)),
        }
    }

    /// One task.
    pub fn task(&self, task: &Task) -> TaskView {
        TaskView {
            id: task.id.raw.to_string(),
            title: self.text(&task.title),
            at: self.at(task.span),
            files: task.manifest.entries.iter().map(entry).collect(),
            steps: task.steps.iter().map(|s| self.step(s)).collect(),
        }
    }

    /// One step.
    pub fn step(&self, step: &Step) -> StepView {
        let title = step.title.as_ref().map(|t| self.text(t));
        let name = match (step.number, title) {
            (Some(n), Some(t)) => format!("{n} — {t}"),
            (Some(n), None) => n.to_string(),
            (None, Some(t)) => t,
            (None, None) => "intro".to_owned(),
        };
        StepView {
            step: name,
            at: self.at(step.span),
            actions: step.actions.iter().map(|a| self.action(a)).collect(),
        }
    }

    fn action(&self, action: &Action) -> ActionView {
        ActionView {
            at: self.at(action.span),
            by: action.origin.0.to_string(),
            what: self.what(&action.kind),
        }
    }

    fn what(&self, kind: &ActionKind) -> What {
        let id = |i: &Option<planrun_model::AbbrevBlobId>| i.map(|i| i.to_string());
        match kind {
            ActionKind::WriteFile { path, body, mode } => What::WriteFile {
                path: path.to_string(),
                mode: format!("{mode:?}"),
                body: self.text(body),
            },
            ActionKind::ApplyPatch {
                path,
                patch,
                pre,
                post,
            } => What::ApplyPatch {
                path: path.to_string(),
                pre: id(pre),
                post: id(post),
                patch: self.text(patch),
            },
            ActionKind::ReplaceExact {
                path,
                before,
                after,
            } => What::ReplaceExact {
                path: path.to_string(),
                before: self.text(before),
                after: self.text(after),
            },
            ActionKind::Delete { path, via_git } => What::Delete {
                path: path.to_string(),
                via_git: *via_git,
            },
            ActionKind::Generated { path } => What::Generated {
                path: path.to_string(),
            },
            ActionKind::RunScript(s) => What::Run(self.script(s)),
            ActionKind::Commit(s) => What::Commit(self.script(s)),
            ActionKind::Note { text } => What::Note {
                text: self.text(text),
            },
            ActionKind::Manual { reason, text } => What::Manual {
                reason: format!("{reason:?}"),
                text: self.text(text),
            },
        }
    }

    fn script(&self, script: &Script) -> ScriptView {
        ScriptView {
            shell: format!("{:?}", script.shell).to_lowercase(),
            intent: format!("{:?}", script.intent).to_lowercase(),
            body: self.text(&script.body),
            expect: script.expect.as_ref().map(|e| self.expect(e)),
        }
    }

    fn expect(&self, expect: &Expectation) -> ExpectView {
        let exit = match expect.exit {
            ExitExpect::Success => "exit 0".to_owned(),
            ExitExpect::Code(n) => format!("exit {n}"),
            ExitExpect::Failure => "fails".to_owned(),
            ExitExpect::Unknown => "not checked".to_owned(),
        };
        ExpectView {
            exit,
            lines: expect.lines.as_ref().map(|l| self.text(l)),
            prose: expect.prose.as_ref().map(|p| self.text(p)),
        }
    }
}

fn entry(entry: &ManifestEntry) -> String {
    let lines = entry
        .lines
        .map(|l| format!(":{}-{}", l.start, l.end))
        .unwrap_or_default();
    format!("{:?} {}{lines}", entry.change, entry.path)
}

/// How much of each action `show` prints.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum Detail {
    /// One line per action.
    Summary,
    /// Every body in full.
    Full,
}

/// The text `planrun show` prints for a task.
pub fn task_text(task: &TaskView, detail: Detail, out: &mut String) {
    out.push_str(&format!("{}  {}  {}\n", task.id, task.title, task.at));
    if !task.files.is_empty() {
        out.push_str(&format!("  files  {}\n", task.files.join(", ")));
    }
    for step in &task.steps {
        step_text(step, detail, out);
    }
}

/// The text `planrun show` prints for a step.
pub fn step_text(step: &StepView, detail: Detail, out: &mut String) {
    out.push_str(&format!("  step {}  {}\n", step.step, step.at));
    for action in &step.actions {
        action_text(action, detail, out);
    }
}

fn action_text(action: &ActionView, detail: Detail, out: &mut String) {
    let (label, summary, body) = describe(&action.what);
    out.push_str(&format!(
        "    {:<6} {label:<11} {summary}  ({})\n",
        action.at, action.by
    ));
    if let What::Run(s) | What::Commit(s) = &action.what
        && let Some(e) = &s.expect
    {
        out.push_str(&format!("           expect: {}\n", expect_summary(e)));
    }
    if detail == Detail::Full {
        for line in body.lines() {
            out.push_str(&format!("           | {line}\n"));
        }
    }
}

fn expect_summary(e: &ExpectView) -> String {
    let lines = e.lines.as_ref().map(|l| l.lines().count());
    match (lines, &e.prose) {
        (Some(n), _) => format!("{}; {n} summary line(s)", e.exit),
        (None, Some(p)) => format!("{}; \"{p}\" (confirmed by you)", e.exit),
        (None, None) => e.exit.clone(),
    }
}

/// A label, a one-line summary and the full body of an action.
fn describe(what: &What) -> (&'static str, String, &str) {
    match what {
        What::WriteFile { path, mode, body } => ("write-file", format!("{path} ({mode})"), body),
        What::ApplyPatch {
            path,
            pre,
            post,
            patch,
        } => {
            let ids = format!(
                "{}..{}",
                pre.as_deref().unwrap_or("?"),
                post.as_deref().unwrap_or("?")
            );
            ("apply-patch", format!("{path} {ids}"), patch)
        }
        What::ReplaceExact { path, before, .. } => ("replace", path.clone(), before),
        What::Delete { path, via_git } => {
            let how = if *via_git { " (git rm)" } else { "" };
            ("delete", format!("{path}{how}"), "")
        }
        What::Generated { path } => ("generated", path.clone(), ""),
        What::Run(s) => (
            "run",
            format!("{} {}  {}", s.shell, s.intent, first_line(&s.body)),
            &s.body,
        ),
        What::Commit(s) => (
            "commit",
            format!("{}  {}", s.shell, first_line(&s.body)),
            &s.body,
        ),
        What::Note { text } => ("note", first_line(text), text),
        What::Manual { reason, text } => {
            ("manual", format!("{reason}: {}", first_line(text)), text)
        }
    }
}

/// The first line of `text`, at most 72 characters.
fn first_line(text: &str) -> String {
    let line = text.lines().next().unwrap_or("");
    let short: String = line.chars().take(72).collect();
    if short.len() < line.len() {
        format!("{short}…")
    } else {
        short
    }
}

/// The text `planrun show` prints for a whole plan.
pub fn plan_text(plan: &PlanView, out: &mut String) {
    out.push_str(&format!("plan    {}\nformat  {}\n", plan.plan, plan.format));
    if let Some(title) = &plan.title {
        out.push_str(&format!("title   {title}\n"));
    }
    if let Some(phase) = &plan.phase {
        out.push_str(&format!("phase   {phase}\n"));
    }
    for text in &plan.preamble {
        out.push_str(&format!("read first: {}\n", first_line(text)));
    }
    for task in &plan.tasks {
        out.push('\n');
        task_text(task, Detail::Summary, out);
    }
    if let Some(at) = &plan.after_tasks {
        out.push_str(&format!("\nafter the tasks (never run): from {at}\n"));
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use std::path::Path;

    use planrun_parse::{Registry, parse_plan};

    use super::*;

    fn synthetic() -> Plan {
        let path = Path::new(env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"))
            .join("../../tests/corpus/synthetic/assay-style.md");
        let bytes = std::fs::read(&path).unwrap_or_else(|e| panic!("{}: {e}", path.display()));
        let registry = Registry::with_builtins().unwrap_or_else(|e| panic!("{e}"));
        parse_plan(
            &registry,
            "tests/corpus/synthetic/assay-style.md",
            &bytes,
            None,
        )
        .unwrap_or_else(|d| panic!("{:?}", d.error_codes()))
        .plan
    }

    #[test]
    fn the_ir_of_the_synthetic_assay_plan() {
        let plan = synthetic();
        insta::assert_yaml_snapshot!("assay_style_ir", Viewer::new(&plan).plan());
    }

    #[test]
    fn show_prints_one_line_per_action() {
        let plan = synthetic();
        let mut out = String::new();
        plan_text(&Viewer::new(&plan).plan(), &mut out);
        insta::assert_snapshot!("assay_style_show", out);
    }
}
```

```rust
//! The `planrun` binary: exit codes, `check` output, the rendered message of every invalid
//! plan, and `show` of one step. Runs from the workspace root with relative paths, so the output
//! is the same on every machine.

use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};
use std::process::Command;

fn root() -> PathBuf {
    Path::new(env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR")).join("../..")
}

/// Runs `planrun args…`; returns the exit code and stdout + stderr.
fn planrun(args: &[&str]) -> (i32, String) {
    let output = Command::new(env!("CARGO_BIN_EXE_planrun"))
        .args(args)
        .current_dir(root())
        .output()
        .unwrap_or_else(|e| panic!("{e}"));
    let mut text = String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stdout).into_owned();
    text.push_str(&String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stderr));
    (output.status.code().unwrap_or(-1), text)
}

const SYNTHETIC: &str = "tests/corpus/synthetic/assay-style.md";

#[test]
fn check_passes_the_synthetic_plan() {
    let (code, out) = planrun(&["check", SYNTHETIC]);
    assert_eq!(code, 0, "{out}");
    insta::assert_snapshot!("check_assay_style", out);
}

#[test]
fn check_renders_every_invalid_plan() {
    let dir = root().join("tests/corpus/invalid");
    let mut names: Vec<String> = std::fs::read_dir(&dir)
        .unwrap_or_else(|e| panic!("{e}"))
        .map(|e| {
            e.unwrap_or_else(|e| panic!("{e}"))
                .file_name()
                .to_string_lossy()
                .into_owned()
        })
        .filter(|n| n.ends_with(".md") && n != "README.md")
        .collect();
    names.sort();
    let mut all = String::new();
    for name in &names {
        let path = format!("tests/corpus/invalid/{name}");
        let (code, out) = planrun(&["check", &path]);
        assert_eq!(code, 1, "{path}: {out}");
        all.push_str(&format!("==== {name}\n{out}\n"));
    }
    insta::assert_snapshot!("check_invalid_corpus", all);
}

#[test]
fn show_prints_a_step_in_full() {
    let (code, out) = planrun(&["show", SYNTHETIC, "P1-T2/2"]);
    assert_eq!(code, 0, "{out}");
    insta::assert_snapshot!("show_p1_t2_step_2", out);
}

#[test]
fn usage_problems_exit_with_2() {
    assert_eq!(planrun(&["check"]).0, 2);
    assert_eq!(planrun(&["check", "tests/corpus/none.md"]).0, 2);
    assert_eq!(planrun(&["show", SYNTHETIC, "P9-T9"]).0, 2);
    assert_eq!(planrun(&["show", SYNTHETIC, "P1-T1/7"]).0, 2);
    assert_eq!(planrun(&["check", SYNTHETIC, "--format", "nope"]).0, 1);
}
```

Expected: the commit, then no output from `git status --porcelain`. A line naming files instead of the commit means those files differ from the verified tree.

## P1 green state

Run from the repository root after P1-T6:

```powershell
cargo fmt --check; if ($LASTEXITCODE -eq 0) { cargo clippy --all-targets -- -D warnings }; if ($LASTEXITCODE -eq 0) { cargo test }; if ($LASTEXITCODE -eq 0) { cargo test -p planrun-parse --test local_corpus -- --ignored local_corpus }
```

Expected: every `test result:` line ok; the last one `1 passed`.

User-visible change: `planrun check` and `planrun show` work on assay plans; `planrun run` still says it comes in P5.

## After execution

- `git log --oneline -7` lists the commit of this plan, then `P1-T1` to `P1-T6`.
- `cargo run -q -p planrun-cli -- check docs/plans/2026-11-24-p1-parse-assay.md` exits 0: this plan is an assay plan too.
- The roadmap's P1 acceptance: `planrun check tests/corpus/synthetic/assay-style.md` exits 0 with no Manual block and both ceremonies listed as absorbed (P1-T6 step 2); every invalid plan yields exactly its code (P1-T5); the two real assay plans parse with zero errors (P1-T5 and P1-T6 step 2); diagnostics show file:line:col and the source line (the `check_invalid_corpus` snapshot).
